-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts]
-- ==================================================




 

 /*RZ980416*/
/*RZ14020324- Change sp Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/ 
/*RZ14020807- Change status*/
/*RZ14020823- Add condition for update ordStatus*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
/*RZ14030126- Add calculate OrderOtherAmounts*/
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts]
/* 	
	exec ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts 40784,3,1065,1065	
*/
(
@OrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
DECLARE @CalcItemTaxResult INT = 0
EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderItemsTax]
@OrderId = @OrderId,
@Month1 = @Month1,
@Result = @CalcItemTaxResult OUTPUT	
/*RZ14021030- END*/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14020324- Change sp Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT ordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] 
												  WHERE OrderID = @OrderId))
/*RZ14020324- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*Calculate & Update the order*/	
/**/
DECLARE @SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
DECLARE @SumQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TotalTaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
/*RZ14020324- End*/

SELECT  /*@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(/*Round(*/
								CAST(SUM(
									(ISNULL(ordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL( ordIFCR, 1)* ISNULL( ordIFCRI, 1)) *
									(ISNULL(ordIPrice, 0) - ( ISNULL(ordIDiscount, 0) + ISNULL(ordIDiscountI, 0) + ISNULL(ordIRound, 0)) 
									+ ISNULL(ordIRoundPlus, 0) )		
									+ ISNULL(ordITax, 0) * ISNULL(ordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL( ordIFCR, 1)* ISNULL( ordIFCRI, 1)		
								) AS NUMERIC(18,2))
								/*, 0)*/ ,0)*/
		/*RZ14020324- Change sp Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(CAST(SUM( ISNULL([ordIPriceSum],0) + ISNULL([ordITaxSum],0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)),0)
		,@SumQuantity = SUM(ordIQuantity)
		,@TotalDiscountSum = SUM(ordIDiscountSum)
		,@TotalTaxSum = SUM(ordITaxSum)
		/*RZ14020324- End*/	
FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
WHERE OrderId = @OrderID
AND ISNULL(ordINotInSum, 0) = 0
IF(@SumPriceAmount IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @ordItemCount INT, @ordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordInstallAmount NUMERIC(18,2), @ordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordSumAmount NUMERIC(18,2), @ordTax NUMERIC(18,2), @ordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordCalculateTax BIT,@ordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@ordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),@ordRound NUMERIC(18,2),@ordStatusId INT,
	@ordTotalAmountStr NVARCHAR(1000)
	/**/
	SELECT 
       @ordInstallAmount =[ordInstallAmount],
       @ordDiscount = [ordDiscount],
       @ordTaxIndex = [ordTaxIndex],
       @ordCalculateTax = [ordCalculateTax],
       @ordDiscountPercent = [ordDiscountPercent],
       @ordTransportation = [ordTransportation],
       @ordRound = [ordRound],
	   @ordStatusId= [ordStatusId] 
	FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O
	WHERE O.[OrderID] = @OrderId
	/**/
	SET @ordItemsAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)
	IF(@ordDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL AND @ordDiscountPercent > 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @ordDiscount = (ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) * ISNULL(@ordDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
	END
	/**/
	SET @ordSumAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) + ISNULL(@ordInstallAmount,0) - ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0)
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@ordCalculateTax,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @ordTax = ISNULL(@ordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@ordTaxIndex,0) / 100
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @ordTax = 0
	END
	/**/
	SET @ordTotalAmount = ISNULL(ISNULL(@ordSumAmount,0) + ISNULL(@ordTax,0) + ISNULL(@ordTransportation,0) - ISNULL(@ordRound,0),0)
	/**/
	SET @ordItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId)
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14020324- Change sp Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
	IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
	BEGIN							
		/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/ 
		SET @ordItemsAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordItemsAmount))
		SET @ordSumAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSumAmount))		
		SET @ordTax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordTax))		
		SET @ordTotalAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordTotalAmount))		
		SET @ordDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordDiscount))		
		SET @TotalDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)))
		SET @TotalTaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)))
		/*RZ14020626-END*/ 
	END
	/*RZ14020324- End*/
	/*************** TotalAmountStr *******************/
	DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
	IF(@ordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN			
		SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
		SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
		/**/
		IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr /*+ N' —Ì«·' */
		END
		ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
			END
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = REPLACE(@ordTotalAmountStr,'-',' ') 
		END
	END
	/**************************************************/
	/*Update*/
	UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder]
	SET  [ordItemCount] = ISNULL(@ordItemCount,0)
		,[ordItemsAmount] = ISNULL(@ordItemsAmount,0)
		,[ordSumAmount] = ISNULL(@ordSumAmount,0)
		,[ordTax] = ISNULL(@ordTax,0)
		,[ordTotalAmount] = @ordTotalAmount
		,[ordTotalAmountStr] = @ordTotalAmountStr
		,[ordDiscount] = ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0)
		,[Month1] = @Month1
		/*RZ14020324- Change sp Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		,[ordItemsQty] = ISNULL(@SumQuantity,0)
		,[ordItemsDiscount] = ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)
		,[ordItemsTax] = ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)
		/*RZ14020324- End*/
	WHERE OrderID = @OrderId
	/**/
	IF(@ordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @LangId INT
		/**/
		DECLARE Crsr CURSOR FOR
			SELECT LanguageID
			FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
			WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
		/**/
		OPEN Crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			While @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN	 
				DECLARE @ordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				IF(@LangId = 1065)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang /*+ N' —Ì«·'*/
				END
				ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
					END							
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@ordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
				END
				/**/
				DECLARE @OrdIdLang INT
				SET @OrdIdLang = (SELECT [WSOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang] WHERE OrderID = @OrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
				IF(@OrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
						SET [ordTotalAmountStr] = @ordTotalAmountStrLang
					WHERE [WSOrderLangID] = @OrdIdLang						
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
						([OrderID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[ordTotalAmountStr])
					VALUES
						(@OrderId
						,@LangId
						,@ordTotalAmountStrLang)
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			END
		CLOSE Crsr
		DEALLOCATE Crsr	
	END							
	/*==================================================*/
	/*================= Update status ==================*/	
	DECLARE @SumOrdPayAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
	SET @SumOrdPayAmount = ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL( SUM(ordpayAmount), 0) sumordpayAmount
									FROM dbo.WSOrderPayments
									WHERE ordpayOrderId = @OrderId
									AND ISNULL(ordpayDeActive,0) = 0/*RZ14020823- Add condition for update ordStatus*/),0)
	/*IF(@SumOrdPayAmount IS NOT NULL AND (@ordStatusId <> 4 AND @ordStatusId <> 5))*/
	IF(ISNULL(@SumOrdPayAmount,0) <> 0 AND (@ordStatusId <> 4 AND @ordStatusId <> 5))
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(@SumOrdPayAmount = 0 AND @ordStatusId <>1)
			SET @ordStatusId = 1
		/**/
		ELSE IF(@SumOrdPayAmount < @ordTotalAmount)/*RZ14020807- Change status - <>*/
			SET @ordStatusId = 2
		/**/
		ELSE IF(@SumOrdPayAmount >= @ordTotalAmount)/*RZ14020807- Change status - =*/
			SET @ordStatusId = 3
		/**/	 		
		/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder]
		SET [ordStatusId] = @ordStatusId 
		WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId
	/**/
	END
	/*==================================================*/
END	
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14030126- Add calculate OrderOtherAmounts*/
DECLARE @ResultCalcOrderOtherAmounts  INT = 0
EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderOtherAmounts]
@OrderId = @OrderId,
@Month1 = @Month1,
@CurrentDate = NULL,
@Result = @ResultCalcOrderOtherAmounts OUTPUT	
/*RZ14030126- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmountsCurrency]	
@OrderId = @OrderId,
@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
/*RZ14030301- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END










-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts]
-- ==================================================




 



 /*RZ990206*/
/*RZ14020324- Cahnge sp and add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts]
/* 	
	exec ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts 3507,3,1033,1065	
*/
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
DECLARE @CalcItemTaxResult INT = 0
EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderItemsTax]
@PreOrderId = @PreOrderId,
@Month1 = @Month1,
@Result = @CalcItemTaxResult OUTPUT	
/*RZ14021030- END*/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14020324- Cahnge sp and add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT pordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] 
												  WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId))
/*RZ14020324- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*Calculate & Update the order*/	
/**/

DECLARE @SumQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TotalTaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
/*RZ14020324- End*/
DECLARE @SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
SELECT 
	/*@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(/*ROUND(*/
							CAST(SUM(
							 (ISNULL(pordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL(pordIFCR, 1) * ISNULL(pordIFCRI, 1)) 
							 * 
							 (( ISNULL(pordIPrice, 0) - ( ISNULL(pordIDiscount, 0) + ISNULL(pordIDiscountI, 0) + ISNULL(pordIRound, 0))
							 /*ADBF960120*/
							 + ISNULL(pordIRoundPlus, 0)
							 ))	  
	 							+ (ISNULL( pordITax, 0) * ISNULL(pordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL(pordIFCR, 1) * ISNULL(pordIFCRI, 1))
							) AS NUMERIC(18,2))
							/*, 0)*/,0)*/
	/*RZ14020324- Cahnge sp and add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
	@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(CAST(SUM(ISNULL([pordIPriceSum],0) + ISNULL([pordITaxSum],0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)),0)
	,@SumQuantity = SUM(pordIQuantity)
	,@TotalDiscountSum = SUM(pordIDiscountSum)
	,@TotalTaxSum = SUM(pordITaxSum)
	/*RZ14020324- End*/	
FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId
AND ISNULL(pordINotInSum, 0) = 0
/**/
IF(@SumPriceAmount IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @pordItemCount  INT, @pordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordInstallAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordSumAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTax NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordCalculateTax BIT,@pordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),@pordRound NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordTotalAmountStr NVARCHAR(1000)	
	/**/			
	SELECT 
       @pordInstallAmount =[pordInstallAmount],
       @pordDiscount = [pordDiscount],
       @pordTaxIndex = [pordTaxIndex],
       @pordCalculateTax = [pordCalculateTax],
       @pordDiscountPercent = [pordDiscountPercent],
       @pordTransportation = [pordTransportation],
       @pordRound = [pordRound]      
	FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] PO
	WHERE PO.[PreOrderID] = @PreOrderId
	/**/
	SET @pordItemsAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)
	IF(@pordDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL AND @pordDiscountPercent > 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordDiscount = (ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
	END
	/**/
	SET @pordSumAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) + ISNULL(@pordInstallAmount,0) - ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0)
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@pordCalculateTax,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTax = ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordTaxIndex,0) / 100
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTax = 0
	END
	/**/
	SET @pordTotalAmount = ISNULL(ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0) + ISNULL(@pordTax,0) + ISNULL(@pordTransportation,0) - ISNULL(@pordRound,0),0)
	/**/
	SET @pordItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] POI WHERE POI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14020324- Cahnge sp and add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
	IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
	BEGIN	
		/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
		SET @pordItemsAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordItemsAmount))					
		SET @pordSumAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordSumAmount))		
		SET @pordTax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTax))		
		SET @pordTotalAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTotalAmount))		
		SET @pordDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordDiscount))					
		SET @TotalDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)))	
		SET @TotalTaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)))			
		/*RZ14020626-END*/
	END
	/*RZ14020324- End*/

	


	/*************** ToTalAmountStr *******************/
	DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
	IF(@pordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN			
		SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
		SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
		/**/
		IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr /*+ N' —Ì«·'*/
		END
		ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = REPLACE(@pordTotalAmountStr,'-',' ') 
		END
	END
	/**************************************************/
	/*Update*/
	UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
	SET  [pordItemCount] = ISNULL(@pordItemCount,0)
		,[pordItemsAmount] = ISNULL(@pordItemsAmount,0)
		,[pordSumAmount] = ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0)
		,[pordTax] = ISNULL(@pordTax,0)
		,[pordTotalAmount] = @pordTotalAmount
		,[pordTotalAmountStr] = @pordTotalAmountStr
		,[pordDiscount] = ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0)
		,[Month1] = @Month1 
		/*RZ14020324- Cahnge sp and add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		,[pordItemsQty] = ISNULL(@SumQuantity,0)
		,[pordItemsDiscount] = ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)
		,[pordItemsTax] = ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)
		/*RZ14020324- End*/
	WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId
	/**/
	IF(@pordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @LangId INT
		/**/
		DECLARE Crsr CURSOR FOR
			SELECT LanguageID
			FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
			WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
		/**/
		OPEN Crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			While @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN	 
				DECLARE @pordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				IF(@LangId = 1065)
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang /*+ N' —Ì«·'*/
				END
				ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
					END							
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@pordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
				END
				/**/
				DECLARE @PreOrdIdLang INT
				SET @PreOrdIdLang = (SELECT [WSPreOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang] WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
				IF(@PreOrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
						SET [pordTotalAmountStr] = @pordTotalAmountStrLang
					WHERE [WSPreOrderLangID] = @PreOrdIdLang						
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
						([PreOrderID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[pordTotalAmountStr])
					VALUES
						(@PreOrderId
						,@LangId
						,@pordTotalAmountStrLang)
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			END
		CLOSE Crsr
		DEALLOCATE Crsr	
	END							
	/*==================================================*/			
END	

/*==================================================*/	
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmountsCurrency]	
@PreOrderId = @PreOrderId,
@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
/*RZ14030301- End*/
/*==================================================*/
/**/
END

				''' OR prsTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR prsFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsMobile = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' 
					+ CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), CP.prsLName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') )' 
			end
		END 
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID <> 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN 
		SET @from =

						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
						NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			 ') AND cmpTitleId = ' + Cast(@TitleID AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
	END
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID = 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ 
						REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			  /*AND cmpTitleId = @TitleID*/
			') AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
	END
END

/*--*/
/*--*/
/*--*/
IF @cmpDataPermision <> ''
BEGIN
EXECUTE(
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fld+ @from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +
' ) C union ' + 
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fldLimit+ @from+ @term +
') d
where CmpId Not IN ( SELECT CompanyId '+
@from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +' )'
)
END
ELSE
EXECUTE(@fld+ @from+ @term)
/**/
END






-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[ApispCheckDuplicatePrsEmail]
-- ==================================================



/*RZ970301*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispCheckDuplicatePrsEmail]
(		
/*
	declare @ResultDupcmpNumber NVARCHAR(50) = ''
	 EXEC ApispCheckDuplicatePrsEmail 
		@prsEmail = 'a@a.com', 
		@CurrId = 0,
		@ResultDupcmpNumber = @ResultDupcmpNumber OUTPUT 
		
	select @ResultDupcmpNumber
*/
@prsEmail NVARCHAR(50),
@CurrId INT,
@ResultDupcmpNumber  NVARCHAR(50) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @DupcmpNumber VARCHAR(50)
IF @prsEmail <> ''
	SELECT 
		@DupcmpNumber = 
			(SELECT CmpNumber FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) 
	FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons
	WHERE (ISNULL(prsEmail, '') = @prsEmail OR ISNULL(prsEmailII, '') = @prsEmail )
	AND CompanyPersonID <> @CurrId

SELECT @ResultDupcmpNumber = ISNULL(@DupcmpNumber, '') 
/**/
END




-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[ApispCheckDuplicatePrsMobile]
-- ==================================================


/*RZ970301*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispCheckDuplicatePrsMobile]
(		
/*
	declare @ResultDupcmpNumber NVARCHAR(50) = ''
	 EXEC ApispCheckDuplicatePrsMobile 
		@prsMobile = '09352461110', 
		@CurrId = 0,
		@ResultDupcmpNumber = @ResultDupcmpNumber OUTPUT 
		
	select @ResultDupcmpNumber
*/
@prsMobile NVARCHAR(50),
@CurrId INT,
@ResultDupcmpNumber  NVARCHAR(50) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @DupcmpNumber VARCHAR(50)
IF @prsMobile <> ''
	SELECT 
		@DupcmpNumber = 
			(SELECT CmpNumber FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) 
	FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons
	WHERE prsMobile = @prsMobile
	AND CompanyPersonID <> @CurrId
	
SELECT @ResultDupcmpNumber = ISNULL(@DupcmpNumber, '') 
/**/
END

-- ==================================================







/*NKR14030301*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertBuyOrder]
(
	@ApiBuyOrderOprId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordTitle NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordNumber BIGINT OUTPUT,
	@ApiBordOrderNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ApiBordDate NVARCHAR(8),
	@ApiBordOrderDate NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ApiBordOrderDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordCompanyId INT,
	@ApiBordCompanyNumber INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordCompanyCode NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCompanyPersonId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordProjectId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordSubject NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCalculateTax BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIsVerified BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordStatusId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIsFixed BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordPrintFormatId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordImportDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@ApiBordVerifierId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordVerifiedRegisterDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@ApiBordSettlementTypeId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordTime NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ApiBordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordReferUserId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordRound NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordDiscountTitle NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordNote NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordImportDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordCmpTitle NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
	/**/
	@ApiBordFacilityCmpId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordFacilityCmpNumber INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordFacilityCmpCode NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
	@ApiBordFacilityPrsId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordFacilityPrjId INT = NULL,
	/**/
	@ApiBordReqCmpId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordReqCmpNumber INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordReqCmpCode NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
	@ApiBordReqPrsId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordReqPrjId INT = NULL,
	/**/
	@ApiBordDeliveryTerm NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordDeliveryDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordDeliveryDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@ApiBordTaxCleared BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordTaxClearedDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordTaxClearedDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@ApiBordFacilityCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordFacilityCostPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordSupplierDeliveryDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordSupplierDeliveryDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@ApiBordForwarderName NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordFreightCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordFreightCurrUnId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordFreightCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordFinalFreightCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCustomsPart NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCustomsFee NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCustomsCurrUnId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordCustomsCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordFinalCustomsFee NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordFinalDeliveryDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordFinalDeliveryDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@ApiBordTotalFinalCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCommission NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCommissionCurrUnId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordCommissionCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordFinalCommission NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCostSheetId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordNumRef NVARCHAR(150) = NULL,
	@ApiBordDefaultSettingId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordVerificationWorkFlowId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordLinkAccFirstTransferDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordLinkAccLastTransferDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordCurrencyUnitIdI INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordCurrencyUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,
	/**/
	@UserId INT,
	@FPId INT,
	@Month1 SMALLINT,
	@RegisterDate DATETIME,
	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@AppType VARCHAR(20) = NULL,
	@FirstRegisterUserId INT = NULL,
	@FirstRegisterDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@FirstRegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	/**/
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT  = NULL,
	/**/
	@InsertedId INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	BEGIN TRY
		/**/
		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@FPId, 0) = 0)
			SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)

		IF (@ApiBordDateTime IS NULL)
			SET @InsertedId = -10

		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBuyOrderOprId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM WSBuyOrderOpr O WHERE O.BuyOrderOprID = @ApiBuyOrderOprId))
				SET @InsertedId = -20

		IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordCompanyId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordCompanyNumber, 0) <> 0)
			SET @ApiBordCompanyId = (SELECT C.CompanyID FROM CrmCompany C WHERE C.cmpNumber = @ApiBordCompanyNumber)
		IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordCompanyId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordCompanyCode, '') <> '')
			SET @ApiBordCompanyId = (SELECT C.CompanyID FROM CrmCompany C WHERE C.cmpCode = @ApiBordCompanyCode)

		IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordCompanyId, 0) = 0)
			SET @InsertedId = -30
		ELSE IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordCompanyId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @ApiBordCompanyId))
				SET @InsertedId = -40

		IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordCompanyPersonId, 0) = 0)
			SET @InsertedId = -50
		ELSE IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordCompanyPersonId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = @ApiBordCompanyPersonId))
				SET @InsertedId = -60

		IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordProjectId, 0) = 0)
			SET @InsertedId = -70
		ELSE IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordProjectId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM CrmProjects P WHERE P.ProjectID = @ApiBordProjectId))
				SET @InsertedId = -80

		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordStatusId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM WSBuyOrderStatus S WHERE S.BuyOrderStatusId = @ApiBordStatusId))
				SET @InsertedId = -90

		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordVerifierId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM Users U WHERE U.UserId = @ApiBordVerifierId))
				SET @InsertedId = -100

		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordReferUserId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM USERS U WHERE U.UserId = @ApiBordReferUserId))
				SET @InsertedId = -110
				
		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordCurrencyUnitId, 0) = 0)
			SET @InsertedId = -120
		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordCurrencyUnitId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM WSCurrencyUnits C WHERE C.CurrencyUnitID = @ApiBordCurrencyUnitId))
				SET @InsertedId = -125

		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordFacilityCmpNumber, 0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @ApiBordFacilityCmpId = (SELECT TOP 1 C.CompanyID FROM CrmCompany C WHERE C.cmpNumber = @ApiBordFacilityCmpNumber)
			/**/
		END
		ELSE IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordFacilityCmpCode, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @ApiBordFacilityCmpId = (SELECT TOP 1 C.CompanyID FROM CrmCompany C WHERE C.cmpCode = @ApiBordFacilityCmpCode)
			/**/
		END
		ELSE 
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordFacilityCmpId, 0) <> 0)
				IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @ApiBordFacilityCmpId))
					SET @InsertedId = -130
			/**/
		END
				
		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordFacilityPrsId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM CrmCompanyPersons P WHERE P.CompanyPersonID = @ApiBordFacilityPrsId))
				SET @InsertedId = -140
				
		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordFacilityPrjId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM CrmProjects P WHERE P.ProjectID = @ApiBordFacilityPrjId))
				SET @InsertedId = -150
				
		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordReqCmpNumber, 0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @ApiBordReqCmpId = (SELECT TOP 1 C.CompanyID FROM CrmCompany C WHERE C.cmpNumber = @ApiBordReqCmpNumber)
			/**/
		END
		ELSE IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordReqCmpCode, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @ApiBordReqCmpId = (SELECT TOP 1 C.CompanyID FROM CrmCompany C WHERE C.cmpCode = @ApiBordReqCmpCode)
			/**/
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordReqCmpId, 0) <> 0)
				IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @ApiBordReqCmpId))
					SET @InsertedId = -160
			/**/
		END

		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordReqPrsId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM CrmCompanyPersons P WHERE P.CompanyPersonID = @ApiBordReqPrsId))
				SET @InsertedId = -170
				
		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordReqPrjId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM CrmProjects P WHERE P.ProjectID = @ApiBordReqPrjId))
				SET @InsertedId = -180
				
		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordFreightCurrUnId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM WSCurrencyUnits C WHERE C.CurrencyUnitID = @ApiBordFreightCurrUnId))
				SET @InsertedId = -190
				
		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordCustomsCurrUnId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM WSCurrencyUnits C WHERE C.CurrencyUnitID = @ApiBordCustomsCurrUnId))
				SET @InsertedId = -200
				
		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordCommissionCurrUnId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM WSCurrencyUnits C WHERE C.CurrencyUnitID = @ApiBordCommissionCurrUnId))
				SET @InsertedId = -210
				
		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordCostSheetId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM WSCostSheets C WHERE C.CostSheetId = @ApiBordCostSheetId))
				SET @InsertedId = -220

		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordDefaultSettingId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM WSBuyOrderDefaultSetting S WHERE S.BuyOrderDefaultSettingId = @ApiBordDefaultSettingId))
				SET @InsertedId = -230
				
		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordVerificationWorkFlowId, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM WSVerificationWorkFlows W WHERE W.VerificationWorkFlowId = @ApiBordVerificationWorkFlowId))
				SET @InsertedId = -240

		ELSE IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordCurrencyUnitIdI, 0) <> 0)
			IF (NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM WSCurrencyUnits C WHERE C.CurrencyUnitID = @ApiBordCurrencyUnitIdI))
				SET @InsertedId = -250
				


		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) = 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			DECLARE @Lang TABLE(
				[Value] NVARCHAR(MAX),
				[LangId] INT,
				[FieldName] VARCHAR(500)
			)
			DELETE @Lang
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordTitle, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				/**/
				INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
				SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordTitle' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordTitle)
				WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(100) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
				WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
				/**/
			END
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordSubject, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				/**/
				INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
				SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordSubject' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordSubject)
				WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(100) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
				WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
				/**/
			END
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordDiscountTitle, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				/**/
				INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
				SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordDiscountTitle' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordDiscountTitle)
				WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(100) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
				WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
				/**/
			END
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordNote, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				/**/
				INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
				SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordNote' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordNote)
				WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(300) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
				WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
				/**/
			END
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordCmpTitle, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				/**/
				INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
				SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordcmpTitle' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordCmpTitle)
				WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(100) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
				WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
				/**/
			END
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordDeliveryTerm, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				/**/
				INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
				SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordDeliveryTerm' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordDeliveryTerm)
				WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
				WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
				/**/
			END
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordForwarderName, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				/**/
				INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
				SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordForwarderName' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordForwarderName)
				WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(1000) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
				WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
				/**/
			END
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordCustomsPart, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				/**/
				INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
				SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordCustomsPart' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordCustomsPart)
				WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(1000) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
				WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
				/**/
			END

		
			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordNumber, '') = '')
				SET @ApiBordNumber = (SELECT [dbo].[ApifnReturnBuyOrderNumber] (@ApiBuyOrderOprId))

			INSERT INTO WSBuyOrder(
				[BuyOrderOprId],
				[bordTitle],
				[bordNumber],
				[bordOrderNumber],
				[bordDate],
				[bordOrderDate],
				[bordOrderDateTime],
				[bordCompanyId],
				[bordCompanyPersonId],
				[bordProjectId],
				[bordSubject],
				[bordDiscount],
				[bordTaxIndex],
				[bordCalculateTax],
				[bordIsVerified],
				[bordStatusId],
				[bordIsFixed],
				[bordPrintFormatId],
				[bordImportDate],
				[bordVerifierId],
				[bordVerifiedRegisterDate],
				[bordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian],
				[bordSettlementTypeId],
				[bordDay],
				[bordTime],
				[bordTransportation],
				[bordReferUserId],
				[bordRound],
				[bordDiscountTitle],
				[bordNote],
				[bordDateTime],
				[bordImportDateTime],
				[bordcmpTitle],
				[bordCurrencyUnitId],
				[bordFacilityCmpId],
				[bordFacilityPrsId],
				[bordFacilityPrjId],
				[bordReqCmpId],
				[bordReqPrsId],
				[bordReqPrjId],
				[bordDeliveryTerm],
				[bordDeliveryDate],
				[bordDeliveryDatePersian],
				[bordTaxCleared],
				[bordTaxClearedDate],
				[bordTaxClearedDatePersian],
				[bordFacilityCost],
				[bordFacilityCostPercent],
				[bordSupplierDeliveryDate],
				[bordSupplierDeliveryDatePersian],
				[bordForwarderName],
				[bordFreightCost],
				[bordFreightCurrUnId],
				[bordFreightCurrencyRate],
				[bordFinalFreightCost],
				[bordCustomsPart],
				[bordCustomsFee],
				[bordCustomsCurrUnId],
				[bordCustomsCurrencyRate],
				[bordFinalCustomsFee],
				[bordFinalDeliveryDate],
				[bordFinalDeliveryDatePersian],
				[bordTotalFinalCost],
				[bordCommission],
				[bordCommissionCurrUnId],
				[bordCommissionCurrencyRate],
				[bordFinalCommission],
				[bordCostSheetId],
				[bordNumRef],
				[bordDefaultSettingId],
				[bordVerificationWorkFlowId],
				[bordLinkAccFirstTransferDate],
				[bordLinkAccLastTransferDate],
				[bordCurrencyUnitIdI],
				[bordCurrencyUnitIRate],
				/**/
				[UserId],
				[FPId],
				[Month1],
				[RegisterDate],
				[RegisterDatePersian],
				[AppType],
				[LanguageID],
				[bordFirstRegisterUserId],
				[FirstRegisterUserId],
				[FirstRegisterDate],
				[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			)
			VALUES(
				@ApiBuyOrderOprId,
				(SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordTitle'),
				@ApiBordNumber,
				@ApiBordOrderNumber,
				@ApiBordDate,
				@ApiBordOrderDate,
				@ApiBordOrderDateTime,
				@ApiBordCompanyId,
				@ApiBordCompanyPersonId,
				@ApiBordProjectId,
				(SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordSubject'),
				@ApiBordDiscount,
				@ApiBordTaxIndex,
				@ApiBordCalculateTax,
				@ApiBordIsVerified,
				@ApiBordStatusId,
				@ApiBordIsFixed,
				@ApiBordPrintFormatId,
				@ApiBordImportDate,
				@ApiBordVerifierId,
				@ApiBordVerifiedRegisterDate,
				@ApiBordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian,
				@ApiBordSettlementTypeId,
				CAST((DATEPART(WEEKDAY, @ApiBordDateTime)) AS NVARCHAR(20)),
				@ApiBordTime,
				@ApiBordTransportation,
				@ApiBordReferUserId,
				@ApiBordRound,
				(SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordDiscountTitle'),
				(SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordNote'),
				@ApiBordDateTime,
				@ApiBordImportDateTime,
				(SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordcmpTitle'),
				@ApiBordCurrencyUnitId,
				@ApiBordFacilityCmpId,
				@ApiBordFacilityPrsId,
				@ApiBordFacilityPrjId,
				@ApiBordReqCmpId,
				@ApiBordReqPrsId,
				@ApiBordReqPrjId,
				(SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordDeliveryTerm'),
				@ApiBordDeliveryDate,
				@ApiBordDeliveryDatePersian,
				@ApiBordTaxCleared,
				@ApiBordTaxClearedDate,
				@ApiBordTaxClearedDatePersian,
				@ApiBordFacilityCost,
				@ApiBordFacilityCostPercent,
				@ApiBordSupplierDeliveryDate,
				@ApiBordSupplierDeliveryDatePersian,
				(SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordForwarderName'),
				@ApiBordFreightCost,
				@ApiBordFreightCurrUnId,
				@ApiBordFreightCurrencyRate,
				@ApiBordFinalFreightCost,
				(SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordCustomsPart'),
				@ApiBordCustomsFee,
				@ApiBordCustomsCurrUnId,
				@ApiBordCustomsCurrencyRate,
				@ApiBordFinalCustomsFee,
				@ApiBordFinalDeliveryDate,
				@ApiBordFinalDeliveryDatePersian,
				@ApiBordTotalFinalCost,
				@ApiBordCommission,
				@ApiBordCommissionCurrUnId,
				@ApiBordCommissionCurrencyRate,
				@ApiBordFinalCommission,
				@ApiBordCostSheetId,
				@ApiBordNumRef,
				@ApiBordDefaultSettingId,
				@ApiBordVerificationWorkFlowId,
				@ApiBordLinkAccFirstTransferDate,
				@ApiBordLinkAccLastTransferDate,
				@ApiBordCurrencyUnitIdI,
				@ApiBordCurrencyUnitIRate,
				/**/
				@UserId,
				@FPId,
				@Month1,
				@RegisterDate,
				@RegisterDatePersian,
				@AppType,
				@DefaultLanguageID,
				@FirstRegisterUserId,
				@FirstRegisterUserId,
				@FirstRegisterDate,
				@FirstRegisterDatePersian
			)

			SET @InsertedId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

			IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) <> 0 AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM @Lang) > 0)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				INSERT INTO WSBuyOrder_Lang(
					[BuyOrderID], 
					[LanguageID], 
					[bordTitle], 
					[bordSubject], 
					[bordDiscountTitle], 
					[bordNote], 
					[bordcmpTitle], 
					[bordDeliveryTerm], 
					[bordForwarderName], 
					[bordCustomsPart]
				)
				SELECT @InsertedId,
						L.LanguageID,
						(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordTitle'),
						(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordSubject'),
						(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordDiscountTitle'),
						(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordNote'),
						(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordcmpTitle'),
						(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordDeliveryTerm'),
						(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordForwarderName'),
						(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordCustomsPart')
				FROM Languages L
				WHERE ISNULL(L.IsActive, 0) = 1
				  AND L.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
				  AND L.LanguageID IN (SELECT DISTINCT LL.[LangId] FROM @Lang LL)
				/**/
			END
		
		
			/*=========================== Calculate Order ====================*/
			EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmounts] @InsertedId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID	
			/**/
		END
		/**/
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		/**/
		SET @InsertedId = -1
		/**/
	END CATCH
	/**/
END









-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[ApispInsertBuyOrderItem]
-- ==================================================







/*NKR14030305*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertBuyOrderItem]
(
	@ApiBuyOrderItemId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBuyOrderId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordICost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordITotalCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIQuantityDesc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordICurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIOrderView INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICalculateTax BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordINotVisible BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordINotInSum BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIWHId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdCostGroupID INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICostSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordITaxCleared BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordITaxClearedDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordITaxClearedDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFacilityCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFacilityCostPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIForwarderName NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFreightCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFreightCurrUnId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFreightCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFinalFreightCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICustomsPart NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICustomsFee NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICustomsCurrUnId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordICustomsCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFinalCustomsFee NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordITotalFinalCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICommission NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICommissionCurrUnId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordICommissionCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFinalCommission NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICostSheetItemId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdUnitIdI INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdColorId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordILinkAccFirstTransferDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordILinkAccLastTransferDate DATETIME = NULL,
	/**/
	@FirstRegisterUserId INT = NULL,
	@FirstRegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@FirstRegisterDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@UserId INT = NULL,
	@FPId INT = NULL,
	@Month1 SMALLINT = NULL,
	@RegisterDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@AppType VARCHAR(20) = NULL,
	/**/
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT  = NULL,
	/**/
	@InsertedId INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	BEGIN TRY
		/**/
		IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIPrdId, 0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ApiBordIPrdCode, '') <> '')
			SET @ApiBordIPrdId = (SELECT P.ProductID FROM WSProducts P WHERE P.PrdCode = @ApiBordIPrdCode)

		IF (ISNULL(@FPId, 0) = 0)
			SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
		
		DECLARE @Lang TABLE(
			[Value] NVARCHAR(MAX),
			[LangId] INT,
			[FieldName] VARCHAR(500)
		)
		DELETE @Lang
		IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIDescription, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
			SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordIDescription' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordIDescription)
			WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
			WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
			/**/
		END
		IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIQuantityDesc, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
			SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordIQuantityDesc' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordIQuantityDesc)
			WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(1000) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
			WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
			/**/
		END
		IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIForwarderName, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
			SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordIForwarderName' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordIForwarderName)
			WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(1000) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
			WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
			/**/
		END
		IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordICustomsPart, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
			SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordICustomsPart' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordICustomsPart)
			WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(1000) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
			WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
			/**/
		END
		IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIPrdNickname, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
			SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordIPrdNickname' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordIPrdNickname)
			WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(1000) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
			WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
			/**/
		END

		INSERT INTO WSBuyOrderItems(
			[BuyOrderId],
			[bordIPrdId],
			[bordIQuantity],
			[bordICost],
			[bordITotalCost],
			[bordIDescription],
			[bordIQuantityDesc],
			[bordIDiscount],
			[bordIDiscountPercent],
			[bordIPrdUnitId],
			[bordICurrencyUnitId],
			[bordIOrderView],
			[bordIFCR],
			[bordITax],
			[bordITaxIndex],
			[bordICalculateTax],
			[bordINotVisible],
			[bordIFCRI],
			[bordINotInSum],
			[bordIWHId],
			[bordIPrdCostGroupID],
			[bordITracing],
			[bordICostSum],
			[bordIDiscountSum],
			[bordITaxSum],
			[bordITaxCleared],
			[bordITaxClearedDate],
			[bordITaxClearedDatePersian],
			[bordIFacilityCost],
			[bordIFacilityCostPercent],
			[bordIForwarderName],
			[bordIFreightCost],
			[bordIFreightCurrUnId],
			[bordIFreightCurrencyRate],
			[bordIFinalFreightCost],
			[bordICustomsPart],
			[bordICustomsFee],
			[bordICustomsCurrUnId],
			[bordICustomsCurrencyRate],
			[bordIFinalCustomsFee],
			[bordITotalFinalCost],
			[bordICommission],
			[bordICommissionCurrUnId],
			[bordICommissionCurrencyRate],
			[bordIFinalCommission],
			[bordICostSheetItemId],
			[bordIQuantityTotal],
			[bordIPrdUnitIdI],
			[bordIPrdUnitIRateResult],
			[bordIPrdColorId],
			[bordIPrdNickname],
			[bordILinkAccFirstTransferDate],
			[bordILinkAccLastTransferDate],
			/**/
			[UserId],
			[FPId],
			[Month1],
			[RegisterDate],
			[RegisterDatePersian],
			[AppType],
			[LanguageID],
			[FirstRegisterUserId],
			[FirstRegisterDate],
			[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
		)
		VALUES(
			@ApiBuyOrderId,
			@ApiBordIPrdId,
			@ApiBordIQuantity,
			@ApiBordICost,
			@ApiBordITotalCost,
			(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordIDescription'),
			(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordIQuantityDesc'),
			@ApiBordIDiscount,
			@ApiBordIDiscountPercent,
			@ApiBordIPrdUnitId,
			@ApiBordICurrencyUnitId,
			@ApiBordIOrderView,
			@ApiBordIFCR,
			@ApiBordITax,
			@ApiBordITaxIndex,
			@ApiBordICalculateTax,
			@ApiBordINotVisible,
			@ApiBordIFCRI,
			@ApiBordINotInSum,
			@ApiBordIWHId,
			@ApiBordIPrdCostGroupID,
			@ApiBordITracing,
			@ApiBordICostSum,
			@ApiBordIDiscountSum,
			@ApiBordITaxSum,
			@ApiBordITaxCleared,
			@ApiBordITaxClearedDate,
			@ApiBordITaxClearedDatePersian,
			@ApiBordIFacilityCost,
			@ApiBordIFacilityCostPercent,
			(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordIForwarderName'),
			@ApiBordIFreightCost,
			@ApiBordIFreightCurrUnId,
			@ApiBordIFreightCurrencyRate,
			@ApiBordIFinalFreightCost,
			(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordICustomsPart'),
			@ApiBordICustomsFee,
			@ApiBordICustomsCurrUnId,
			@ApiBordICustomsCurrencyRate,
			@ApiBordIFinalCustomsFee,
			@ApiBordITotalFinalCost,
			@ApiBordICommission,
			@ApiBordICommissionCurrUnId,
			@ApiBordICommissionCurrencyRate,
			@ApiBordIFinalCommission,
			@ApiBordICostSheetItemId,
			@ApiBordIQuantityTotal,
			@ApiBordIPrdUnitIdI,
			@ApiBordIPrdUnitIRateResult,
			@ApiBordIPrdColorId,
			(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordIPrdNickname'),
			@ApiBordILinkAccFirstTransferDate,
			@ApiBordILinkAccLastTransferDate,
			/**/
			@UserId,
			@FPId,
			@Month1,
			@RegisterDate,
			@RegisterDatePersian,
			@AppType,
			@DefaultLanguageID,
			@UserId,
			@RegisterDate,
			@RegisterDatePersian
		)

		SET @InsertedId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

		IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) <> 0 AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM @Lang) > 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			INSERT INTO WSBuyOrderItems_Lang(
				[BuyOrderItemId], 
				[LanguageID], 
				[bordIDescription], 
				[bordIQuantityDesc], 
				[bordIForwarderName], 
				[bordICustomsPart], 
				[bordIPrdNickname]
			)
			SELECT @InsertedId,
					L.LanguageID,
					(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordIDescription'),
					(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordIQuantityDesc'),
					(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordIForwarderName'),
					(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordICustomsPart'),
					(SELECT LG.[Value] FROM @Lang LG WHERE LG.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LG.FieldName = 'BordIPrdNickname')
				FROM Languages L
				WHERE ISNULL(L.IsActive, 0) = 1
				AND L.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			/**/
		END
		/**/
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		/**/
		SET @InsertedId = -1
		/**/
	END CATCH

	IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) > 0)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		BEGIN TRY
			/**/
			EXEC ApispCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmounts
				@BuyOrderId=@ApiBuyOrderId,
				@Month1=@Month1,
				@LanguageID=@LanguageID,
				@DefaultLanguageID=@DefaultLanguageID
			/**/
		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
			/**/
			SET @InsertedId = -2
			/**/
		END CATCH
		/**/
	END
	/**/
END

/*RZ14011124*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertCompanyChangeHistory]
/*
	declare @InsertedId int = 0
	exec ApispInsertCompanyChangeHistory 
	@CompanyId = 68697,
	@FieldName = N'cmpReferUserId',
	@OldValue = N'56',
	@NewValue = N'3',
	@Desc = N'', 
	@CmpChangeHistory_Lang = NULL, 	
	@UserId = 3,
	@RegisterDate = '2023-02-13',
	@RegisterDatePersian = '14011124',
	@AppType = 'dasti',
	@DefaultLanguageID = 1065,		
	@InsertedId = @InsertedId OUTPUT
	select @InsertedId 
*/
(
@CompanyId INT,
@FieldName NVARCHAR(300),
@OldValue NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@NewValue NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@Desc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, 
@CmpChangeHistory_Lang NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, 
/**/
@UserId INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(20),
@DefaultLanguageID INT,
/**/
@InsertedId INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	/*============================================================================*/
	/*================================ Insert ====================================*/
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory]
        ([CompanyID]
        ,[FieldName]
        ,[OldValue]
        ,[NewValue]
        ,[Desc]
        ,[UserId]
        ,[RegisterDate]
        ,[RegisterDatePersian]        
        ,[AppType]
        ,[LanguageID])
    VALUES
        (@CompanyID
        ,@FieldName
        ,@OldValue
        ,@NewValue
        ,@Desc
        ,@UserId
        ,@RegisterDate
        ,@RegisterDatePersian
        ,@AppType
        ,@DefaultLanguageID)
	SET @InsertedId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()	
	IF(ISNULL(@InsertedId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@CmpChangeHistory_Lang,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory_Lang]
			([CompanyChangeHistoryId]
			,[LanguageID]
			,[OldValue]
			,[NewValue]
			,[Desc])
		SELECT 
			@InsertedId,
			JS.[LanguageID],
			JS.[OldValue],
			JS.[NewValue],
			JS.[Desc]
		FROM OPENJSON(ISNULL(@CmpChangeHistory_Lang,'[]'))
		WITH (
			[LanguageID] INT '$.LanguageID',
			[OldValue] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.OldValue',
			[NewValue] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.NewValue',
			[Desc] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.Desc'
		) AS JS
		WHERE JS.[LanguageID] <> @DefaultLanguageID
		AND JS.[LanguageID] IN(SELECT L.[LanguageID]
							   FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
							   WHERE ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1)
	/**/
	END		
	/*===========================================================================*/
/**/
END












-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderItemSpecificationAll]
-- ==================================================




/*RZ990206*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderItemSpecificationAll]
(		
/*  
	exec ApispInsertPreOrderItemSpecificationAll 2782,7932,3,2,2,'13960216','web',1065 
*/
@PrdId INT,
@PreOrderItemId INT,
@UserId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@FPId INT,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50),
@LanguageID INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*-------------------------- Begin define variables ------------------------------*/
DECLARE @Specification NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @SpecificationI NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @SpecificationII NVARCHAR(MAX)
DECLARE @ProductSpecificationID INT
DECLARE @ItemSpecificationId INT
/*-------------------------- End define variables --------------------------------*/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION
    Declare Crsr_Add CURSOR For
		SELECT  
			Specification,
			SpecificationI,
			SpecificationII,
			ProductSpecificationID
		FROM dbo.WSProductSpecification
		WHERE PrdId = @PrdId				
	OPEN Crsr_Add
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add INTO @Specification, @SpecificationI,
				@SpecificationII, @ProductSpecificationID
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN
		INSERT INTO dbo.WSPreOrderItemSpecification (
			PreOrderItemId,
			Specification,
			SpecificationI,
			SpecificationII,
			UserId,
			Month1,
			FPId,
			RegisterDate,
			RegisterDatePersian,
			AppType,			
			FirstRegisterUserId,
			FirstRegisterDate,
			FirstRegisterDatePersian,
			LanguageID
		) 
		VALUES(
			@PreOrderItemId,
			@Specification,
			@SpecificationI,
			@SpecificationII,
			@UserId,
			@Month1,
			@FPId,
			GETDATE(),
			@RegisterDatePersian ,
			@AppType,			
			@UserId,
			GETDATE(),
			@RegisterDatePersian,
			@LanguageID 
		)
	
		SET @ItemSpecificationId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		/*Insert records in lang table*/
			
		INSERT INTO dbo.WSPreOrderItemSpecification_Lang (
			 PreOrderItemSpcflID
			,LanguageID
			,Specification	
			,SpecificationI
			,SpecificationII			    			  
		) 
		SELECT
			@ItemSpecificationId,
			PSLN.LanguageID,
			PSLN.Specification,
			PSLN.SpecificationI,
			PSLN.SpecificationII
		FROM dbo.WSProductSpecification PS 
		INNER JOIN dbo.WSProductSpecification_Lang PSLN ON PS.ProductSpecificationID = PSLN.ProductSpecificationID
		WHERE  PS.PrdId = @PrdId 
		AND PS.ProductSpecificationID = @ProductSpecificationID
		AND @ItemSpecificationId NOT IN 
			(SELECT PreOrderItemSpcflID FROM WSPreOrderItemSpecification_Lang POISLN
			 WHERE POISLN.LanguageID =  PSLN.LanguageID AND @ItemSpecificationId = POISLN.PreOrderItemSpcflID) 
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add INTO @Specification, @SpecificationI,
				@SpecificationII, @ProductSpecificationID
	END
	CLOSE Crsr_Add
	DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add
    /*SELECT 1 AS Result*/
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
    CLOSE Crsr_Add
	DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add	
	/*SELECT 0 AS Result */
END CATCH

/**/	
END








-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[ApispInsertProductPrice]
-- ==================================================





/*RZ980415*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertProductPrice]
/*
	exec ApispInsertProductPrice 						
*/
(
/*Info for Login*/
@UserName NVARCHAR(50),
@Password NVARCHAR(50),
/*Price*/
@DocDate NVARCHAR(50),
@DocDateTime DATETIME,
@DocNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@Price NUMERIC(18, 2),
@CurrencyPrice NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@CurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
@CooperationPrice NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@PricePercent NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@DiscountPrecent NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@Discount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@OtherPrice NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@Description NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
@WHId INT = NULL,
@ProductPriceGroupId INT,
/*Product*/
@ProductId INT = NULL,
@PrdCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
/**/
@InsertedPrdPriceId INT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/**********************************Default LanguageID ***************************/
DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
DECLARE @FPId INT
SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Error & Variables *******************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
DECLARE @PrdId INT

/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Group ****************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*Price group*/
IF((@ProductPriceGroupId IS NULL OR @ProductPriceGroupId <= 0))
BEGIN
	SET @ProductPriceGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProductPriceGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductPriceGroup])
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	DECLARE @PrdPriceId INT
	SET @PrdPriceId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProductPriceGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductPriceGroup] WHERE [ProductPriceGroupID] = @ProductPriceGroupId)
	IF(@PrdPriceId IS NULL OR @PrdPriceId <= 0)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Not Group|'
END
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Product **************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
IF(@PrdCode IS NOT NULL AND @PrdCode <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @PrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProductID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE [PrdCode] = @PrdCode)
END
ELSE IF (@ProductId IS NOT NULL AND @ProductId > 0)
BEGIN
	SET @PrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProductID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE [ProductID] = @ProductId)
END
/**/
IF(@PrdId IS NULL OR @PrdId <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Not Prd|'
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Insert Product Price ****************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRY  
    IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')/*No Error*/
	BEGIN
		/*BEGIN TRAN*/
			/*Main table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSProductPrice]
				   ([PrdId]
				   ,[DocDate]
				   ,[DocNumber]
				   ,[Price]
				   ,[CurrencyPrice]
				   ,[CurrencyUnitId]
				   ,[CooperationPrice]
				   ,[PricePercent]
				   ,[DiscountPrecent]
				   ,[Discount]
				   ,[OtherPrice]
				   ,[Description]
				   ,[UserId]
				   ,[Month1]
				   ,[FPId]
				   ,[RegisterDate]
				   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
				   ,[WHId]
				   ,[ProductPriceGroupId]
				   ,[AppType]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
				   ,[DocDateTime])
			 VALUES
				   (@PrdId
				   ,@DocDate
				   ,@DocNumber
				   ,@Price
				   ,@CurrencyPrice
				   ,@CurrencyUnitId
				   ,@CooperationPrice
				   ,@PricePercent
				   ,@DiscountPrecent
				   ,@Discount
				   ,@OtherPrice
				   ,@Description
				   ,@UserId
				   ,@Month1
				   ,@FPId
				   ,@RegisterDate
				   ,@RegisterDatePersian
				   ,@WHId
				   ,@ProductPriceGroupId
				   ,@AppType
				   ,@DefaultLanguageID
				   ,@UserId
				   ,@RegisterDatePersian
				   ,@RegisterDate
				   ,@DocDateTime)
			/**/
			SET @InsertedPrdPriceId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSProductPrice_Lang]
			   ([PrdPriceID]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[Description])
			SELECT
				@InsertedPrdPriceId
				,LN.LanguageID
				,@Description
			FROM dbo.Languages LN 
			WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
			AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1     
		/*IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		--BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Product Price' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		--ELSE 
		--COMMIT TRAN*/ 
	END
	/**/
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	 /*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
     SET @InsertedPrdPriceId = 0
	 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error'
 	 SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertProducts]
/*
	exec ApispInsertProducts 					
*/
(
/*Info for Login*/
@UserName NVARCHAR(50),
@Password NVARCHAR(50),
/*Product*/
@PrdCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrdName NVARCHAR(1000),
@PrdLatinName NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
@PrdType NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@PrdDesign NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@PrdSize NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrdColorId INT = NULL,
@PrdUnitId INT = NULL,
@PrdMinQty INT = NULL,
@PrdMaxQty INT = NULL,
@PrdSleepDay INT = NULL,
@PrdFirstSerial NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrdSecondSerial NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrdThirdSerial NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrdFirstBarcode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrdSecondBarcode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrdThirdBarcode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrdDescription NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@PrdBarCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrdBarCodeTypeId INT = NULL,
@PrdAutoNumBarcode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrdAutoNumBarCodeTypeId INT = NULL,
@PrdAutoCharBarcode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrdAutoCharBarCodeTypeId INT = NULL,
@PrdModel NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
@PrdInactiveInSale BIT = NULL,
@PrdInactiveInBuy BIT = NULL,
@PrdSHPIsActive  BIT = NULL,
@PrdSHPTitle NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@PrdSHPCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrdSHPModel NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
@PrdSHPDescription NTEXT = NULL,
@PrdSHPDescriptionII NTEXT = NULL,
@PrdSHPStockManageTypeId INT = NULL,
@PrdSHPStockManualyCount FLOAT = NULL,
@PrdSHPHiddenPriceInNoStock BIT = NULL,
@PrdSHPSelfSupplier BIT = NULL,
@PrdSHPOtherSupplierId INT = NULL,
/*groups*/
@PrdFirstGroupId INT = NULL,
@FGCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PrdSecondGroupId INT = NULL,
@SGCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
/**/
@FlagInsertDupPrdCode BIT = 0,
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
/**/
@InsertedProdcutId INT OUTPUT,
@InsertedProductCode NVARCHAR(50) OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,

/*NKR14020727*/
@PrdValidity BIT = NULL,
@PrdValidityDay INT = NULL,
@PrdValidityTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@PrdValidityCounting BIT = NULL,
@PrdValidityCountingNum INT = NULL,
@PrdValidityCountingMin INT = NULL,
@PrdValidityCountingNumIsActive BIT = NULL,
@PrdValidityCountingNumNeedOrder BIT = NULL,
@PrdValidityCountingNumDefaultVal INT = NULL,
@PrdValidityCountingNumNoCheckRemain BIT = NULL,
@PrdValidityCountingMinIsActive BIT = NULL,
@PrdValidityCountingMinNeedOrder BIT = NULL,
@PrdValidityCountingMinDefaultVal INT = NULL,
@PrdValidityCountingMinNoCheckRemain BIT = NULL,
@PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd BIT = NULL,
@PrdValidityControlFOPrjTitleGroup BIT = NULL,
@PrdValidityControlFOPrjTitleGroupIds NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@PrdValidityControlFODeActivePrjTitleGroupDesc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
/*!NKR14020727*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/**********************************Default LanguageID ***************************/
DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])

/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
DECLARE @FPId INT
SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Error & Variables *******************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT

/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Groups ***************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*Product first group*/
IF((@PrdFirstGroupId IS NULL OR @PrdFirstGroupId <= 0) AND (@FGCode IS NOT NULL AND @FGCode <> ''))
BEGIN
	SET @PrdFirstGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 [PrdFirstGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup] WHERE [FGCode] = @FGCode)
END
/**/
IF(@PrdFirstGroupId IS NULL OR @PrdFirstGroupId <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Not FG|'	
/*Product second group*/
IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')
BEGIN
	IF((@PrdSecondGroupId IS NULL OR @PrdSecondGroupId <= 0 ) AND (@SGCode IS NOT NULL AND @SGCode <> ''))
	BEGIN
		SET @PrdSecondGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 [PrdSecondGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] WHERE [SGCode] = @SGCode AND [FirstGroupId] = @PrdFirstGroupId)
	END
	/**/
	IF(@PrdSecondGroupId IS NULL  OR @PrdSecondGroupId < =0)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Not SG|'	

	/**/
	DECLARE @FirstGroupId INT 
	SET @FirstGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] WHERE [PrdSecondGroupID] = @PrdSecondGroupId)	
	IF(@FirstGroupId <> @PrdFirstGroupId)
	BEGIN
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Conflict SFG|'	
	END
END
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* PrdCode **************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')
BEGIN
	IF(@PrdCode IS NULL OR @PrdCode = '')
	BEGIN
		SET @PrdCode = (SELECT dbo.ApifnMakeAndReturnPrdCode (@PrdFirstGroupId, @PrdSecondGroupId))
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @DupPrdId INT, @DupPrdName NVARCHAR(1000)
		SET @DupPrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductId FROM dbo.WSProducts WHERE prdCode = @PrdCode)
		IF(@DupPrdId IS NOT NULL AND @DupPrdId > 0 AND @FlagInsertDupPrdCode = 0)
		BEGIN
			IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
			BEGIN
				SET @DupPrdName = (SELECT prdName FROM dbo.WSProducts WHERE ProductId = @DupPrdId)
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @DupPrdName = ISNULL((SELECT prdName FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN WHERE ProductId = @DupPrdId  AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),
										  (SELECT prdName FROM dbo.WSProducts WHERE ProductId = @DupPrdId))				
			END
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupPrdCode= ' + ISNULL(@DupPrdName,'') + '|'	
		END		
	END
END
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Insert Product **********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRY  
    IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')/*No Error*/
	BEGIN
		/*BEGIN TRAN*/
			/*Main table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSProducts]
			   ([PrdGroupID]
			   ,[PrdCode]
			   ,[PrdName]
			   ,[PrdLatinName]
			   ,[PrdType]
			   ,[PrdDesign]
			   ,[PrdSize]
			   ,[PrdColorId]
			   ,[PrdUnitId]
			   ,[PrdMinQty]

			   ,[PrdSleepDay]
			   ,[PrdFirstSerial]
			   ,[PrdSecondSerial]

			   ,[PrdFirstBarcode]
			   ,[PrdSecondBarcode]
			   ,[PrdThirdBarcode]
			   ,[PrdDescription]
			   ,[UserId]
			   ,[Month1]
			   ,[FPId]
			   ,[RegisterDate]
			   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[PrdBarCode]
			   ,[PrdBarCodeTypeId]
			   ,[PrdAutoNumBarcode]
			   ,[PrdAutoNumBarCodeTypeId]
			   ,[PrdAutoCharBarcode]
			   ,[prdAutoCharBarCodeTypeId]
			   ,[PrdModel]
			   ,[PrdInactiveInSale]
			   ,[PrdInactiveInBuy]
			   ,[AppType]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
			   ,[PrdSHPIsActive]
			   ,[PrdSHPTitle]
			   ,[PrdSHPCode]
			   ,[PrdSHPModel]
			   ,[PrdSHPDescription]
			   ,[PrdSHPDescriptionII]
			   ,[PrdSHPStockManageTypeId]
			   ,[PrdSHPStockManualyCount]
			   ,[PrdSHPHiddenPriceInNoStock]
			   ,[PrdSHPSelfSupplier]
			   ,[PrdSHPOtherSupplierId]
			   /*NKR14020730- Start*/
			   ,[PrdValidity]
			   ,[PrdValidityDay]
			   ,[PrdValidityTime]
			   ,[PrdValidityCounting]
			   ,[PrdValidityCountingNum]
			   ,[PrdValidityCountingMin]
			   ,[PrdValidityCountingNumIsActive]
			   ,[PrdValidityCountingNumNeedOrder]
			   ,[PrdValidityCountingNumDefaultVal]
			   ,[PrdValidityCountingNumNoCheckRemain]
			   ,[PrdValidityCountingMinIsActive]
			   ,[PrdValidityCountingMinNeedOrder]
			   ,[PrdValidityCountingMinDefaultVal]
			   ,[PrdValidityCountingMinNoCheckRemain]
			   ,[PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd]
			   ,[PrdValidityControlFOPrjTitleGroup]
			   ,[PrdValidityControlFOPrjTitleGroupIds]
			   ,[PrdValidityControlFODeActivePrjTitleGroupDesc]
			   /*NKR14020730- End*/
			   )
			VALUES
			   (@PrdSecondGroupId
			   ,@PrdCode
			   ,@PrdName
			   ,@PrdLatinName
			   ,@PrdType
			   ,@PrdDesign
			   ,@PrdSize
			   ,@PrdColorId
			   ,@PrdUnitId
			   ,@PrdMinQty
			   ,@PrdMaxQty
			   ,@PrdSleepDay
			   ,@PrdFirstSerial
			   ,@PrdSecondSerial
			   ,@PrdThirdSerial
			   ,@PrdFirstBarcode
			   ,@PrdSecondBarcode
			   ,@PrdThirdBarcode
			   ,@PrdDescription
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@Month1
			   ,@FPId
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@PrdBarCode
			   ,@PrdBarCodeTypeId
			   ,@PrdAutoNumBarcode
			   ,@PrdAutoNumBarCodeTypeId
			   ,@PrdAutoCharBarcode
			   ,@PrdAutoCharBarCodeTypeId
			   ,@PrdModel
			   ,@PrdInactiveInSale
			   ,@PrdInactiveInBuy
			   ,@AppType
			   ,@DefaultLanguageID
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@PrdSHPIsActive
			   ,@PrdSHPTitle
			   ,@PrdSHPCode

			   ,@PrdSHPDescriptionII
			   ,@PrdSHPStockManageTypeId
			   ,@PrdSHPStockManualyCount
			   ,@PrdSHPHiddenPriceInNoStock
			   ,@PrdSHPSelfSupplier
			   ,@PrdSHPOtherSupplierId
			   /*NKR14020727*/
			   ,@PrdValidity
			   ,@PrdValidityDay
			   ,@PrdValidityTime
			   ,@PrdValidityCounting
			   ,@PrdValidityCountingNum
			   ,@PrdValidityCountingMin
			   ,@PrdValidityCountingNumIsActive

			   ,@PrdValidityCountingNumDefaultVal
			   ,@PrdValidityCountingNumNoCheckRemain
			   ,@PrdValidityCountingMinIsActive
			   ,@PrdValidityCountingMinNeedOrder
			   ,@PrdValidityCountingMinDefaultVal
			   ,@PrdValidityCountingMinNoCheckRemain
			   ,@PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd
			   ,@PrdValidityControlFOPrjTitleGroup
			   ,@PrdValidityControlFOPrjTitleGroupIds
			   ,@PrdValidityControlFODeActivePrjTitleGroupDesc
				/*!NKR14020727*/
			   )
			/**/
			SET @InsertedProdcutId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			SET @InsertedProductCode = @PrdCode
			/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang]
			   ([ProductID]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[PrdName]
			   ,[PrdLatinName]
			   ,[PrdType]
			   ,[PrdDesign]
			   ,[PrdSize]
			   ,[PrdDescription]
			   ,[PrdModel]
			   ,[PrdSHPTitle]
			   ,[PrdSHPModel]
			   ,[PrdSHPDescription]
			   ,[PrdSHPDescriptionII])
			SELECT
				@InsertedProdcutId
			   ,LN.LanguageID
			   ,@PrdName
			   ,@PrdLatinName
			   ,@PrdType
			   ,@PrdDesign
			   ,@PrdSize
			   ,@PrdDescription
			   ,@PrdModel
			   ,@PrdSHPTitle
			   ,@PrdSHPModel
			   ,@PrdSHPDescription
			   ,@PrdSHPDescriptionII
			FROM dbo.Languages LN 
			WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
			AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1
		/*IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		--BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Product' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		--ELSE 
		--COMMIT TRAN*/ 
	END
	/**/
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	 /*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
     SET @InsertedProdcutId = 0
	 SET @InsertedProductCode = ''
	 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error'
 	 SELECT 0	 
END CATCH

END
