	select @InsertedDocId INT = 0,	@InsertedDocNumber INT = 0,	@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) =''

	*/

	@OprId INT = NULL,
	@DocNumber INT = NULL,
	@DocDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@DocDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@SenderId INT = NULL,
	@SenderName NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
	@FactorNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@Description NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
	@IsFix SMALLINT = NULL,
	@WHId INT = NULL,
	@OrderId INT = NULL,
	@BuyOrderId INT = NULL,
	@SendAddress NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
	@DriverName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
	@DriverTel NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@FreightName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
	@BijakNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@CarNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@WHIdI INT = NULL,	
	@cmpTitle NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,
	@DocLinkAccCodeId INT = NULL,
	@DocLinkAccCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@DocLinkAccDocId INT = NULL,
	@DocLinkAccDocNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@DocLinkAccVoucherId INT = NULL,
	@DocLinkAccVoucherNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	/*Company AND Person AND Project*/
	@CompanyId INT = NULL,
	@CompanyPersonId INT = NULL,
	@ProjectId INT = NULL,
	/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
	@CompanyNumber INT = NULL,
	@CompanyCode NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
	@ProjectTitle NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
	@DefualtProjectTitle NVARCHAR(100),

	/*RZ990519- End*/
	/*DocItems*/
	@DocItemsJson NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	/*DocItemSerials*/
	@DocItemSerialsJson NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	/**/
	@Month1 SMALLINT,
	@UserId INT,
	@RegisterDate DATETIME,
	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),	
	@RegisterDay NVARCHAR(50),
	@RegisterTime NVARCHAR(50),
	/**/
	@AppType VARCHAR(20),
	@LanguageID INT,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT,
	@InsertedDocId INT OUTPUT,
	@InsertedDocNumber INT OUTPUT,/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
	@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/

	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main

	DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT

	/********************************************************************************/
	/************************************ FPId **************************************/
	DECLARE @FPId INT
	SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
	/************************************ WHId **************************************/
	/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
	IF(@WHId IS NULL OR @WHId <= 0)
		SET @WHId = (SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE ISNULL([WrhsDeActive],0) = 1)
	/*RZ990519- End*/
	/*===================== Start Checks =====================*/
	
	BEGIN/*No DocOprID*/
		IF (ISNULL(@OprId, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 DO.DocOprID FROM WSDocOpr DO WHERE DO.DocOprID = @OprId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No DocOprID|'
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @OprId = (SELECT TOP 1 [DocOprID] FROM dbo.WSDocOpr OO ORDER BY OrderView )/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
		END
	END

	/*========== Doc Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN/*No WHId*/
		IF (ISNULL(@WHId, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 WH.WareHouseID FROM WsWareHouse WH WHERE WH.WareHouseID = @WHId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No WHId|'
		END
	END

	/*========== Doc Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN/*No OrderId*/
		IF (ISNULL(@OrderId, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 O.OrderID FROM WSOrder O WHERE O.OrderID = @OrderId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrderId|'
		END
	END

	/*========== Doc Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN/*No BuyOrderId*/
		IF (ISNULL(@BuyOrderId, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 BO.BuyOrderID FROM WSBuyOrder BO WHERE BO.BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BuyOrderId|'
		END
	END

	/*========== Doc Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN/*No WHIdI*/
		IF (ISNULL(@WHIdI, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 WH.WareHouseID FROM WsWareHouse WH WHERE WH.WareHouseID = @WHIdI) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No WHIdI|'
		END
	END

	/*========== Doc Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN/*No CompanyId*/
		IF (ISNULL(@CompanyId, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 DO.CompanyID FROM CrmCompany DO WHERE DO.CompanyID = @CompanyId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpId|'
		END
		/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
		ELSE IF (@CompanyNumber IS NOT NULL AND @CompanyNumber <> '')
		BEGIN
			SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [cmpNumber] = @CompanyNumber)
		END
		ELSE IF (@CompanyCode IS NOT NULL AND @CompanyCode <> '')
		BEGIN
			SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [cmpCode] = @CompanyCode)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@CompanyId, 0) = 0)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpId|'
		/*RZ990519- End*/
	END
	
	/*========== Doc Checking ==========*/
	BEGIN/*No CompanyPersonId*/
		DECLARE @PrsId INT
		IF (ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 CCP.CompanyPersonID FROM CrmCompanyPersons CCP WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = @CompanyPersonId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsId|'
		END
		/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
		ELSE IF((ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId, 0) > 0) AND ISNULL(@CompanyId, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN	
			SET @PrsId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] 
							WHERE [CompanyPersonID] = @CompanyPersonId AND [prsCompanyId] = @CompanyId)
			IF(@PrsId IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsId|'
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @CompanyPersonId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons WHERE prsCompanyId = @CompanyId ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID))
			IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
					([prsCompanyId]
					,[prsLName]           
					,[UserId]
					,[Year1]
					,[Month1]
					,[RegisterDate]
					,[RegisterDatePersian]
					,[ImportDate]
					,[ImportDateTime]
					,[prsFirstRegisterUserId]           
					,[AppType]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[FirstRegisterUserId]
					,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]           
					,[FirstRegisterDate])
				VALUES
					(@CompanyId
					,N'«Ì‰ —‰ Ì'           
					,@UserId
					,CAST(@FPId AS SMALLINT)
					,@Month1
					,@RegisterDate
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDate
					,@UserId
					,@AppType
					,@DefaultLanguageID
					,@UserId
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDate)
	
				SET @CompanyPersonId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
				/*MultiLanguage*/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang]
					   ([CompanyPersonID]
					   ,[LanguageID]
					   ,[prsLName])
				SELECT
						@CompanyPersonId
					   ,L.LanguageID
					   ,N'«Ì‰ —‰ Ì'
				FROM dbo.Languages L
				WHERE ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1 
				AND L.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			/**/
			END		
		/**/
		END		
		/*RZ990519- End*/
	END
	
	/*========== Doc Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN/*No ProjectId*/
		DECLARE @PrjId INT
		IF (ISNULL(@ProjectId, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 CCP.ProjectID FROM CrmProjects CCP WHERE CCP.ProjectID = @ProjectId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrjId|'
		END
		/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
		ELSE IF ((ISNULL(@ProjectId, 0) > 0) AND (ISNULL(@CompanyId, 0) > 0))
		BEGIN
			SET @PrjId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProjectID] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId AND [prjCompanyId] = @CompanyId)
			IF(@PrjId IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrjId|'
		END		
		/*RZ990519- End*/
	END
	/*PORODUCT*/
	IF(ISNULL(@DocItemsJson,'') = '')	/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No Prd|'
	
	/********************************************************************************/
	/********************************* DocNumber ************************************/
	/********************************************************************************/	
	/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
	IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')
	BEGIN
		IF(ISNULL(@DocNumber,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @DocNumber = (SELECT [dbo].[ApifnReturnDocNumber] (@OprId))
		END 
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			DECLARE @DupDocNumber INT
			SET @DupDocNumber = (SELECT TOP 1 [DocNumber] from [dbo].[ApifnCheckDuplicateDocNumber](@DocNumber, 0, @OprId))
			IF(ISNULL(@DupDocNumber,0) > 0)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupDocNumber= ' + CAST(ISNULL(@DupDocNumber,0) AS NVARCHAR(200)) + '|'				
		END
	END
	/*RZ990519- End*/

	/*====================== End Checks ======================*/
	
	IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')/*No Error*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		BEGIN TRAN
			/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
			/*============================== Project =========================*/
			BEGIN TRAN
				IF(@ProjectId IS NULL)
				BEGIN
					DECLARE @prjcnvsStatusGroupId INT			
					SET @prjcnvsStatusGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 ConversationStatusGroupID FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatusGroups WHERE ConversationStatusGroupID > 0)
					/**/
					IF(@ProjectTitle IS NULL OR @ProjectTitle = '')
						SET @ProjectTitle = @DefualtProjectTitle
					/**/
					SET @ProjectId =(SELECT TOP 1 P.ProjectID FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P 
							  WHERE P.prjCompanyId = @CompanyId 
							  AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(P.prjTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
							  NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like '%' + 
							  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@ProjectTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
							  NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'))

					IF(@ProjectId IS NULL)
					BEGIN
						SET @ProjectId = (SELECT TOP 1 ProjectId From dbo.CrmProjects WHERE prjCompanyId = @CompanyId ORDER BY [prjOrderView] DESC,prjStartDate DESC,[ProjectID] DESC)/*RZ14021005- Change orderView in project add desc to prjOrderView*/
						IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							/*-------------------------------------------------*/
							/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/
							DECLARE @prjOrderView INT = 0
							EXEC [dbo].[ApispReturnCompanyProjectOrderView]
							@CompanyId  = @CompanyId,
							@prjOrderView = @prjOrderView  OUTPUT					
							/*RZ14021004- End*/
							/*-------------------------------------------------*/
							/*Main table*/
							INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmProjects]
								   ([prjCompanyId]
								   ,[prjTitle]
								   ,[prjStartDate]
								   ,[prjStartDay]
								   ,[prjStartTime]
								   ,[prjStartDateTime]
								   ,[prjStarterUserId]
								   ,[prjFirstRegisterDate]
								   ,[prjFirstRegisterUserId]
								   ,[UserId]
								   ,[Year1]
								   ,[Month1]
								   ,[RegisterDate]
								   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
								   ,[AppType]
								   ,[LanguageID]
								   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
								   ,[FirstRegisterDateTime]
								   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
								   ,[prjFirstRegisterDateTime]
								   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
								   ,[prjcnvsStatusGroupId]
								   ,[prjOrderView]/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/)
							 VALUES
								   (@CompanyId
								   ,@ProjectTitle
								   ,@RegisterDatePersian
								   ,@RegisterDay
								   ,@RegisterTime
								   ,@RegisterDate
								   ,@UserId
								   ,@RegisterDatePersian
								   ,@UserId
								   ,@UserId
								   ,CAST(@FPId AS SMALLINT)
								   ,@Month1
								   ,@RegisterDate
								   ,@RegisterDatePersian
								   ,@AppType
								   ,@DefaultLanguageID
								   ,@UserId
								   ,@RegisterDate
								   ,@RegisterDatePersian
								   ,@RegisterDate
								   ,@RegisterDate
								   ,@prjcnvsStatusGroupId
								   ,@prjOrderView/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/)
							/**/
							SET @ProjectId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
							/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
							INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang]
								([ProjectID]
								,[LanguageID]
								,[prjTitle])
							SELECT 
								 @ProjectId
								,L.LanguageID
								,@ProjectTitle			
							FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
							WHERE L.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
							AND ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1
						/**/
						END					
					END
				END	
			IF (@@ERROR > 0)
			BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Project' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
			ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN
			/*RZ990519- END*/

			/*========================================= Doc ======================================*/

			INSERT INTO WSDoc(
						[OprId],
						[DocNumber],
						[DocDate],
						[DocDateTime],
						[SenderId],
						[SenderName],
						[FactorNumber],
						[Description],
						[IsFix],
						[WHId],
						[OrderId],
						[BuyOrderId],
						[SendAddress],
						[DriverName],
						[DriverTel],
						[FreightName],
						[BijakNumber],
						[CarNumber],
						[WHIdI],
						[CompanyId],
						[CompanyPersonId],
						[ProjectId],
						[cmpTitle],
						[DocLinkAccCodeId],
						[DocLinkAccCode],
						[DocLinkAccDocId],
						[DocLinkAccDocNumber],
						[DocLinkAccVoucherId],
						[DocLinkAccVoucherNumber],
						[FirstRegisterUserId],
						[FirstRegisterDate],
						[FirstRegisterDatePersian],
						[FPId],
						[UserId],
						[RegisterDate],
						[RegisterDatePersian],
						[Month1],
						[AppType],
						[LanguageID])
				VALUES(
						@OprId,
						@DocNumber,
						@DocDate,
						@DocDateTime,
						@SenderId,
						@SenderName,
						@FactorNumber,
						@Description,
						@IsFix,
						@WHId,
						@OrderId,
						@BuyOrderId,
						@SendAddress,
						@DriverName,
						@DriverTel,
						@FreightName,
						@BijakNumber,
						@CarNumber,
						@WHIdI,
						@CompanyId,
						@CompanyPersonId,
						@ProjectId,
						@cmpTitle,
						@DocLinkAccCodeId,
						@DocLinkAccCode,
						@DocLinkAccDocId,
						@DocLinkAccDocNumber,
						@DocLinkAccVoucherId,
						@DocLinkAccVoucherNumber,
						@UserId,
						@RegisterDate,
						@RegisterDatePersian,
						@FPId,
						@UserId,
						@RegisterDate,
						@RegisterDatePersian,
						@Month1,
						@AppType,
						@DefaultLanguageID
				)

			SET @InsertedDocId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			SET @InsertedDocNumber = @DocNumber/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
			/*====================== Start Multi Language [en] ======================*/

			INSERT INTO WSDoc_Lang(
					[DocID],
					[LanguageID],
					[SenderName],
					[Description],
					[SendAddress],
					[DriverName],
					[FreightName],
					[cmpTitle])
			SELECT
					@InsertedDocId,
					LN.LanguageID,
					@SenderName,
					@Description,
					@SendAddress,
					@DriverName,
					@FreightName,
					@cmpTitle
			FROM Languages LN
			WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1

			/*======================= End Multi Language [en] =======================*/

		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
			BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert WSDoc' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN

		/*====================== Start Insert Doc Item ======================*/

		DECLARE
		@ItmId INT,
		@PrdCode NVARCHAR(50),
		@ItmDocId INT,
		@ItmPrdId INT,
		@ItmQuantity FLOAT = NULL,
		@ItmCost NUMERIC(18,0) = NULL,
		@ItmDescription NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
		@ItmIsFix SMALLINT = NULL,
		@ItmWHId INT = NULL,
		@ItmNotVisible BIT = NULL,           
		@ItmFCR FLOAT = NULL,
		@ItmFCRI FLOAT = NULL,
		@ItmTracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
		@DocItem NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL

		BEGIN TRAN
		DECLARE CrsrItem CURSOR FOR 
			SELECT 
			(STUFF((SELECT '|' + CAST(PJIN.[Name] AS NVARCHAR(max)) + '=' + CAST(PJIN.[StringValue] AS NVARCHAR(max))							
								FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@DocItemsJson) PJIN
								WHERE PJIN.[Parent_ID] =PJ.[SequenceNO]
								FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')) 
			FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@DocItemsJson) PJ
			WHERE PJ.[ValueType] = 'object'
		OPEN CrsrItem
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrItem INTO @DocItem
		/**/
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN		
		/**/
			DECLARE @InsertedDocItemId INT
			/**/
			SET @ItmId = NULL
			SET @PrdCode = NULL
			SET @ItmPrdId = NULL
			SET @ItmQuantity = NULL
			SET @ItmCost = NULL
			SET @ItmDescription = NULL
			SET @ItmIsFix = NULL
			SET @ItmWHId = NULL
			SET @ItmNotVisible = NULL
			SET @ItmFCR = NULL
			SET @ItmFCRI = NULL
			SET @ItmTracing = NULL

			/**/
			IF(@DocItem IS NOT NULL AND @DocItem <> '')
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @DocItem = @DocItem + '|'/*for last field*/
				DECLARE @ItmField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
				WHILE LEN(@DocItem) > 0
				BEGIN
					/**/
					IF(PATINDEX('%|%',@DocItem) > 0)
					BEGIN
						/**/
						SET @ItmField = SUBSTRING(@DocItem, 0, PATINDEX('%|%', @DocItem))
						/**/
						DECLARE @ItmFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
						DECLARE @ItmFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
						SET @ItmFieldName = SUBSTRING(@ItmField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@ItmField)-1)                                    
						SET @ItmFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@ItmField, CHARINDEX('=', @ItmField)+1, Len(@ItmField))
						
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'ItemId'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS INT) END									  
						END	
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'PrdId'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmPrdId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS INT) END									  
						END		
						/*--------------------------*/						
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'PrdCode'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @PrdCode = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'')  = '' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE @ItmFieldValue END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'Quantity'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmQuantity = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS FLOAT) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'Cost'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmCost = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,0)) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'Description'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmDescription = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'')  = '' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(4000)) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'IsFix'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmIsFix = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,0) = 0
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS BIT) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'WHId'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmWHId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,0)  = 0 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'NotVisible'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmNotVisible = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'')  = '' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS BIT) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'FCR'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmFCR = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS FLOAT) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'FCRI'
						BEGIN						
							SELECT @ItmFCRI = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'0')  = '0'
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS FLOAT) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'Tracing'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmTracing = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'')  = ''
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(200)) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						SET @DocItem = SUBSTRING(@DocItem,
                                  LEN(@ItmField + '|') + 1,
							      LEN(@DocItem))
						
						
						/**/
						END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @ItmField = @DocItem
						SET @DocItem = NULL						
					END
				END

				/*=========================== Start Item Checks ===========================*/
				/*========== Item Checking ==========*/		
				/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
				BEGIN/*No PrdId*/
					IF(@PrdCode IS NOT NULL AND @PrdCode <> '')
					BEGIN
						SET @ItmPrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProductID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE [PrdCode] = @PrdCode)
					END
					ELSE IF (ISNULL(@ItmPrdId, 0) > 0)
					BEGIN
						IF (SELECT P.ProductID FROM WSProducts P WHERE P.ProductID = @ItmPrdId) IS NULL
							SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrdId|'
					END
					
					IF (ISNULL(@ItmPrdId, 0) <= 0)
						SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrdId|'
				END
				/*RZ990519- End*/
				
				/*========== Item Checking ==========*/

				BEGIN/*No WHId*/
					IF (ISNULL(@ItmWHId, 0) > 0)
					BEGIN
						IF (SELECT P.WareHouseID FROM WsWareHouse P WHERE P.WareHouseID = @ItmWHId) IS NULL
							SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No WHId|'
					END
				END

				/*============================ End Item Checks ============================*/

				/*=========================== Start Insert Item ===========================*/

				IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')/*No Error*/
				BEGIN
					/**/			
					INSERT INTO WSDocItems(
						[DocId],
						[PrdId],
						[Quantity],
						[CostId],
						[Cost],
						[Description],
						[IsFix],
						[WHId],
						[NotVisible],
						[FCR],
						[FCRI],
						[DocILinkAccItemId],
						[Tracing],
						[FPId],
						[Month1],
						[UserId],
						[RegisterDate],
						[RegisterDatePersian],
						[AppType],
						[LanguageID],
						[FirstRegisterUserId],
						[FirstRegisterDate],
						[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
					)
					VALUES(
						@InsertedDocId,
						@ItmPrdId,
						@ItmQuantity,
						NULL,
						@ItmCost,
						@ItmDescription,
						@ItmIsFix,
						@ItmWHId,
						@ItmNotVisible,
						@ItmFCR,
						@ItmFCRI,
						NULL,
						@ItmTracing,
						@FPId,
						@Month1,
						@UserId,
						@RegisterDate,
						@RegisterDatePersian,
						@AppType,
						@DefaultLanguageID,
						@UserId,
						@RegisterDate,
						@RegisterDatePersian
					)
					SET @InsertedDocItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()					
					/*=================== Start Multi Language [en] ===================*/
					/*======================= Start Add Columns =======================*/			
			
					INSERT INTO WSDocItems_Lang(
						DocItemID,
						LanguageID,
						[Description]
					)				
					SELECT
						@InsertedDocItemId,
						LN.LanguageID,
						@ItmDescription
					FROM Languages LN
					WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID AND 
					ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1

					/*======================= End Add Columns =======================*/
					/*=================== End Multi Language [en] ===================*/			

					/*=================== Start Insert Doc Item Serial ===================*/

					IF (ISNULL(@InsertedDocItemId, 0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@DocItemSerialsJson,'') <> '')
					BEGIN

						/**/
						DECLARE
							@ItmSnID INT,
							@ItmSnSerial NVARCHAR(50),
							@ItmSnStatusId INT,
							@ItmSnDescription NVARCHAR(300),
							@ItmSnIsFix SMALLINT,
							@ItmSnWHId INT,
							@ItmSnSerialId INT,
							@ItmSnIsAutoBarcode BIT,
							@ItmSntblTmpBarcodeId INT,           
							@ItmSnExpDate DATETIME,
							@ItmSnExpDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
							@ItmSnInfoI NVARCHAR(200),
							@ItmSnInfoII NVARCHAR(200),
							@ItmSnInfoIII NVARCHAR(200),
							@ItmSnInfoIV NVARCHAR(200),
							@ItmSnInfoV NVARCHAR(200),
							@ItmSnInfoVI NVARCHAR(200),
							@DocItemSn NVARCHAR(MAX)

						/*BEGIN TRAN*/
						DECLARE CrsrItemSn CURSOR LOCAL FOR 
							SELECT 
							(STUFF((SELECT '|' + CAST(PJIN.[Name] AS NVARCHAR(max)) + '=' + CAST(PJIN.[StringValue] AS NVARCHAR(max))							
												FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@DocItemSerialsJson) PJIN
												WHERE PJIN.[Parent_ID] =PJ.[SequenceNO]
												FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')) 
							FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@DocItemSerialsJson) PJ
							WHERE PJ.[ValueType] = 'object'
						OPEN CrsrItemSn
						FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrItemSn INTO @DocItemSn
						/**/
						WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
						BEGIN
							/**/
							SET @ItmSnID = NULL
							SET @ItmSnSerial = NULL
							SET @ItmSnStatusId = NULL
							SET @ItmSnDescription = NULL
							SET @ItmSnIsFix = NULL
							SET @ItmSnWHId = NULL
							SET @ItmSnSerialId = NULL
							SET @ItmSnIsAutoBarcode = NULL
							SET @ItmSntblTmpBarcodeId = NULL
							SET @ItmSnExpDate = NULL
							SET @ItmSnExpDatePersian = NULL
							SET @ItmSnInfoI = NULL
							SET @ItmSnInfoII = NULL
							SET @ItmSnInfoIII = NULL
							SET @ItmSnInfoIV = NULL
							SET @ItmSnInfoV = NULL
							SET @ItmSnInfoVI = NULL

							/**/

							IF(@DocItemSn IS NOT NULL AND @DocItemSn <> '')
							BEGIN
								/**/
								SET @DocItemSn = @DocItemSn + '|'/*for last field*/
								DECLARE @ItmSnField NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
								WHILE LEN(@DocItemSn) > 0
								BEGIN																		
									IF(PATINDEX('%|%',@DocItemSn) > 0)
									BEGIN
										/*BEGIN TRAN*/
											SET @ItmSnField = SUBSTRING(@DocItemSn, 0, PATINDEX('%|%', @DocItemSn))
											/**/
											DECLARE @ItmSnFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
											DECLARE @ItmSnFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
											SET @ItmSnFieldName = SUBSTRING(@ItmSnField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@ItmSnField)-1)                                    
											SET @ItmSnFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@ItmSnField, CHARINDEX('=', @ItmSnField)+1, Len(@ItmSnField))
						
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'ItemId'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnID = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS INT) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'Serial'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnSerial = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(50)) END									  
											END		
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'StatusId'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnStatusId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS INT) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'Description'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnDescription = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(300)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'IsFix'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnIsFix = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = '' 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS SMALLINT) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'WHId'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnWHId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,0) = 0
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS INT) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'IsAutoBarcode'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnIsAutoBarcode = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,0)  = 0 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS BIT) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'tblTmpBarcodeId'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSntblTmpBarcodeId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,0)  = 0 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS INT) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'SerialId'/*NKR14020921*/
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnSerialId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,0)  = 0 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS INT) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'ExpDate'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnExpDate = CASE WHEN @ItmSnFieldValue IS NULL
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS DATETIME) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'ExpDatePersian'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnExpDatePersian = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = ''
															THEN NULL
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(8)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'InfoI'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnInfoI = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = ''
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(200)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'InfoII'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnInfoII = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = ''
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(200)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'InfoIII'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnInfoIII = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = ''
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(200)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'InfoIV'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnInfoIV = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = ''
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(200)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'InfoV'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnInfoV = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = ''
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(200)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'InfoVI'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnInfoVI = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'')  = ''
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(200)) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											SET @DocItemSn = SUBSTRING(@DocItemSn,
														LEN(@ItmSnField + '|') + 1,
														LEN(@DocItemSn))
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/											
										/**/
									END
									ELSE
									BEGIN
										SET @ItmSnField = @DocItemSn
										SET @DocItemSn = NULL						
									END
								END									
								/*=========================================================*/
								BEGIN TRAN	
									/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
									DECLARE @OprType INT,@DocISerialIdFound INT
									SET @OprType = (SELECT OprType FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE DocOprID = @OprId)
									/*Check the serial Id */											
									/*RZ990519- End*/
									/*select @ItmSnID,@ItmId*/
									/*IF ((@ItmSnID = @ItmId) AND ((SELECT DIS.DocItemSerialID FROM WSDocItemSerials DIS WHERE DIS.ItemId = @InsertedDocItemId AND DIS.Serial = @ItmSnSerial) IS NULL) AND (ISNULL(@ItmSnSerial, '') <> ''))*/
									IF ((@ItmSnID = @ItmId))
									BEGIN
										/**/
												
										DECLARE @InsertedItemSerialId INT

										INSERT INTO WSDocItemSerials(
													[ItemId],
													[Serial],
													[StatusId],
													[Description],
													[IsFix],
													[WHId],
													[SerialId],
													[IsAutoBarcode],
													[tblTmpBarcodeId],
													[ExpDate],
													[ExpDatePersian],
													[InfoI],
													[InfoII],
													[InfoIII],
													[InfoIV],
													[InfoV],
													[InfoVI],
													[DocISLinkAccItemSerialId],
													[FPId],
													[FirstRegisterUserId],
													[FirstRegisterDatePersian],
													[FirstRegisterDate],
													[UserId],
													[Month1],
													[RegisterDate],
													[RegisterDatePersian],
													[AppType],
													[LanguageID])
											VALUES (
													@InsertedDocItemId,
													@ItmSnSerial,
													@ItmSnStatusId,
													@ItmSnDescription,
													@ItmSnIsFix,
													@ItmSnWHId,
													@ItmSnSerialId,
													@ItmSnIsAutoBarcode,
													@ItmSntblTmpBarcodeId,
													@ItmSnExpDate,
													@ItmSnExpDatePersian,
													@ItmSnInfoI,
													@ItmSnInfoII,
													@ItmSnInfoIII,
													@ItmSnInfoIV,
													@ItmSnInfoV,
													@ItmSnInfoVI,
													NULL,
													@FPId,
													@UserId,
													@RegisterDatePersian,
													@RegisterDate,
													@UserId,
													@Month1,
													@RegisterDate,
													@RegisterDatePersian,
													@AppType,
													@DefaultLanguageID
											)

										SET @InsertedItemSerialId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
										
										/*=================== Start Multi Language [en] ===================*/
										/*======================= Start Add Columns =======================*/			
						
										INSERT INTO WSDocItemSerials_Lang(
											[DocItemSerialID],
											[LanguageID],
											[Description],
											[InfoI],
											[InfoII],
											[InfoIII],
											[InfoIV],
											[InfoV],
											[InfoVI]
											)
										SELECT 
											@InsertedItemSerialId,
											LN.LanguageID,
											@Description,
											@ItmSnInfoI,
											@ItmSnInfoII,
											@ItmSnInfoIII,
											@ItmSnInfoIV,
											@ItmSnInfoV,
											@ItmSnInfoVI
										FROM Languages LN
										WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID 
										AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive, 0) = 1
																
										/*======================= End Add Columns =======================*/
										/*=================== End Multi Language [en] ===================*/
			
									END
									/**/
								IF (@@ERROR > 0)
									BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert DocItemSerials' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
								ELSE 
									COMMIT TRAN 
																										
							/**/
							END
						/**/
						FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrItemSn INTO  @DocItemSn
						END
						CLOSE CrsrItemSn
						DEALLOCATE CrsrItemSn
						
						/**/
					END

					/*=================== End Insert Doc Item Serial ===================*/
				END
				/**/	
					
				/*============================ End Insert Item ============================*/
							
			/**/
			END
		/**/
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrItem INTO  @DocItem
		END
		CLOSE CrsrItem
		DEALLOCATE CrsrItem
		/**/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
			BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert DocItems' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN 	

		/*======================= End Insert Doc Item ========================*/
		/**/
	END /* END = IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '') */
	/*==============================*/
	IF((ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'' ) <> ''))
	BEGIN
	/**/
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 		SELECT 1
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF((ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'' ) = ''))
	BEGIN
	/**/
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SELECT 1
	/**/
	END
	
	/*===========================*/
	IF (@ErrorNumber > 0 OR (ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'' ) <> ''))
	BEGIN 
		RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 		SELECT 0
	END 
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SELECT 1
	END	
	/**/
END








-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[ApispNeedUpdateUsersMobBaseInfo]
-- ==================================================






/*RZ14010714*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispNeedUpdateUsersMobBaseInfo]			
(
/* 
	exec ApispNeedUpdateUsersMobBaseInfo 
	@UpdateDetail = N'CrmCompanyJustAccessCity',
	@UserId = 3,
	@RollId = NULL
*/	
@UpdateDetail NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@UserId INT = NULL,
@RollId INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/		
	IF(@UpdateDetail IS NULL OR @UpdateDetail = '')
		SET @UpdateDetail = ISNULL(@UpdateDetail,'') 
	/**/
	IF(@UserId IS NOT NULL AND @UserId <> 0)
	BEGIN
		UPDATE [dbo].[Users]
		SET 
			[MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdate] = 1
			,[MobAppBaseInfoLastUpdate] = GETDATE()
			,[MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail] = (SELECT (
												  CASE 
													WHEN 
														((SELECT TOP 1 splitdata
														FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail],',')
														WHERE splitdata = @UpdateDetail) IS NULL)						
													 THEN ISNULL([MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail],'') + @UpdateDetail + ','  
													 ELSE [MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail]  
												  END  
											  ))
		WHERE [UserId] = @UserId

	END
	ELSE IF (@RollId IS NOT NULL AND @RollId <> 0)
	BEGIN
		UPDATE [dbo].[Users]
		SET 
			[MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdate] = 1
			,[MobAppBaseInfoLastUpdate] = GETDATE() 
			,[MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail] = (SELECT (
												  CASE 
													WHEN 
														((SELECT TOP 1 splitdata
														FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail],',')
														WHERE splitdata = @UpdateDetail) IS NULL)						
													 THEN ISNULL([MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail],'') + @UpdateDetail + ','  
													 ELSE [MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail]  
												  END  
											  ))
		WHERE [UserId] IN (SELECT [UserId] FROM [dbo].[UsersPermission] WHERE [RollId] = @RollId)

	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		UPDATE [dbo].[Users]
		SET 
			[MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdate] = 1
			,[MobAppBaseInfoLastUpdate] = GETDATE() 
			,[MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail] = (SELECT (
												  CASE 
													WHEN 
														((SELECT TOP 1 splitdata
														FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString]([MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail],',')
														WHERE splitdata = @UpdateDetail) IS NULL)						
													 THEN ISNULL([MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail],'') + @UpdateDetail + ','  
													 ELSE [MobAppBaseInfoNeedUpdateDetail]  
												  END  
											  ))
	END
/**/
END










-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[ApispReturnCompanyProjectOrderView]
-- ==================================================





/*RZ14021004*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispReturnCompanyProjectOrderView]
(
/*
	declare @prjOrderView INT = 0
	exec ApispReturnCompanyProjectOrderView 
	@CompanyId  = 6156,
	@prjOrderView = @prjOrderView  OUTPUT
	select @prjOrderView
*/
@CompanyId INT,
@prjOrderView INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/	
	DECLARE @ItemCount INT=NULL
	SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] WHERE prjCompanyId = @CompanyId)
	SET @prjOrderView = (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) + 1) + (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) * 9 ) - 1
/**/
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispUpdateBuyOrder]
(

	@ApiBuyOrderOprId INT = NULL,

	@ApiBordNumber BIGINT = NULL,

	@ApiBordDate NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ApiBordOrderDate NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCompanyId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordCompanyPersonId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordProjectId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordSubject NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCalculateTax BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIsVerified BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordStatusId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIsFixed BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordPrintFormatId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordImportDate NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ApiBordVerifierId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordVerifiedRegisterDate NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ApiBordSettlementTypeId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordTime NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ApiBordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordReferUserId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordRound NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordDiscountTitle NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordNote NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordcmpTitle NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordFacilityCmpId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordFacilityPrsId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordFacilityPrjId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordReqCmpId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordReqPrsId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordReqPrjId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordDeliveryTerm NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordDeliveryDate NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ApiBordTaxCleared BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordTaxClearedDate NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ApiBordFacilityCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordFacilityCostPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordSupplierDeliveryDate NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ApiBordForwarderName NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordFreightCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordFreightCurrUnId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordFreightCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordFinalFreightCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCustomsPart NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCustomsFee NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCustomsCurrUnId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordCustomsCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordFinalCustomsFee NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordFinalDeliveryDate NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ApiBordTotalFinalCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCommission NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCommissionCurrUnId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordCommissionCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordFinalCommission NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordCostSheetId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordNumRef NVARCHAR(150) = NULL,
	@ApiBordDefaultSettingId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordVerificationWorkFlowId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordLinkAccFirstTransferDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordLinkAccLastTransferDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordCurrencyUnitIdI INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordCurrencyUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,
	/**/
	@UserId INT = NULL,
	@Month1 SMALLINT = NULL,
	@RegisterDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@AppType VARCHAR(20) = NULL,
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT  = NULL,
	/**/
	@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	
	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
	DECLARE @LanguageCollation NVARCHAR(50) = (SELECT L.LanguageCollation FROM Languages L WHERE L.LanguageID = @LanguageID),
			@ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50),
			@ErrorNumber INT

	BEGIN TRY
		/**/
		begin/* Check Paramters */
		/**/
	
			DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
			SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 bordIsFixed FROM dbo.WSBuyOrder WHERE BuyOrderID = @ApiBuyOrderID)
			IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed, 0) = 1)
			BEGIN
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'IsFixed|'
			END

			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordDate, '') = '')
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Emp BordDateTime|'

			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBuyOrderOprId, 0) > 0)
				IF ((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSBuyOrderOpr O WHERE O.BuyOrderOprID = @ApiBuyOrderOprId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BuyOrderOprId|'

			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordCompanyId, 0) = 0)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Emp BordCompanyId|'
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordCompanyId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @ApiBordCompanyId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordCompanyId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordCompanyPersonId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM CrmCompanyPersons P WHERE P.CompanyPersonID = @ApiBordCompanyPersonId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordCompanyPersonId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordProjectId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM CrmProjects P WHERE P.ProjectID = @ApiBordProjectId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordProjectId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordStatusId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSBuyOrderStatus S WHERE S.BuyOrderStatusId = @ApiBordStatusId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordStatusId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordPrintFormatId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM PrintFormat P WHERE P.PrintFormatID = @ApiBordPrintFormatId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordPrintFormatId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordVerifierId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM Users U WHERE U.UserId = @ApiBordVerifierId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordVerifierId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordReferUserId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM Users U WHERE U.UserId = @ApiBordReferUserId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordReferUserId|'
				
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordCurrencyUnitId, 0) = 0)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Emp BordCurrencyUnitId|'
			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordCurrencyUnitId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSCurrencyUnits C WHERE C.CurrencyUnitID = @ApiBordCurrencyUnitId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordCurrencyUnitId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordFacilityCmpId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @ApiBordFacilityCmpId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordFacilityCmpId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordFacilityPrsId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM CrmCompanyPersons P WHERE P.CompanyPersonID = @ApiBordFacilityPrsId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordFacilityPrsId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordFacilityPrjId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM CrmProjects P WHERE P.ProjectID = @ApiBordFacilityPrjId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordFacilityPrjId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordReqCmpId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @ApiBordReqCmpId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordReqCmpId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordReqPrsId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM CrmCompanyPersons P WHERE P.CompanyPersonID = @ApiBordReqPrsId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordReqPrsId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordReqPrjId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM CrmProjects P WHERE P.ProjectID = @ApiBordReqPrjId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordReqPrjId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordFreightCurrUnId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSCurrencyUnits C WHERE C.CurrencyUnitID = @ApiBordFreightCurrUnId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordFreightCurrUnId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordCustomsCurrUnId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSCurrencyUnits C WHERE C.CurrencyUnitID = @ApiBordCustomsCurrUnId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordCustomsCurrUnId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordCommissionCurrUnId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSCurrencyUnits C WHERE C.CurrencyUnitID = @ApiBordCommissionCurrUnId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordCommissionCurrUnId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordCostSheetId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSCostSheets CS WHERE CS.CostSheetId = @ApiBordCostSheetId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordCostSheetId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordDefaultSettingId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSBuyOrderDefaultSetting D WHERE D.BuyOrderDefaultSettingId = @ApiBordDefaultSettingId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordDefaultSettingId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordVerificationWorkFlowId, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSVerificationWorkFlows W WHERE W.VerificationWorkFlowId = @ApiBordVerificationWorkFlowId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordVerificationWorkFlowId|'

			IF(ISNULL(@ApiBordCurrencyUnitIdI, 0) > 0)
				IF((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSCurrencyUnits C WHERE C.CurrencyUnitID = @ApiBordCurrencyUnitIdI) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordCurrencyUnitIdI|'

		/**/
		end/* !Check Paramters */

		IF (ISNULL(@ErrorMessage, '') = '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			BEGIN TRAN

			DECLARE @QRY NVARCHAR(MAX) = 'UPDATE WSBuyOrder SET ', 
					@Columns NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', 
					@Conditions NVARCHAR(MAX) = '',
					/**/
					@NumberIsNULL INT = -9999, 
					@FloatIsNULL FLOAT = -9999, 
					@NumericIsNULL NUMERIC(18,2) = -9999, 
					@StringISNull NVARCHAR(50) = N'NULL'
		
		
			DECLARE @Lang TABLE(
				[Value] NVARCHAR(MAX),
				[LangId] INT,
				[FieldName] VARCHAR(500)
			)
			begin /* Insert @Lang */
			/**/

				DELETE @Lang
				IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordTitle, '') <> '')
				BEGIN
					/**/
					INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
					SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordTitle' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordTitle)
					WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(100) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
					WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
					/**/
				END
				IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordSubject, '') <> '')
				BEGIN
					/**/
					INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
					SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordSubject' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordSubject)
					WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(100) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
					WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
					/**/
				END
				IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordDiscountTitle, '') <> '')
				BEGIN
					/**/
					INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
					SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordDiscountTitle' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordDiscountTitle)
					WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(100) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
					WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
					/**/
				END
				IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordNote, '') <> '')
				BEGIN
					/**/
					INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
					SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordNote' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordNote)
					WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(300) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
					WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
					/**/
				END
				IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordcmpTitle, '') <> '')
				BEGIN
					/**/
					INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
					SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordcmpTitle' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordcmpTitle)
					WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(100) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
					WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
					/**/
				END
				IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordDeliveryTerm, '') <> '')

					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordCustomsCurrencyRate] = NULL , '
				END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordFinalCustomsFee IS NOT NULL)
				IF (@ApiBordFinalCustomsFee <> @NumericIsNULL)
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordFinalCustomsFee] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordFinalCustomsFee AS VARCHAR(54)) + ' , '
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordFinalCustomsFee] = NULL , '
				END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordFinalDeliveryDate IS NOT NULL)
				IF (UPPER(TRIM(@ApiBordFinalDeliveryDate)) <> @StringISNull)
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordFinalDeliveryDate] = CAST(''' + @ApiBordFinalDeliveryDate + ''' AS DATETIME) , '
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordFinalDeliveryDatePersian] = FORMAT(CAST(''' + @ApiBordFinalDeliveryDate + ''' AS DATETIME), ''yyyyMMdd'', ''' + @LanguageCollation + ''') , '
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordFinalDeliveryDate] = NULL , '
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordFinalDeliveryDatePersian] = NULL , '
				END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordTotalFinalCost IS NOT NULL)
				IF (@ApiBordTotalFinalCost <> @NumericIsNULL)
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordTotalFinalCost] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordTotalFinalCost AS VARCHAR(54)) + ' , '
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordTotalFinalCost] = NULL , '
				END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordCommission IS NOT NULL)
				IF (@ApiBordCommission <> @NumericIsNULL)
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordCommission] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordCommission AS VARCHAR(54)) + ' , '
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordCommission] = NULL , '
				END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordCommissionCurrUnId IS NOT NULL)
				IF (@ApiBordCommissionCurrUnId <> @NumberIsNULL)
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordCommissionCurrUnId] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordCommissionCurrUnId AS VARCHAR(27)) + ' , '
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordCommissionCurrUnId] = NULL , '
				END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordCommissionCurrencyRate IS NOT NULL)
				IF (@ApiBordCommissionCurrencyRate <> @FloatIsNULL)
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordCommissionCurrencyRate] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordCommissionCurrencyRate AS VARCHAR(38)) + ' , '
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordCommissionCurrencyRate] = NULL , '
				END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordFinalCommission IS NOT NULL)
				IF (@ApiBordFinalCommission <> @NumericIsNULL)
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordFinalCommission] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordFinalCommission AS VARCHAR(54)) + ' , '
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordFinalCommission] = NULL , '
				END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordCostSheetId IS NOT NULL)
				IF (@ApiBordCostSheetId <> @NumberIsNULL)
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordCostSheetId] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordCostSheetId AS VARCHAR(27)) + ' , '
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordCostSheetId] = NULL , '
				END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordNumRef IS NOT NULL)
				IF (UPPER(TRIM(@ApiBordNumRef)) <> @StringISNull)
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordNumRef] = N''' + @ApiBordNumRef + ''' , '
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordNumRef] = NULL , '
				END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordDefaultSettingId IS NOT NULL)
				IF (@ApiBordDefaultSettingId <> @NumberIsNULL)
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordDefaultSettingId] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordDefaultSettingId AS VARCHAR(27)) + ' , '
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordDefaultSettingId] = NULL , '
				END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordVerificationWorkFlowId IS NOT NULL)
				IF (@ApiBordVerificationWorkFlowId <> @NumberIsNULL)
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordVerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordVerificationWorkFlowId AS VARCHAR(27)) + ' , '
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordVerificationWorkFlowId] = NULL , '
				END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordLinkAccFirstTransferDate IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordLinkAccFirstTransferDate] = N''' + CAST(@ApiBordLinkAccFirstTransferDate AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ''' , '
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordLinkAccFirstTransferDate] = NULL , '
			END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordLinkAccLastTransferDate IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordLinkAccLastTransferDate] = N''' + CAST(@ApiBordLinkAccLastTransferDate AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ''' , '
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordLinkAccLastTransferDate] = NULL , '
			END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordCurrencyUnitIdI IS NOT NULL)
				IF (@ApiBordCurrencyUnitIdI <> @NumberIsNULL)
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordCurrencyUnitIdI] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordCurrencyUnitIdI AS VARCHAR(27)) + ' , '
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordCurrencyUnitIdI] = NULL , '
				END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@ApiBordCurrencyUnitIRate IS NOT NULL)
				IF (@ApiBordCurrencyUnitIRate <> @FloatIsNULL)
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordCurrencyUnitIRate] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordCurrencyUnitIRate AS VARCHAR(38)) + ' , '
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordCurrencyUnitIRate] = NULL , '
				END
			
			/**/
			end/* !Add Columns */

			IF (ISNULL(@Columns, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @Columns = @Columns + ' BordFirstRegisterUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' , '
				SET @Columns = @Columns + ' UserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' , '
				SET @Columns = @Columns + ' RegisterDate = ''' + CAST(@RegisterDate AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ''' , '
				SET @Columns = @Columns + ' RegisterDatePersian = ''' + @RegisterDatePersian + ''' , '
				SET @Columns = @Columns + ' AppType = ''' + @AppType + ''' '

				SET @Conditions = ' WHERE [BuyOrderID] = ' + CAST(@ApiBuyOrderID AS NVARCHAR(50))
			

				EXECUTE (@QRY + @Columns + @Conditions)


				/* Multi-Lang*/

				IF ((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM @Lang) > 0)
				BEGIN
					/**/
					UPDATE WSBuyOrder_Lang
					   SET bordTitle = (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordTitle'),
						   bordSubject = (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordSubject'),
						   bordDiscountTitle = (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordDiscountTitle'),
						   bordNote = (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordNote'),
						   bordcmpTitle = (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordcmpTitle'),
						   bordDeliveryTerm = (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordDeliveryTerm'),
						   bordForwarderName = (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordForwarderName'),
						   bordCustomsPart = (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordCustomsPart')
					WHERE BuyOrderID = @ApiBuyOrderID
					  AND LanguageID IN (SELECT DISTINCT L.[LangId] FROM @Lang L)


					INSERT INTO WSBuyOrder_Lang (
						[BuyOrderID], 
						[LanguageID], 
						[bordTitle], 
						[bordSubject], 
						[bordDiscountTitle], 
						[bordNote], 
						[bordcmpTitle], 
						[bordDeliveryTerm], 
						[bordForwarderName], 
						[bordCustomsPart]
					)
					SELECT @ApiBuyOrderID,
						   L.LanguageID,
						   (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordTitle'),
						   (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordSubject'),
						   (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordDiscountTitle'),
						   (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordNote'),
						   (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordcmpTitle'),
						   (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordDeliveryTerm'),
						   (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordForwarderName'),
						   (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordCustomsPart')
					  FROM Languages L
					 WHERE L.LanguageID NOT IN (SELECT BOL.LanguageID FROM WSBuyOrder_Lang BOL)
					   AND L.LanguageID IN (SELECT DISTINCT LL.[LangId] FROM @Lang LL)
					   AND ISNULL(L.IsActive, 0) = 1
					/**/
				END

				/* !Multi-Lang*/

					
				IF (@@ERROR > 0)
				BEGIN
					/**/
					BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Update Order'
					SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
					/**/
				END
				ELSE 
				BEGIN
					/**/
					COMMIT TRAN
					/**/
				END

				/*=========================== Calculate Order ====================*/
				EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmounts] @ApiBuyOrderID, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID		

				/**/
			END
		END
	
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SELECT 1
		/**/
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		/**/
		 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
		 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error|'
		 IF(@ErrorFrom IS NOT NULL AND @ErrorFrom <> '')
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + @ErrorFrom
 		 SELECT 0
		/**/
	END CATCH
	/**/
END

/*NKR14030307*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispUpdateBuyOrderItems]
(
	@ApiBuyOrderItemId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBuyOrderId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordICost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordITotalCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIQuantityDesc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordICurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIOrderView INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICalculateTax BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordINotVisible BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordINotInSum BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIWHId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdCostGroupID INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICostSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordITaxCleared BIT = NULL,
	@ApiBordITaxClearedDate NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFacilityCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFacilityCostPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIForwarderName NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFreightCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFreightCurrUnId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFreightCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFinalFreightCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICustomsPart NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICustomsFee NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICustomsCurrUnId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordICustomsCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFinalCustomsFee NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordITotalFinalCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICommission NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICommissionCurrUnId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordICommissionCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIFinalCommission NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ApiBordICostSheetItemId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdUnitIdI INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdColorId INT = NULL,
	@ApiBordIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ApiBordILinkAccFirstTransferDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@ApiBordILinkAccLastTransferDate DATETIME = NULL,
	/**/
	@UserId INT = NULL,
	@Month1 SMALLINT = NULL,
	@RegisterDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@AppType VARCHAR(20) = NULL,
	/**/
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT  = NULL,
	/**/
	@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
	DECLARE @LanguageCollation NVARCHAR(50) = (SELECT L.LanguageCollation FROM Languages L WHERE L.LanguageID = @LanguageID),
			@ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50),
			@ErrorNumber INT


	BEGIN TRY
		/**/
		
		begin/* Check Paramters */
		/**/
	
			DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
			SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 bordIsFixed FROM dbo.WSBuyOrder WHERE BuyOrderID = @ApiBuyOrderID)
			IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed, 0) = 1)
			BEGIN
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'IsFixed|'
			END

			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIPrdId, 0) <> 0)
				IF ((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSProducts P WHERE P.ProductID = @ApiBordIPrdId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordIPrdId|'
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIPrdUnitId, 0) <> 0)
				IF ((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSProductUnits U WHERE U.ProductUnitID = @ApiBordIPrdUnitId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordIPrdUnitId|'
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordICurrencyUnitId, 0) <> 0)
				IF ((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSCurrencyUnits U WHERE U.CurrencyUnitID = @ApiBordICurrencyUnitId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordICurrencyUnitId|'
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIWHId, 0) <> 0)
				IF ((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WsWareHouse U WHERE U.WareHouseID = @ApiBordIWHId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordIWHId|'
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIFreightCurrUnId, 0) <> 0)
				IF ((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSCurrencyUnits U WHERE U.CurrencyUnitID = @ApiBordIFreightCurrUnId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordIFreightCurrUnId|'
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordICustomsCurrUnId, 0) <> 0)
				IF ((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSCurrencyUnits U WHERE U.CurrencyUnitID = @ApiBordICustomsCurrUnId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordICustomsCurrUnId|'
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordICommissionCurrUnId, 0) <> 0)
				IF ((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSCurrencyUnits U WHERE U.CurrencyUnitID = @ApiBordICommissionCurrUnId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordICommissionCurrUnId|'
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordICostSheetItemId, 0) <> 0)
				IF ((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSCostSheetItems U WHERE U.CostSheetItemId = @ApiBordICostSheetItemId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordICostSheetItemId|'
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIPrdUnitIdI, 0) <> 0)
				IF ((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSProductUnits U WHERE U.ProductUnitID = @ApiBordIPrdUnitIdI) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordIPrdUnitIdI|'
			IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIPrdColorId, 0) <> 0)
				IF ((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM WSProductColor U WHERE U.ProductColorID = @ApiBordIPrdColorId) <> 1)
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BordIPrdColorId|'

		/**/
		end/* !Check Paramters */


		IF (ISNULL(@ErrorMessage, '') = '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			BEGIN TRAN

			DECLARE @QRY NVARCHAR(MAX) = 'UPDATE WSBuyOrderItems SET ', 
					@Columns NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', 
					@Conditions NVARCHAR(MAX) = '',
					/**/
					@NumberIsNULL INT = -9999, 
					@FloatIsNULL FLOAT = -9999, 
					@NumericIsNULL NUMERIC(18,2) = -9999, 
					@StringISNull NVARCHAR(50) = N'NULL'


			DECLARE @Lang TABLE(
				[Value] NVARCHAR(MAX),
				[LangId] INT,
				[FieldName] VARCHAR(500)
			)
			begin/* Insert Into @Lang */
			/**/
				DELETE @Lang
				IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIDescription, '') <> '')
				BEGIN
					/**/
					INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
					SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordIDescription' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordIDescription)
					WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
					WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
					/**/
				END
				IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIQuantityDesc, '') <> '')
				BEGIN
					/**/
					INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
					SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordIQuantityDesc' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordIQuantityDesc)
					WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(1000) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
					WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
					/**/
				END
				IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIForwarderName, '') <> '')
				BEGIN
					/**/
					INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
					SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordIForwarderName' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordIForwarderName)
					WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(1000) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
					WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
					/**/
				END
				IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordICustomsPart, '') <> '')
				BEGIN
					/**/
					INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
					SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordICustomsPart' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordICustomsPart)
					WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(1000) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
					WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
					/**/
				END
				IF (ISNULL(@ApiBordIPrdNickname, '') <> '')
				BEGIN
					/**/
					INSERT INTO @Lang([Value], [LangId], [FieldName])
					SELECT [Value], LanguageId, 'BordIPrdNickname' FROM OPENJSON(@ApiBordIPrdNickname)
					WITH([Value] NVARCHAR(1000) '$.Value', LanguageId INT '$.LanguageId')
					WHERE ISNULL([Value], '') <> '' AND LOWER([Value]) <> 'null'
					/**/
				END
			/**/
			end/* !Insert Into @Lang */

			begin/* Add Columns */
			/**/

				IF (@ApiBuyOrderId IS NOT NULL)
					IF (@ApiBuyOrderId <> @NumberIsNULL)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BuyOrderId] = ' + CAST(@ApiBuyOrderId AS NVARCHAR(33)) + ' , '
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BuyOrderId] = NULL , '
					END

				/* Add Columns */

				IF (@ApiBordIPrdId IS NOT NULL)
					IF (@ApiBordIPrdId <> @NumberIsNULL)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordIPrdId] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(33)) + ' , '
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordIPrdId] = NULL , '
					END

				/* Add Columns */

				IF (@ApiBordIQuantity IS NOT NULL)
					IF (@ApiBordIQuantity <> @FloatIsNULL)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordIQuantity] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordIQuantity AS NVARCHAR(27)) + ' , '
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordIQuantity] = NULL , '
					END

				/* Add Columns */

				IF (@ApiBordICost IS NOT NULL)
					IF (@ApiBordICost <> @NumericIsNULL)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordICost] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordICost AS NVARCHAR(48)) + ' , '
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordICost] = NULL , '
					END

				/* Add Columns */

				IF (@ApiBordITotalCost IS NOT NULL)
					IF (@ApiBordITotalCost <> @NumericIsNULL)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordITotalCost] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordITotalCost AS NVARCHAR(48)) + ' , '
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordITotalCost] = NULL , '
					END

				/* Add Columns */

				IF (@ApiBordIDescription IS NOT NULL)
					IF (@ApiBordIDescription <> @StringISNull)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordIDescription] = ''' + (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND L.[FieldName] = 'BordIDescription') + ''' , '
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordIDescription] = NULL , '
					END

				/* Add Columns */

				IF (@ApiBordIQuantityDesc IS NOT NULL)
					IF (@ApiBordIQuantityDesc <> @StringISNull)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordIQuantityDesc] = ''' + (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = @DefaultLanguageID AND L.[FieldName] = 'BordIQuantityDesc') + ''' , '
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordIQuantityDesc] = NULL , '
					END

				/* Add Columns */

				IF (@ApiBordIDiscount IS NOT NULL)
					IF (@ApiBordIDiscount <> @NumericIsNULL)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordIDiscount] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordIDiscount AS NVARCHAR(48)) + ' , '
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordIDiscount] = NULL , '
					END

				/* Add Columns */

				IF (@ApiBordIDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL)
					IF (@ApiBordIDiscountPercent <> @NumericIsNULL)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordIDiscountPercent] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordIDiscountPercent AS NVARCHAR(48)) + ' , '
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordIDiscountPercent] = NULL , '
					END

				/* Add Columns */

				IF (@ApiBordIPrdUnitId IS NOT NULL)
					IF (@ApiBordIPrdUnitId <> @NumberIsNULL)
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + N' [BordIPrdUnitId] = ' + CAST(@ApiBordIPrdUnitId AS NVARCHAR(33)) + ' , '
					END

				SET @Conditions = ' WHERE BuyOrderItemId = ' + CAST(@ApiBuyOrderItemId AS NVARCHAR(50))
				
				EXECUTE (@QRY + @Columns + @Conditions)

				IF ((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM @Lang) > 0)
				BEGIN
					/**/
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems_Lang]
						SET [bordIDescription] = (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordIDescription'),
							[bordIQuantityDesc] = (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordIQuantityDesc'),
							[bordIForwarderName] = (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordIForwarderName'),
							[bordICustomsPart] = (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordICustomsPart'),
							[bordIPrdNickname] = (SELECT L.[Value] FROM @Lang L WHERE L.[LangId] = LanguageID AND L.FieldName = 'BordIPrdNickname')
					 WHERE BuyOrderItemId = @ApiBuyOrderItemId
					   AND LanguageID IN (SELECT DISTINCT L.[LangId] FROM @Lang L)


					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems_Lang] (
						[BuyOrderItemId], 
						[LanguageID], 
						[bordIDescription], 
						[bordIQuantityDesc], 
						[bordIForwarderName], 
						[bordICustomsPart], 
						[bordIPrdNickname]
					)
					SELECT @ApiBuyOrderItemId,
						   L.LanguageID,
						   (SELECT LL.[Value] FROM @Lang LL WHERE LL.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LL.FieldName = 'BordIDescription'),
						   (SELECT LL.[Value] FROM @Lang LL WHERE LL.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LL.FieldName = 'BordIQuantityDesc'),
						   (SELECT LL.[Value] FROM @Lang LL WHERE LL.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LL.FieldName = 'BordIForwarderName'),
						   (SELECT LL.[Value] FROM @Lang LL WHERE LL.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LL.FieldName = 'BordICustomsPart'),
						   (SELECT LL.[Value] FROM @Lang LL WHERE LL.[LangId] = L.LanguageID AND LL.FieldName = 'BordIPrdNickname')
					  FROM Languages L 
					 WHERE L.LanguageID IN (SELECT DISTINCT LL.[LangId] FROM @Lang LL)
					   AND L.LanguageID NOT IN (SELECT LL.LanguageID FROM [WSBuyOrderItems_Lang] LL)
					/**/
				END

				/* !Multi-Lang */

				
					
				IF (@@ERROR > 0)
				BEGIN
					/**/
					BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Update Order'
					SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
					/**/
				END
				ELSE 
				BEGIN
					/**/
					COMMIT TRAN
					/**/
				END


				/*=========================== Calculate Order ====================*/
				EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmounts] @ApiBuyOrderID, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID		


				/**/
			END

			/**/
		END

			
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SELECT 1
	/**/
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		/**/
		 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
		 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error|'
		 IF(@ErrorFrom IS NOT NULL AND @ErrorFrom <> '')
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + @ErrorFrom
 		 SELECT 0
		/**/
	END CATCH
	/**/
END

	@PrdPriceID INT,
	@PrdId INT,	
	@DocNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@Price NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@CurrencyPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@CurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
	@CooperationPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@PricePercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@DiscountPrecent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@Discount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@OtherPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@Description NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,	
	@FPId INT,	
	@WHId INT = NULL,
	@ProductPriceGroupId INT = NULL,

	@DocDate NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,

	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@AppType VARCHAR(20),
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,

)
AS
BEGIN

	DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
	/********************************************************************************/
	/************************************ FPId **************************************/
	SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)/*RZ990514- Modify this sp*/
	/********************************************************************************/

	/*================ Start Checks ================*/
	/*RZ990514- Modify this sp*/
	BEGIN/*Not Group*/
		IF((@ProductPriceGroupId IS NOT NULL AND @ProductPriceGroupId > 0))
		BEGIN			 
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProductPriceGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductPriceGroup] WHERE [ProductPriceGroupID] = @ProductPriceGroupId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Not Group|'
		END
	END

	/*PrdID*/
	IF(@PrdId IS NOT NULL AND @PrdId > 0)
	BEGIN	
		IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProductID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE [ProductID] = @PrdId) IS NULL)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Not Prd|'
	END

	/*CurrencyUnitId*/
	IF(@CurrencyUnitId IS NOT NULL AND @CurrencyUnitId > 0)
	BEGIN	
		IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID] FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @CurrencyUnitId) IS NULL)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Not CurrencyUnitId|'
	END	
	/*RZ990514- End*/
	

	/*================= End Checks =================*/
	
	BEGIN TRAN		
		IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')
		BEGIN

			DECLARE @QRY NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @Columns NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @Conditions NVARCHAR(MAX) = '',
			@NumberIsNULL INT = -9999, @NumericIsNULL NUMERIC(18,2) = -9999, @StringISNill NVARCHAR(50) = N'NULL'

			SET @QRY = ' UPDATE  WSProductPrice

				END

				BEGIN

				END
				ELSE

					SET @Columns = @Columns + ' OtherPrice = NULL , '
				END
			
			/*Add Columns*/

			IF (@WHId IS NOT NULL)
				IF (@WHId <> @NumberIsNULL)
				BEGIN
					SET @Columns = @Columns + ' WHId = ' + CAST(@WHId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' , '
				END

			END

				SET @Conditions = ''

					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Columns = @Columns + ' Description = NULL '
					END

					CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50))

				IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(@Columns)) <> '')
					EXECUTE (@QRY + @Columns + @Conditions)

				/**/
			END

		END
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Update Product Price' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN 
	
	/**/
END
