					SET @Columns = LTRIM(substring(LTRIM(@Columns), 2, len(@Columns)));		


				EXECUTE (@QRY + @Columns + @Conditions)

			END

		END	
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Update Product' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN 	
	/*================ End Update ================*/

	
	IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
	BEGIN 
		RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 		SELECT 0
	END 
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
		SELECT 1
	END	

	/**/
END








-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts]
-- ==================================================






/*RZ980231*/
/*AI981221 Created*/
/*AI991014 - change SumPriceAmount calc way*/
/*AI14010707 - Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
/*AI14020325 - Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
/*AI14020627 - Change Ceiling to Round*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts]
/* 	
	EXEC MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts 2149,2,1033,1065	
*/
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
DECLARE @CalcItemTaxResult INT = 0
EXEC [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderItemsTax]
@PreOrderId = @PreOrderId,
@Month1 = @Month1,
@Result = @CalcItemTaxResult OUTPUT	
/*RZ14021030- END*/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*AI14010707- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT pordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] 
												  WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId))
/*AI14010707- End*/
/*Calculate & Update the order*/	
/**/

/*AI14020325 - Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
DECLARE @SumQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TotalTaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
/*AI14020325 - End*/

DECLARE @SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
SELECT 
	/*@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(/*ROUND(*/
							CAST(SUM(
							 (ISNULL(pordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL(pordIFCR, 1) * ISNULL(pordIFCRI, 1)) 
							 * 
							 (( ISNULL(pordIPrice, 0) - ( ISNULL(pordIDiscount, 0) + ISNULL(pordIDiscountI, 0) + ISNULL(pordIRound, 0))
							 + ISNULL(pordIRoundPlus, 0)
							 ))	  
	 							+ (ISNULL( pordITax, 0) * ISNULL(pordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL(pordIFCR, 1) * ISNULL(pordIFCRI, 1))
							) AS NUMERIC(18,2))
							/*, 0)*/,0)		*/ /*AI991014*/
	@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(CAST(SUM(ISNULL([pordIPriceSum],0) + ISNULL([pordITaxSum],0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)),0) /*AI991014*/
	/*AI14020325 - Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
	,@SumQuantity = SUM(pordIQuantity)
	,@TotalDiscountSum = SUM(pordIDiscountSum)
	,@TotalTaxSum = SUM(pordITaxSum)
	/*AI14020325 - End*/	
FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId
AND ISNULL(pordINotInSum, 0) = 0
/**/
IF(@SumPriceAmount IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @pordItemCount  INT, @pordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordInstallAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordSumAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTax NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordCalculateTax BIT,@pordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),@pordRound NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordTotalAmountStr NVARCHAR(1000)	
	/**/			
	SELECT 
       @pordInstallAmount =[pordInstallAmount],
       @pordDiscount = [pordDiscount],
       @pordTaxIndex = [pordTaxIndex],
       @pordCalculateTax = [pordCalculateTax],
       @pordDiscountPercent = [pordDiscountPercent],
       @pordTransportation = [pordTransportation],
       @pordRound = [pordRound]      
	FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] PO
	WHERE PO.[PreOrderID] = @PreOrderId
	/**/
	SET @pordItemsAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)
	IF(@pordDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL AND @pordDiscountPercent > 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordDiscount = (ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
	END
	/**/
	SET @pordSumAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) + ISNULL(@pordInstallAmount,0) - ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0)
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@pordCalculateTax,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTax = ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordTaxIndex,0) / 100
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTax = 0
	END
	/**/
	SET @pordTotalAmount = ISNULL(ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0) + ISNULL(@pordTax,0) + ISNULL(@pordTransportation,0) - ISNULL(@pordRound,0),0)
	/**/
	SET @pordItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] POI WHERE POI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*AI14010707- Add Check IsDecimal in CurrencyUnit*/
	IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
	BEGIN					
		/*AI14020627-Change Ceiling to Round*/
		SET @pordItemsAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordItemsAmount))					
		SET @pordSumAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordSumAmount))		
		SET @pordTax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTax))		
		SET @pordTotalAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTotalAmount))		
		SET @pordDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordDiscount))	
		
		/*AI14020325- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/	
		SET @TotalDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)))	
		SET @TotalTaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)))	
		/*AI14020325- End*/
		/*AI14020627- End*/
	END
	/*AI14010707- End*/




	/*************** ToTalAmountStr *******************/
	DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
	IF(@pordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN			
		SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
		SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
		/**/
		IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr /*+ N' —Ì«·'*/
		END
		ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = REPLACE(@pordTotalAmountStr,'-',' ') 
		END
	END
	/**************************************************/
	/*Update*/
	UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
	SET  [pordItemCount] = ISNULL(@pordItemCount,0)
		,[pordItemsAmount] = ISNULL(@pordItemsAmount,0)
		,[pordSumAmount] = ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0)
		,[pordTax] = ISNULL(@pordTax,0)
		,[pordTotalAmount] = @pordTotalAmount
		,[pordTotalAmountStr] = @pordTotalAmountStr
		,[pordDiscount] = ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0)
		,[Month1] = @Month1
		/*AI14020325 - Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		,[pordItemsQty] = ISNULL(@SumQuantity,0)
		,[pordItemsDiscount] = ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)
		,[pordItemsTax] = ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)
		/*AI14020325 - End*/
	WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId
	/**/
	IF(@pordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @LangId INT
		/**/
		DECLARE Crsr CURSOR FOR
			SELECT LanguageID
			FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
			WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
		/**/
		OPEN Crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			While @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN	 
				DECLARE @pordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				IF(@LangId = 1065)
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang /*+ N' —Ì«·'*/
				END
				ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
					END							
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@pordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
				END
				/**/
				DECLARE @PreOrdIdLang INT
				SET @PreOrdIdLang = (SELECT [WSPreOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang] WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
				IF(@PreOrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
						SET [pordTotalAmountStr] = @pordTotalAmountStrLang
					WHERE [WSPreOrderLangID] = @PreOrdIdLang						
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
						([PreOrderID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[pordTotalAmountStr])
					VALUES
						(@PreOrderId
						,@LangId
						,@pordTotalAmountStrLang)
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			END
		CLOSE Crsr
		DEALLOCATE Crsr	
	END							
	/*==================================================*/			
END		

/*==================================================*/	
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
EXEC [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmountsCurrency]	
@PreOrderId = @PreOrderId,
@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
/*RZ14030301- End*/
/*==================================================*/
/**/
END









-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser]
-- ==================================================




/*RZ971221*/
/*AI990313- add user site*/
CREATE Procedure [dbo].[MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser]
(
/*  
	exec MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 		
*/
@Mode INT,
@Id INT,
@CrmUserId INT,
@IsAccess BIT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @CheckForbidden BIT = 0, @CheckJustAccess BIT = 1
	IF(@CrmUserId= -10) /*AI990313 user is site*/
	BEGIN
		SET @IsAccess = 1
	END
	ELSE
	IF(@Mode = 1)/*Province*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenProvince
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 [ForbiddenProvinceId] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenProvince
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [ForbiddenProvinceId] = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessProvince
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessProvinceId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessProvince]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessProvinceId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 2)/*City*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCity
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCity
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [ForbiddenCityId] = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCity
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessCityId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessCity]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessCityId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 3)/*Group*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroup
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroup
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND ForbiddenGroupId = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroup
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessGroupId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessGroup]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessGroupId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 4)/*SubGroup*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenSubGroup
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenSubGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenSubGroup
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND ForbiddenSubGroupId = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessSubGroup
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessSubGroupId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessSubGroup]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessSubGroupId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 5)/*GroupIV*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroupIV
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenGroupIVId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroupIV
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND ForbiddenGroupIVId = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroupIV
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessGroupIVId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessGroupIV]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessGroupIVId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 6)/*Company*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		/*Forbidden*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCompany
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 ForbiddenCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCompany
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND ForbiddenCompanyId = @Id) IS NOT NULL)
				SET @CheckForbidden = 1 
			ELSE
				SET @CheckForbidden = 0
		END
		/*JustAccess*/
		IF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCompany
			WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId ) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF((SELECT TOP 1 [JustAccessCompanyId]     
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyJustAccessCompany]
				WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId
				AND [JustAccessCompanyId] = @Id) IS NULL)
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 0
			ELSE
				SET @CheckJustAccess = 1
		END
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CheckForbidden = 0 AND @CheckJustAccess = 1)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 7)/*cmpReferUser*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(
			(SELECT * FROM
			(SELECT @CrmUserId ReferUserId 
			 UNION 
			 SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers 
			 WHERE CrmUserId = @CrmUserId 
			 AND LinkedTypeId = 14 ) as tmp
			 WHERE ReferUserId = @Id			 			 
			 ) IS NOT NULL		
		  )
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------*/
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 8)/*Access see company with all permission*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		DECLARE @Qry NVARCHAR(MAX) = '' , @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(max) = '', @CmpId INT		
		/*SET DataPermission*/		 
		EXEC dbo.MobspSetcmpDataPermission @CrmUserId
		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
		FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
		WHERE TypeId = 20
		AND UserId = @CrmUserId
		/*SET Query*/
		SET @Qry = 'SELECT @CmpId = [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
					WHERE CompanyID = ' + CAST(@Id AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' ' + ISNULL(@cmpDataPermision,'')		
		/*RUN Query*/
        EXECUTE sp_executesql @Qry, N' @CmpId INT OUTPUT', @CmpId OUTPUT			
		/*Set Access*/
		IF(@CmpId IS NOT NULL)
			SET @IsAccess = 1		
		/**/
	END
/**/
END





-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCmpEconomicCode]
-- ==================================================





/*AI14010329 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCmpEconomicCode]
(		
/* 
	EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateCmpEconomicCode '555',0,1033,1065
*/
@CmpEconomicCode NVARCHAR(50),
@CurrId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT TOP 1
		 [CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
					ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)CompanyName
		  				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C  
	WHERE C.[CompanyID] <> @CurrId
	AND C.[cmpEconomicCode] = @CmpEconomicCode
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT TOP 1
		 [CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
					ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
						ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)CompanyName		
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C  
	WHERE C.[CompanyID] <> @CurrId
	AND C.[cmpEconomicCode] = @CmpEconomicCode	
END
/**/
END







-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCmpEmail]
-- ==================================================





/*AI14010329 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCmpEmail]
(		
/* 
	EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateCmpEmail 'email',7387,1033,1065
*/
@CmpEmail NVARCHAR(100),
@CurrId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT TOP 1
		 [CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
					ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END) CompanyName				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C  
	WHERE C.[CompanyID] <> @CurrId
	AND C.[cmpEmail] = @CmpEmail
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT TOP 1
		 [CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
					ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
						ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)CompanyName
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C  
	WHERE C.[CompanyID] <> @CurrId
	AND C.[cmpEmail] = @CmpEmail	
END
/**/
END

/*AI990311 - Copied from web*/
/*AI991008 - Add cmpMainPersonId*/
/*AI14010329 - Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCmpMobile]
(		
/* 
	EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateCmpMobile '09902008462',0,1033,1065
*/
@CmpMobile NVARCHAR(100),
@CurrId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT TOP 1
		[CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		/*,(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
			ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''))  CompanyName	AI14010329*/
		/*AI14010329 - Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
					ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END) CompanyName
		/*AI14010329 - End*/	
		,[cmpMainPersonId]	/*AI991008*/
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C  
	WHERE C.[CompanyID] <> @CurrId
	AND C.[cmpMobile] = @CmpMobile
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT TOP 1
		[CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		/*,(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
			ISNULL((SELECT [cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.[CompanyID] = C.[CompanyID] AND CLN.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID),''))  CompanyName  AI14010329*/
		/*AI14010329 - Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
					ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
						ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END) CompanyName
		/*AI14010329 - End*/	
		,[cmpMainPersonId]	/*AI991008*/
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C  
	WHERE C.[CompanyID] <> @CurrId
	AND C.[cmpMobile] = @CmpMobile	
END

/**/
END

BEGIN
	SELECT TOP 1
		 [CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
					ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')

				)

		 [CompanyId]

			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
					ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))

						ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)

	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C

	AND C.[cmpNationalCode] = @CmpNationalCode

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompany]

*/

@LackOfShowText NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)

AS
BEGIN
/**/

	SET @LackOfShowText = N'⁄œ„ ‰„«Ì‘'

/*--*/
EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(4000)

FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId

			THEN (ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''''))

			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')

			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')

					REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') )

				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = '

		BEGIN

				' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+ 
						REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
						NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')  
				OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR prsFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsMobile = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' 
					+ CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), CP.prsLName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+
					REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') )' 
			end
		END 
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID <> 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN 
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
					NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			 ') AND cmpTitleId = ' + Cast(@TitleID AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
	END
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID = 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
					NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ 
					REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			  /*AND cmpTitleId = @TitleID*/
			') AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
END
END


/*--*/
/*--*/
IF @cmpDataPermision <> ''
BEGIN
EXECUTE(
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fld+ @from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +
' ) C union ' + 
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fldLimit+ @from+ @term +
') d
where CmpId Not IN ( SELECT CompanyId '+
@from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +' )'
)
END
ELSE
EXECUTE(@fld+ @from+ @term)
/**/
END







-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompanyCode]
-- ==================================================





/*AI990818*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompanyCode]
(	
/*		
	DECLARE	@Result NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''	
	 EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateCompanyCode 'òœ', 0, 1065, 1065 , @RecordCount = @Result OUTPUT;
	  SELECT @Result AS Record
 */
@cmpCode NVARCHAR(500), 
@CurrCmpID INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL ,
@RecordCount NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)

AS
BEGIN
/**/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT @RecordCount = (SELECT TOP 1
		cmpName
	FROM dbo.CrmCompany C
	WHERE cmpCode = @cmpCode
	AND C.CompanyID <> @CurrCmpID)
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT @RecordCount = (SELECT TOP 1
		cmpName
	FROM dbo.CrmCompany C
	WHERE cmpCode = @cmpCode
	AND C.CompanyID <> @CurrCmpID)
END
/**/
END

			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
						NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			 ') AND cmpTitleId = ' + Cast(@TitleID AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
	END
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID = 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ 
						REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			  /*AND cmpTitleId = @TitleID*/
			') AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
	END
END

/*--*/
/*--*/
/*--*/
IF @cmpDataPermision <> ''
BEGIN
EXECUTE(
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fld+ @from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +
' ) C union ' + 
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fldLimit+ @from+ @term +
') d
where CmpId Not IN ( SELECT CompanyId '+
@from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +' )'
)
END
ELSE
EXECUTE(@fld+ @from+ @term)
/**/
END























-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicatePreOrderNumRef]
-- ==================================================






/*AI14000205*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicatePreOrderNumRef]
(		
/*
	EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePreOrderNumRef] 'dddd', 0
*/
@PordNumRef NVARCHAR(150),
@CurrPreOrderId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT pordNumber,pordNumberRevision,pordNumRef
	FROM dbo.WSPreOrder
	WHERE pordNumRef = @PordNumRef
	AND PreOrderID <> @CurrPreOrderID		
/**/
END







-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateTel]
-- ==================================================





/*AI14010329 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateTel]
(		
/* 
	EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateTel '555',2, 0,1033,1065
*/
@Tel NVARCHAR(100),
@Mode INT,/*Mode = 1 ==> company, Mode = 2 ==> person*/
@CurrId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	IF(@Mode = 1)/*Company*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT TOP 1
			 C.[CompanyID]
			,C.[cmpNumber]		
			,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
						ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
							ISNULL(CCP.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCP.prsLName,'')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName
			,CP.[CompanyPersonID]		
			,(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.[PersonTitleID] = CP.[prsTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
			  ISNULL(CP.[prsFName],'') + ' ' + ISNULL(CP.[prsLName],'')) [PersonName]
		FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C LEFT JOIN 
			  [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId
		WHERE C.[CompanyID] <> @CurrId/*company*/
		AND ( 
			LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) = @Tel		
 			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '')))+ LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) = @Tel 	 	
 			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '') )) , 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTel))+LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) =  @Tel	 	
			OR STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) ) = @Tel 			 	
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '')))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) ) = @Tel 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTel))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) ) = @Tel 			
			OR (@Tel BETWEEN /*C.cmpTel*/ 
				LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)) AND 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))  )), 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM(  C.cmpTel)) )))-
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )))+1, 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), 
						LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) )) 
			AND 
			( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM(@Tel)), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM(@Tel)))-
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )))+1, 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), 
					'' ))	
			 = 
			( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)) )), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + 
					LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)) )))-
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )))+1, 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), 
					'' ))/**/) 		
			/**/				
			/*--*/						
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII )) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, '')))+ LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII )) = @Tel 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTelII)) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII )) =  @Tel		
			OR STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, '')))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ) = @Tel			 	 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTelII))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ) = @Tel 
			/**/
			OR (@Tel BETWEEN /*C.cmpTelII*/ 
					LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)) AND 
					( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))  )), 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM(  C.cmpTelII)) )))-
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )))+1, 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), 
							LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) )) 
				AND 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM(  @Tel)), 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM(  @Tel)))-
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )))+1, 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), 
						'' ))	
				 = 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)) )), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))) + 
					LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)) )))-
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )))+1, 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), 
					'' ))/**/) 		
			/**/				
			/*--*/
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpFax )) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '')))+LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpFax )) = @Tel 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM(ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTel)) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpFax )) =  @Tel	
			/*--*/
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpMobile )) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpMobile, '') )) , 2, LEN(C.cmpMobile)) = @Tel	 	 
			/*--*/ 
			/*--*/
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel )) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, '') ))+LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel )) = @Tel 

			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '021') + LTRIM(RTRIM( prsTel)) = @Tel 
			/*--*/
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '21') + LTRIM(RTRIM( prsTel)) = @Tel
			/*--*/
			OR SUBSTRING( LTRIM(RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + LTRIM(RTRIM( prsTel)) = @Tel		
			OR STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO )) ) = @Tel 			 	
			
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, '')))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO )) ) = @Tel 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + 
				STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))), 
				LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )) ) = @Tel 
			/**/
			OR (@Tel BETWEEN /*CP.prsTel*/ 
					LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)) AND 
					( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))  )), 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM(  CP.prsTel)) )))-
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )))+1, 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), 
							LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )) )) 
				AND 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM(  @Tel)), 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM(  @Tel)))-
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )))+1, 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), 
						'' ))	
				 = 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)) )), 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + 
						LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)) )))-
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )))+1, 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), 
						'' ))/**/) 		
			/**/			
			/*--*/			
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelII )) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, '')))+LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelII )) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '021') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsTelII)) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '21') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsTelII)) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsTelII)) = @Tel 
			/*--*/		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsFax )) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '021') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsFax)) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '21') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsFax)) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsFax)) = @Tel 		 	
	
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel))+LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsFax )) = @Tel 
			/*--*/
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsMobile )) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM(RTRIM( prsMobile)), 2, LEN(prsMobile)) = @Tel )
	END
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 2)/*Person*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT TOP 1
			 C.[CompanyID]
			,C.[cmpNumber]		
			,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
						ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
							ISNULL(CCP.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCP.prsLName,'')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName
			,CP.[CompanyPersonID]		
			,(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.[PersonTitleID] = CP.[prsTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
			  ISNULL(CP.[prsFName],'') + ' ' + ISNULL(CP.[prsLName],'')) [PersonName]
		FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C LEFT JOIN 
			  [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId
		WHERE (CP.[CompanyPersonID] <> @CurrId OR CP.[CompanyPersonID] IS NULL)/*person*/
		AND ( 
			LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) = @Tel		
 			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '')))+ LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) = @Tel 	 	
 			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '') )) , 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTel))+LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) =  @Tel	 	
			OR STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) ) = @Tel 			 	
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '')))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) ) = @Tel 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTel))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) ) = @Tel 			
			OR (@Tel BETWEEN /*C.cmpTel*/ 
				LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)) AND 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))  )), 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM(  C.cmpTel)) )))-
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )))+1, 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), 
						LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) )) 
			AND 
			( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM(@Tel)), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM(@Tel)))-
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )))+1, 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), 
					'' ))	
			 = 
			( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)) )), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + 
					LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)) )))-
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )))+1, 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), 
					'' ))/**/) 		
			/**/				
			/*--*/						
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII )) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, '')))+ LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII )) = @Tel 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTelII)) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII )) =  @Tel		
			OR STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, '')))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ) = @Tel			 	 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTelII))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ) = @Tel 
			/**/
			OR (@Tel BETWEEN /*C.cmpTelII*/ 
					LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)) AND 
					( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))  )), 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM(  C.cmpTelII)) )))-
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )))+1, 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), 
							LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) )) 
				AND 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM(  @Tel)), 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM(  @Tel)))-
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )))+1, 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), 
						'' ))	
				 = 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)) )), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))) + 
					LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)) )))-
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )))+1, 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), 
					'' ))/**/) 		
			/**/				
			/*--*/
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpFax )) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '')))+LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpFax )) = @Tel 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM(ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTel)) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpFax )) =  @Tel	
			/*--*/
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpMobile )) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpMobile, '') )) , 2, LEN(C.cmpMobile)) = @Tel	 	 
			/*--*/ 
			/*--*/
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel )) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, '') ))+LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel )) = @Tel 

			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '021') + LTRIM(RTRIM( prsTel)) = @Tel 
			/*--*/
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '21') + LTRIM(RTRIM( prsTel)) = @Tel
			/*--*/
			OR SUBSTRING( LTRIM(RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + LTRIM(RTRIM( prsTel)) = @Tel		
			OR STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO )) ) = @Tel 			 	
			
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, '')))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO )) ) = @Tel 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + 
				STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))), 
				LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )) ) = @Tel 
			/**/
			OR (@Tel BETWEEN /*CP.prsTel*/ 
					LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)) AND 
					( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))  )), 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM(  CP.prsTel)) )))-
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )))+1, 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), 
							LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )) )) 
				AND 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM(  @Tel)), 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM(  @Tel)))-
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )))+1, 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), 
						'' ))	
				 = 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)) )), 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + 
						LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)) )))-
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )))+1, 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), 
						'' ))/**/) 		
			/**/			
			/*--*/			
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelII )) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, '')))+LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelII )) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '021') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsTelII)) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '21') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsTelII)) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsTelII)) = @Tel 
			/*--*/		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsFax )) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '021') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsFax)) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '21') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsFax)) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsFax)) = @Tel 		 	
	
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel))+LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsFax )) = @Tel 
			/*--*/
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsMobile )) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM(RTRIM( prsMobile)), 2, LEN(prsMobile)) = @Tel )
	END	
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	IF(@Mode = 1)/*Company*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT TOP 1
			 C.[CompanyID]
			,C.[cmpNumber]		
			,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
						ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
							ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName	
			,CP.[CompanyPersonID]		
			,(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.[PersonTitleID] = CP.[prsTitleId] AND PT.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
			  ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL([prsFName],'') + ' ' + ISNULL([prsLName],'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CPLN WHERE CPLN.[CompanyPersonID] = CP.[CompanyPersonID]  AND CPLN.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID),'')) [PersonName]
		FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C LEFT JOIN 
			  [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId
		WHERE C.[CompanyID] <> @CurrId/*company*/
		AND ( 
			LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) = @Tel		
 			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '')))+ LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) = @Tel 	 	
 			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '') )) , 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTel))+LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) =  @Tel	 	
			OR STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) ) = @Tel 			 	
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '')))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) ) = @Tel 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTel))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) ) = @Tel 			
			OR (@Tel BETWEEN /*C.cmpTel*/ 
				LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)) AND 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))  )), 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM(  C.cmpTel)) )))-
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )))+1, 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), 
						LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) )) 
			AND 
			( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM(@Tel)), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM(@Tel)))-
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )))+1, 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), 
					'' ))	
			 = 
			( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)) )), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + 
					LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)) )))-
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )))+1, 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), 
					'' ))/**/) 		
			/**/				
			/*--*/						
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII )) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, '')))+ LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII )) = @Tel 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTelII)) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII )) =  @Tel		
			OR STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, '')))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ) = @Tel			 	 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTelII))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ) = @Tel 
			/**/
			OR (@Tel BETWEEN /*C.cmpTelII*/ 
					LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)) AND 
					( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))  )), 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM(  C.cmpTelII)) )))-
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )))+1, 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), 
							LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ))

						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM(  @Tel)))-

			/*--*/ 
			/*--*/
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel )) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, '') ))+LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel )) = @Tel 

			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '021') + LTRIM(RTRIM( prsTel)) = @Tel 
			/*--*/
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '21') + LTRIM(RTRIM( prsTel)) = @Tel
			/*--*/
			OR SUBSTRING( LTRIM(RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + LTRIM(RTRIM( prsTel)) = @Tel		
			OR STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO )) ) = @Tel 			 	
			
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, '')))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO )) ) = @Tel 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + 
				STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))), 
				LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )) ) = @Tel 
			/**/
			OR (@Tel BETWEEN /*CP.prsTel*/ 
					LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)) AND 
					( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))  )), 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM(  CP.prsTel)) )))-
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )))+1, 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), 
							LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )) )) 
				AND 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM(  @Tel)), 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM(  @Tel)))-
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )))+1, 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), 
						'' ))	
				 = 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)) )), 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + 
						LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)) )))-
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )))+1, 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), 
						'' ))/**/)

			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '021') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsTelII)) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '21') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsTelII)) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsTelII)) = @Tel 
			/*--*/		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsFax )) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '021') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsFax)) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '21') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsFax)) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsFax)) = @Tel 		 	
	
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel))+LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsFax )) = @Tel 
			/*--*/
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsMobile )) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM(RTRIM( prsMobile)), 2, LEN(prsMobile)) = @Tel )
	END
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 2)/*Person*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT TOP 1
			 C.[CompanyID]
			,C.[cmpNumber]		
			,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
						ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
							ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName	
			,CP.[CompanyPersonID]		
			,(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.[PersonTitleID] = CP.[prsTitleId] AND PT.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
			  ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL([prsFName],'') + ' ' + ISNULL([prsLName],'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CPLN WHERE CPLN.[CompanyPersonID] = CP.[CompanyPersonID]  AND CPLN.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID),'')) [PersonName]
		FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C LEFT JOIN 
			  [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId
		WHERE (CP.[CompanyPersonID] <> @CurrId OR CP.[CompanyPersonID] IS NULL)/*person*/
		AND ( 
			LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) = @Tel		
 			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '')))+ LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) = @Tel 	 	
 			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '') )) , 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTel))+LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) =  @Tel

					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) ) = @Tel

				LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)) AND

						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM(  C.cmpTel)) )))-

					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM(@Tel)))-

					LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)) )))-

			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, '')))+ LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII )) = @Tel 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTelII)) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII )) =  @Tel

					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ) = @Tel			 	 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTelII))+

					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ) = @Tel

							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM(  C.cmpTelII)) )))-
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )))+1, 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), 
							LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ))

						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM(  @Tel)))-

						'' ))	
				 =

			/*--*/
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpFax )) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '')))+LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpFax )) = @Tel 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM(ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTel)) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpFax )) =  @Tel	
			/*--*/
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpMobile )) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpMobile, '') )) , 2, LEN(C.cmpMobile)) = @Tel	 	 
			/*--*/ 
			/*--*/
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel )) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, '') ))+LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel )) = @Tel 

			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '021') + LTRIM(RTRIM( prsTel)) = @Tel 
			/*--*/
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '21') + LTRIM(RTRIM( prsTel)) = @Tel
			/*--*/
			OR SUBSTRING( LTRIM(RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + LTRIM(RTRIM( prsTel)) = @Tel		
			OR STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO )) ) = @Tel 			 	
			
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, '')))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpTO )) ) = @Tel 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + 
				STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))), 
				LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )) ) = @Tel 
			/**/
			OR (@Tel BETWEEN /*CP.prsTel*/ 
					LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)) AND 
					( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel))  )), 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM(  CP.prsTel)) )))-
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )))+1, 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), 
							LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )) )) 
				AND 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM(  @Tel)), 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM(  @Tel)))-
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )))+1, 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), 
						'' ))	
				 = 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)) )), 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, ''))) + 
						LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTel)) )))-
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto )))+1, 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelUpto ))), 
						'' ))/**/) 		
			/**/			
			/*--*/			
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelII )) = @Tel 		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(CP.prsPreTel, '')))+LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsTelII )) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '021') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsTelII)) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '21') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsTelII)) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsTelII)) = @Tel 
			/*--*/		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsFax )) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '021') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsFax)) = @Tel 
			OR ISNULL( LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), '21') + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsFax)) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel)), 2, LEN(CP.prsPreTel)) + LTRIM(RTRIM( CP.prsFax)) = @Tel 		 	
	
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsPreTel))+LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsFax )) = @Tel 
			/*--*/
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( CP.prsMobile )) = @Tel
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM(RTRIM( prsMobile)), 2, LEN(prsMobile)) = @Tel )
	END	
END
/**/
END








-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateZipCode]
-- ==================================================





/*AI14010329 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateZipCode]
(		
/* 
	EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateZipCode '123',2, 0,1033,1065
*/
@ZipCode NVARCHAR(100),
@Mode INT,/*Mode = 1 ==> company, Mode = 2 ==> person*/
@CurrId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	IF(@Mode = 1)/*Company*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT TOP 1
			 C.[CompanyID]
			,C.[cmpNumber]		
			,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
						ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
							ISNULL(CCP.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCP.prsLName,'')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName
			,CP.[CompanyPersonID]		
			,(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.[PersonTitleID] = CP.[prsTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
			  ISNULL(CP.[prsFName],'') + ' ' + ISNULL(CP.[prsLName],'')) [PersonName]
		FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C LEFT JOIN 
			  [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId
		WHERE C.[CompanyID] <> @CurrId/*company*/
		AND (C.[cmpZipCode] = @ZipCode
				OR CP.[prsZipCode] = @ZipCode)
	END
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 2)/*Person*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT TOP 1
			 C.[CompanyID]
			,C.[cmpNumber]		
			,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
						ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
							ISNULL(CCP.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCP.prsLName,'')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName
			,CP.[CompanyPersonID]		
			,(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.[PersonTitleID] = CP.[prsTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
			  ISNULL(CP.[prsFName],'') + ' ' + ISNULL(CP.[prsLName],'')) [PersonName]
		FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C LEFT JOIN 
			  [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId
		WHERE (CP.[CompanyPersonID] <> @CurrId OR CP.[CompanyPersonID] IS NULL)/*person*/
		AND (C.[cmpZipCode] = @ZipCode
				OR CP.[prsZipCode] = @ZipCode)
	END	
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	IF(@Mode = 1)/*Company*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT TOP 1
			 C.[CompanyID]
			,C.[cmpNumber]		
			,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
						ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
							ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName	
			,CP.[CompanyPersonID]		
			,(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.[PersonTitleID] = CP.[prsTitleId] AND PT.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
			  ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL([prsFName],'') + ' ' + ISNULL([prsLName],'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CPLN WHERE CPLN.[CompanyPersonID] = CP.[CompanyPersonID]  AND CPLN.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID),'')) [PersonName]
		FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C LEFT JOIN 
			  [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId
		WHERE C.[CompanyID] <> @CurrId/*company*/
		AND (C.[cmpZipCode] = @ZipCode
				OR CP.[prsZipCode] = @ZipCode)
	END
	ELSE IF(@Mode = 2)/*Person*/
	BEGIN
		SELECT TOP 1
			 C.[CompanyID]
			,C.[cmpNumber]		
			,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
						ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
							ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName	
			,CP.[CompanyPersonID]		
			,(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.[PersonTitleID] = CP.[prsTitleId] AND PT.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
			  ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL([prsFName],'') + ' ' + ISNULL([prsLName],'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CPLN WHERE CPLN.[CompanyPersonID] = CP.[CompanyPersonID]  AND CPLN.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID),'')) [PersonName]
		FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C LEFT JOIN 
			  [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId
		WHERE (CP.[CompanyPersonID] <> @CurrId OR CP.[CompanyPersonID] IS NULL)/*person*/
		AND (C.[cmpZipCode] = @ZipCode
				OR CP.[prsZipCode] = @ZipCode)
	END	
END
/**/
END
