/*AI14020529*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspInsertDocFull]
/*
	declare @p13 nvarchar(max)
	set @p13=N'#|^|#Tracing=New|^|NewTracing=newtra'
	declare @p14 int
	set @p14=NULL
	declare @p15 nvarchar(max)
	set @p15=NULL
	declare @p16 nvarchar(max)
	set @p16=NULL
	exec [dbo].[MobspInsertDocFull] @DocParams=N'{"DocID":1,"OprId":1,"DocNumber":null,"DocDate":"1402/05/30","SenderId":null,"SenderName":null,"FactorNumber":"”›«—‘","Description":" Ê÷","IsFix":null,"WHId":2,"OrderId":41394,"BuyOrderId":null,"SendAddress":null,"DriverName":null,"DriverTel":null,"FreightName":null,"BijakNumber":null,"CarNumber":null,"DocDateTime":"2023-08-21 13:24:43.000","WHIdI":null,"CompanyId":68805,"CompanyPersonId":41582,"ProjectId":18195,"cmpTitle":null,"DocNumRef":null,"IsVerified":null,"VerifierId":54,"VerifiedRegisterDate":null,"VerifiedRegisterDatePersian":null,"VerifierRequestId":null,"VerificationWorkFlowId":22,"PreOrderId":null}',@DocItemParams=N'[{"DocItemID":1,"DocId":1,"PrdId":7909,"Quantity":3.0,"CostId":null,"Cost":null,"Description":"","IsFix":null,"WHId":2,"NotVisible":null,"FCR":1.0,"FCRI":2.0,"Tracing":"newtra","PrdUnitId":3,"QuantityTotal":null,"PrdUnitIdI":3,"PrdUnitIRateResult":1.0,"PrdNickname":"„” ⁄«—"},{"DocItemID":2,"DocId":1,"PrdId":7910,"Quantity":1.0,"CostId":null,"Cost":null,"Description":"","IsFix":null,"WHId":2,"NotVisible":null,"FCR":0.0,"FCRI":0.0,"Tracing":null,"PrdUnitId":null,"QuantityTotal":null,"PrdUnitIdI":null,"PrdUnitIRateResult":null,"PrdNickname":null}]',@DocItemSerialParams=N'[]',@UserId=3,@FPId=30,@Month1=5,@RegisterDate='2023-08-21 13:54:43.7000923',@RegisterDatePersian=N'14020530',@AppType='mob',@LanguageID=1065,@DefaultLanguageID=1065,@ResErrorMessage=@p13 output,@ResInsertedDocId=@p14 output,@ResInsertedDocItemIds=@p15 output,@ResInsertedDocItemSerialIds=@p16 output
	select @p13, @p14, @p15, @p16
*/
(
@DocParams NVARCHAR(MAX),
@DocItemParams NVARCHAR(MAX),
@DocItemSerialParams NVARCHAR(MAX),
@UserId INT,
@FPId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
/**/
@ResErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,
@ResInsertedDocId INT OUTPUT,
@ResInsertedDocItemIds NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,
@ResInsertedDocItemSerialIds NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
	BEGIN TRY
		SET @ResErrorMessage = ''

		/**************************************************************************/
		/***********************************HELPER*********************************/
		/**************************************************************************/
		DECLARE @RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8)
		SET @RegisterTime = (CONVERT(CHAR(8), @RegisterDate, 108))	

		DECLARE @DupDocNumber INT, @actAutoCalcNumRef BIT, @actNotDupNumRef BIT,
				@SysMustFullTracingCode BIT, @PrdMustFullTracingCode BIT, @actLoadTracingCodeFromAllPrd BIT,
				@actCanNotAddNewTracingCode BIT, @tracingPrdId INT, @DocItemTracingExist NVARCHAR(150),
				@OprType INT, @TracingRemainCount FLOAT, @SysAddTracingCodeFromSerial BIT

		SELECT
			@SysMustFullTracingCode = [MustFullTracingCode],
			@SysAddTracingCodeFromSerial = [AddTracingCodeFromSerial]
		FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]

		DECLARE @CheckDuplicateDocNumRefTmpTbl TABLE(
			[DocNumber] INT,
			[DocNumRef] NVARCHAR(150)
		)

		DECLARE @DocItemTracingTmpTbl TABLE(
			[Tracing] NVARCHAR(200)
		)

		DECLARE @ReturnPrdQuantityTmpTbl TABLE(
			[QuantityRemain] FLOAT,
			[QuantityIn] FLOAT,
			[QuantityOut] FLOAT,
			[PrdId] INT,
			[PrdName] NVARCHAR(1000),
			[RemainCount] FLOAT,
			[NetPreOrderCount] FLOAT,
			[RemainCountAllWH] FLOAT,
			[RemainCurrAndAll] NVARCHAR(200),
			[TracingRemainCount] FLOAT
		)

		DECLARE @DuplicateSerialTmpTbl TABLE(
			DocItemSerialID INT,
			ItemId INT,
			Serial NVARCHAR(50),
			SerialId INT,
			StatusId INT,
			PrdId INT,
			PrdName NVARCHAR(1000),
			DocNumber INT
		)

		DECLARE @diInsertedDocItemID INT, @diDocItemID INT, @diDocId INT, @diPrdId INT, @diQuantity FLOAT, @diCostId INT, @diCost NUMERIC(18,0),
				@diDescription NVARCHAR(MAX), @diIsFix SMALLINT, @diWHId INT, @diNotVisible BIT, @diFCR FLOAT, @diFCRI FLOAT, @diTracing NVARCHAR(200),
				@diPrdUnitId INT, @diQuantityTotal FLOAT, @diPrdUnitIdI INT, @diPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT, @diPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000)

		DECLARE @disInsertedDocItemSerialID INT, @disDocItemSerialID INT, @disItemId INT, @disSerial NVARCHAR(50), @disStatusId INT,
				@disDescription NVARCHAR(300), @disIsFix SMALLINT, @disWHId INT, @disSerialId INT, @disIsAutoBarcode BIT,
				@distblTmpBarcodeId INT, @disExpDate DATETIME,
				@disExpDatePersian NVARCHAR(8), @disInfoI NVARCHAR(200), @disInfoII NVARCHAR(200), @disInfoIII NVARCHAR(200),
				@disInfoIV NVARCHAR(200), @disInfoV NVARCHAR(200), @disInfoVI NVARCHAR(200), @disRootId INT, @disTracing NVARCHAR(200),
				@disPrdId INT


		/**************************************************************************/
		/***********************************DOC************************************/
		/**************************************************************************/

		BEGIN TRY
			DECLARE
				@InsertedDocId INT
				,@DocID INT
				,@OprId INT
				,@DocNumber INT
				,@DocDate NVARCHAR(8)
				,@SenderId INT
				,@SenderName NVARCHAR(500)
				,@FactorNumber NVARCHAR(50)
				,@Description NVARCHAR(4000)
				,@IsFix SMALLINT
				,@WHId INT
				,@OrderId INT
				,@BuyOrderId INT
				,@SendAddress NVARCHAR(500)
				,@DriverName NVARCHAR(100)
				,@DriverTel NVARCHAR(50)
				,@FreightName NVARCHAR(100)
				,@BijakNumber NVARCHAR(50)
				,@CarNumber NVARCHAR(50)
				,@DocDateTime DATETIME
				,@WHIdI INT
				,@CompanyId INT
				,@CompanyPersonId INT
				,@ProjectId INT
				,@cmpTitle NVARCHAR(300)
				,@DocNumRef NVARCHAR(150)
				,@IsVerified BIT
				,@VerifierId INT
				,@VerifiedRegisterDate DATETIME
				,@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8)
				,@VerifierRequestId INT
				,@VerificationWorkFlowId INT
				,@PreOrderId INT

			IF(ISNULL(@DocParams, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				SELECT
					@DocID = DJ.DocID,
					@OprId = DJ.OprId,
					@DocNumber = DJ.DocNumber,
					@DocDate = DJ.DocDate,
					@SenderId = DJ.SenderId,
					@SenderName = DJ.SenderName,
					@FactorNumber = DJ.FactorNumber,
					@Description = DJ.[Description],
					@IsFix = DJ.IsFix,
					@WHId = DJ.WHId,
					@OrderId = DJ.OrderId,
					@BuyOrderId = DJ.BuyOrderId,
					@SendAddress = DJ.SendAddress,
					@DriverName = DJ.DriverName,
					@DriverTel = DJ.DriverTel,
					@FreightName = DJ.FreightName,
					@BijakNumber = DJ.BijakNumber,
					@CarNumber = DJ.CarNumber,
					@DocDateTime = DJ.DocDateTime,
					@WHIdI = DJ.WHIdI,
					@CompanyId = DJ.CompanyId,
					@CompanyPersonId = DJ.CompanyPersonId,
					@ProjectId = DJ.ProjectId,
					@cmpTitle = DJ.cmpTitle,
					@DocNumRef = DJ.DocNumRef,
					@IsVerified = DJ.IsVerified,
					@VerifierId = DJ.VerifierId,
					@VerifiedRegisterDate = DJ.VerifiedRegisterDate,
					@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian = DJ.VerifiedRegisterDatePersian,
					@VerifierRequestId = DJ.VerifierRequestId,
					@VerificationWorkFlowId = DJ.VerificationWorkFlowId,
					@PreOrderId = DJ.PreOrderId
				FROM OPENJSON(@DocParams)
				WITH(
					DocID INT '$.DocID',
					OprId INT '$.OprId',
					DocNumber INT '$.DocNumber',
					DocDate NVARCHAR(8) '$.DocDate',
					SenderId INT '$.SenderId',
					SenderName NVARCHAR(500) '$.SenderName',
					FactorNumber NVARCHAR(50) '$.FactorNumber',
					[Description] NVARCHAR(4000) '$.Description',
					IsFix SMALLINT '$.IsFix',
					WHId INT '$.WHId',
					OrderId INT '$.OrderId',
					BuyOrderId INT '$.BuyOrderId',
					SendAddress NVARCHAR(500) '$.SendAddress',
					DriverName NVARCHAR(100) '$.DriverName',
					DriverTel NVARCHAR(50) '$.DriverTel',
					FreightName NVARCHAR(100) '$.FreightName',
					BijakNumber NVARCHAR(50) '$.BijakNumber',
					CarNumber NVARCHAR(50) '$.CarNumber',
					DocDateTime DATETIME '$.DocDateTime',
					WHIdI INT '$.WHIdI',
					CompanyId INT '$.CompanyId',
					CompanyPersonId INT '$.CompanyPersonId',
					ProjectId INT '$.ProjectId',
					cmpTitle NVARCHAR(300) '$.cmpTitle',
					DocNumRef NVARCHAR(150) '$.DocNumRef',
					IsVerified BIT '$.IsVerified',
					VerifierId INT '$.VerifierId',
					VerifiedRegisterDate DATETIME '$.VerifiedRegisterDate',
					VerifiedRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) '$.VerifiedRegisterDatePersian',
					VerifierRequestId INT '$.VerifierRequestId',
					VerificationWorkFlowId INT '$.VerificationWorkFlowId',
					PreOrderId INT '$.PreOrderId'
				) AS DJ
			END

			/**************************************************************************/
			/********************************DOC ITEM**********************************/
			/**************************************************************************/

			DECLARE @DocItemsTbl TABLE(
				[InsertedDocItemID] INT,
				[DocItemID] INT,
				[DocId] INT,
				[PrdId] INT,
				[Quantity] FLOAT,
				[CostId] INT,
				[Cost] NUMERIC(18,0),
				[Description] NVARCHAR(MAX),
				[IsFix] SMALLINT,
				[WHId] INT,
				[NotVisible] BIT,
				[FCR] FLOAT,
				[FCRI] FLOAT,
				[Tracing] NVARCHAR(200),
				[PrdUnitId] INT,
				[QuantityTotal] FLOAT,
				[PrdUnitIdI] INT,
				[PrdUnitIRateResult] FLOAT,
				[PrdNickname] NVARCHAR(1000)
			)

			IF(ISNULL(@DocItemParams, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO @DocItemsTbl
				SELECT
					NULL
					,DIJ.[DocItemID]
					,DIJ.[DocId]
					,DIJ.[PrdId]
					,DIJ.[Quantity]
					,DIJ.[CostId]
					,DIJ.[Cost]
					,DIJ.[Description]
					,DIJ.[IsFix]
					,DIJ.[WHId]
					,DIJ.[NotVisible]
					,DIJ.[FCR]
					,DIJ.[FCRI]
					,DIJ.[Tracing]
					,DIJ.[PrdUnitId]
					,DIJ.[QuantityTotal]
					,DIJ.[PrdUnitIdI]
					,DIJ.[PrdUnitIRateResult]
					,DIJ.[PrdNickname]
				FROM OPENJSON(@DocItemParams)
				WITH(
					[DocItemID] INT '$.DocItemID',
					[DocId] INT '$.DocId',
					[PrdId] INT '$.PrdId',
					[Quantity] FLOAT '$.Quantity',
					[CostId] INT '$.CostId',
					[Cost] NUMERIC(18,0) '$.Cost',
					[Description] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.Description',
					[IsFix] SMALLINT '$.IsFix',
					[WHId] INT '$.WHId',
					[NotVisible] BIT '$.NotVisible',
					[FCR] FLOAT '$.FCR',
					[FCRI] FLOAT '$.FCRI',
					[Tracing] NVARCHAR(200) '$.Tracing',
					[PrdUnitId] INT '$.PrdUnitId',
					[QuantityTotal] FLOAT '$.QuantityTotal',
					[PrdUnitIdI] INT '$.PrdUnitIdI',
					[PrdUnitIRateResult] FLOAT '$.PrdUnitIRateResult',
					[PrdNickname] NVARCHAR(1000) '$.PrdNickname'
				) AS DIJ
			END

			/**************************************************************************/
			/****************************DOC ITEM SERIAL*******************************/
			/**************************************************************************/


			DECLARE @DocItemSerialsTbl TABLE (
				[InsertedDocItemSerialID] INT,
				[DocItemSerialID] INT,
				[ItemId] INT,
				[Serial] NVARCHAR(50),
				[StatusId] INT,
				[Description] NVARCHAR(300),
				[IsFix] SMALLINT,
				[WHId] INT,
				[SerialId] INT,
				[IsAutoBarcode] BIT,
				[tblTmpBarcodeId] INT,
				[ExpDate] DATETIME,
				[ExpDatePersian] NVARCHAR(8),
				[InfoI] NVARCHAR(200),
				[InfoII] NVARCHAR(200),
				[InfoIII] NVARCHAR(200),
				[InfoIV] NVARCHAR(200),
				[InfoV] NVARCHAR(200),
				[InfoVI] NVARCHAR(200),
				[RootId] INT,
				[Tracing] NVARCHAR(200)
			)

			IF(ISNULL(@DocItemSerialParams, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO @DocItemSerialsTbl
				SELECT
					NULL,
					DISJ.[DocItemSerialID],
					DISJ.[ItemId],
					DISJ.[Serial],
					DISJ.[StatusId],
					DISJ.[Description],
					DISJ.[IsFix],
					DISJ.[WHId],
					DISJ.[SerialId],
					DISJ.[IsAutoBarcode],
					DISJ.[tblTmpBarcodeId],
					DISJ.[ExpDate],
					DISJ.[ExpDatePersian],
					DISJ.[InfoI],
					DISJ.[InfoII],
					DISJ.[InfoIII],
					DISJ.[InfoIV],
					DISJ.[InfoV],
					DISJ.[InfoVI],
					DISJ.[RootId],
					DISJ.[Tracing]
				FROM OPENJSON(@DocItemSerialParams)
				WITH(
					[DocItemSerialID] INT '$.DocItemSerialID',
					[ItemId] INT '$.ItemId',
					[Serial] NVARCHAR(50) '$.Serial',
					[StatusId] INT '$.StatusId',
					[Description] NVARCHAR(300) '$.Description',
					[IsFix] SMALLINT '$.IsFix',
					[WHId] INT '$.WHId',
					[SerialId] INT '$.SerialId',
					[IsAutoBarcode] BIT '$.IsAutoBarcode',
					[tblTmpBarcodeId] INT '$.tblTmpBarcodeId',
					[ExpDate] DATETIME '$.ExpDate',
					[ExpDatePersian] NVARCHAR(8) '$.ExpDatePersian',
					[InfoI] NVARCHAR(200) '$.InfoI',
					[InfoII] NVARCHAR(200) '$.InfoII',
					[InfoIII] NVARCHAR(200) '$.InfoIII',
					[InfoIV] NVARCHAR(200) '$.InfoIV',
					[InfoV] NVARCHAR(200) '$.InfoV',
					[InfoVI] NVARCHAR(200) '$.InfoVI',
					[RootId] INT '$.RootId',
					[Tracing] NVARCHAR(200) '$.Tracing'
				) AS DISJ
			END

		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
			SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'ParseData=Error'
			GOTO EndOfScript
		END CATCH

		/**************************************************************************/
		/*******************************DocPreCheck********************************/
		/**************************************************************************/

		SET @OprType = ISNULL((SELECT [OprType] FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE [DocOprID] = @OprId), 0)

		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@DocNumber, 0) = 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @DocNumber = (SELECT [dbo].[MobfnReturnDocNumber](@OprId))
		END
		SET @DupDocNumber = (SELECT TOP 1 [DocNumber] FROM [dbo].[MobfnCheckDuplicateDocNumber](@DocNumber, 0, @OprId))
		IF(ISNULL(@DupDocNumber, 0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'DocNumber=Dup|^|DupDocNumber=' + CAST(@DupDocNumber AS NVARCHAR(10))
			SET @DupDocNumber = NULL
			GOTO EndOfScript
		END

		/**/

		IF(@OprType = 1)
		BEGIN
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
				@FormName = 'frmDocDataEntry',
				@ActionName = 'actAutoCalcNumRef',
				@UserId = @UserId,
				@HasAction = @actAutoCalcNumRef OUTPUT
		END
		ELSE IF(@OprType = 2)
		BEGIN
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
				@FormName = 'frmDraftDataEntry',
				@ActionName = 'actAutoCalcNumRef',
				@UserId = @UserId,
				@HasAction = @actAutoCalcNumRef OUTPUT
		END
		ELSE IF(@OprType = 3)
		BEGIN
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
				@FormName = 'frmWareHouseMovementDataEntry',
				@ActionName = 'actAutoCalcNumRef',
				@UserId = @UserId,
				@HasAction = @actAutoCalcNumRef OUTPUT
		END

		IF(@actAutoCalcNumRef = 1)
		BEGIN
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspReturnDocNumRef]
				@DocCompanyId = @CompanyId,
				@DocProjectId = @ProjectId,
				@UserId = @UserId,
				@DocNumber = @DocNumber,
				@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
				@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
				@SelectedNumRef = @DocNumRef OUTPUT
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@DocNumRef, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			IF(@OprType = 1)
			BEGIN
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
					@FormName = 'frmDocDataEntry',
					@ActionName = 'actNotDupNumRef',
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@HasAction = @actNotDupNumRef OUTPUT
			END
			ELSE IF(@OprType = 2)
			BEGIN
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
					@FormName = 'frmDraftDataEntry',
					@ActionName = 'actNotDupNumRef',
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@HasAction = @actNotDupNumRef OUTPUT
			END
			ELSE IF(@OprType = 3)
			BEGIN
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
					@FormName = 'frmWareHouseMovementDataEntry',
					@ActionName = 'actNotDupNumRef',
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@HasAction = @actNotDupNumRef OUTPUT
			END

			IF(@actNotDupNumRef = 1)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO @CheckDuplicateDocNumRefTmpTbl
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateDocNumRef]
					@DocNumRef = @DocNumRef,
					@CurrDocId = 0,
					@DocOprId = @OprId

				SELECT TOP 1 
					@DupDocNumber = [DocNumber]
				FROM @CheckDuplicateDocNumRefTmpTbl

				IF(ISNULL(@DupDocNumber, 0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'DocNumRef=Dup|^|DupDocNumber=' + CAST(@DupDocNumber AS NVARCHAR(10))
					SET @DupDocNumber = NULL
					GOTO EndOfScript
				END

			END
		END


		/**************************************************************************/
		/*******************************Doc Insert*********************************/
		/**************************************************************************/

		IF(ISNULL(@DocDate, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			SET @DocDateTime = dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@DocDate, @RegisterTime)
		END

		INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDoc]
			   ([OprId]
			   ,[DocNumber]
			   ,[DocDate]
			   ,[SenderId]
			   ,[SenderName]
			   ,[FactorNumber]
			   ,[Description]
			   ,[IsFix]
			   ,[WHId]
			   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			   ,[UserId]
			   ,[Month1]
			   ,[FPId]
			   ,[RegisterDate]
			   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[OrderId]
			   ,[BuyOrderId]
			   ,[SendAddress]
			   ,[DriverName]
			   ,[DriverTel]
			   ,[FreightName]
			   ,[BijakNumber]
			   ,[CarNumber]
			   ,[DocDateTime]
			   ,[WHIdI]
			   ,[CompanyId]
			   ,[CompanyPersonId]
			   ,[ProjectId]
			   ,[cmpTitle]
			   ,[AppType]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
			   ,[DocNumRef]
			   ,[IsVerified]
			   ,[VerifierId]
			   ,[VerifiedRegisterDate]
			   ,[VerifiedRegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[VerifierRequestId]
			   ,[VerificationWorkFlowId]
			   ,[PreOrderId])
		 VALUES
			   (@OprId
			   ,@DocNumber
			   ,@DocDate
			   ,@SenderId
			   ,@SenderName
			   ,@FactorNumber
			   ,@Description
			   ,@IsFix
			   ,@WHId
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@Month1
			   ,@FPId
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@OrderId
			   ,@BuyOrderId
			   ,@SendAddress
			   ,@DriverName
			   ,@DriverTel
			   ,@FreightName
			   ,@BijakNumber
			   ,@CarNumber
			   ,@DocDateTime
			   ,@WHIdI
			   ,@CompanyId
			   ,@CompanyPersonId
			   ,@ProjectId
			   ,@cmpTitle
			   ,@AppType
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@DefaultLanguageID
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@DocNumRef
			   ,@IsVerified
			   ,@VerifierId
			   ,@VerifiedRegisterDate
			   ,@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian
			   ,@VerifierRequestId
			   ,@VerificationWorkFlowId
			   ,@PreOrderId
			   )
		SET @InsertedDocId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

		UPDATE @DocItemsTbl SET
			[DocId] = @InsertedDocId

		/******* Insert to lang table ********/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDoc_Lang]
			(
			[DocID]
			,[LanguageID]
			,[SenderName]
			,[Description]
			,[SendAddress]
			,[DriverName]
			,[FreightName]
			,[cmpTitle]
			)
		SELECT
			@InsertedDocId
			,LN.LanguageID
			,@SenderName
			,@Description
			,@SendAddress
			,@DriverName
			,@FreightName
			,@cmpTitle
		FROM dbo.Languages LN
		WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
		AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) =1

		/**************************************************************************/
		/*****************************Doc Item insert******************************/
		/**************************************************************************/

		DECLARE CrsrDocItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		SELECT 
			[InsertedDocItemID], [DocItemID],[DocId],[PrdId],[Quantity],[CostId]
			,[Cost],[Description],[IsFix],[WHId],[NotVisible],[FCR],[FCRI],[Tracing]
			,[PrdUnitId],[QuantityTotal],[PrdUnitIdI],[PrdUnitIRateResult],[PrdNickname]
		FROM @DocItemsTbl OPEN CrsrDocItem FETCH NEXT
		FROM CrsrDocItem INTO @diInsertedDocItemID, @diDocItemID, @diDocId, @diPrdId, @diQuantity, @diCostId, @diCost,
						@diDescription, @diIsFix, @diWHId, @diNotVisible, @diFCR, @diFCRI, @diTracing, @diPrdUnitId, @diQuantityTotal,
						@diPrdUnitIdI, @diPrdUnitIRateResult, @diPrdNickname
		WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0 BEGIN

			SELECT
				@PrdMustFullTracingCode = [PrdMustFullTracingCode]
			FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
			WHERE ProductID = @diPrdId

			IF(ISNULL(@diTracing,'') = '')
			BEGIN
				IF(ISNULL(@SysMustFullTracingCode, 0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Tracing=Force|^|PrdId=' + CAST(@diPrdId AS NVARCHAR(10))
					GOTO EndOfScript
				END
			END
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@PrdMustFullTracingCode, 0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Tracing=Force|^|PrdId=' + CAST(@diPrdId AS NVARCHAR(10))
				GOTO EndOfScript
			END

			IF(@OprType = 1)
			BEGIN
				IF(ISNULL(@diTracing,'') <> '')
				BEGIN
					EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
						@FormName = 'frmDocItemDataEntry',
						@ActionName = 'actLoadTracingCodeFromAllPrd',
						@UserId = @UserId,
						@HasAction = @actLoadTracingCodeFromAllPrd OUTPUT

					EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
						@FormName = 'frmDocItemDataEntry',
						@ActionName = 'actCanNotAddNewTracingCode',
						@UserId = @UserId,
						@HasAction = @actCanNotAddNewTracingCode OUTPUT

					SET @tracingPrdId = @diPrdId
					IF(@actLoadTracingCodeFromAllPrd = 1)
					BEGIN
						SET @tracingPrdId = 0
					END

					IF(@actCanNotAddNewTracingCode = 1)
					BEGIN
						SET @DocItemTracingExist = NULL
						DELETE @DocItemTracingTmpTbl

						INSERT INTO @DocItemTracingTmpTbl
						EXEC [dbo].[MobspSelectDocItemTracing]
							@Tracing = @diTracing,
							@PrdId = @tracingPrdId

					
						SELECT TOP 1
							@DocItemTracingExist = [Tracing]
						FROM @DocItemTracingTmpTbl

						IF(@DocItemTracingExist IS NULL)
						BEGIN
							SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Tracing=New|^|NewTracing=' + @diTracing + 
								'|^|PrdId=' + CAST(@diPrdId AS NVARCHAR(10))
							GOTO EndOfScript
						END
					END
				END
			END

			IF(@OprType = 2 OR @OprType = 3)
			BEGIN
				SET @TracingRemainCount = NULL
				DELETE @ReturnPrdQuantityTmpTbl

				DECLARE @QtyWhId INT = NULL
				IF(@OprType = 3)
				BEGIN
					SET @QtyWhId = @WHIdI
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @QtyWhId = @WHId
				END
				
				INSERT INTO @ReturnPrdQuantityTmpTbl
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspReturnPrdQuantity]
					@PrdId = @diPrdId,
					@FPId = @FPId,
					@WHId = @QtyWhId,
					@CurrId = 0,
					@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
					@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
					@FCR = @diFCR,
					@FCRI = @diFCRI,
					@Tracing = @diTracing,
					@QuantityRemain = NULL

				SELECT TOP 1
					@TracingRemainCount = [TracingRemainCount]
				FROM @ReturnPrdQuantityTmpTbl

				IF(ISNULL(@diQuantity, 1) > ISNULL(@TracingRemainCount, 0))
				BEGIN
					SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'QtyRemainLimit|^|PrdId=' + CAST(@diPrdId AS NVARCHAR(10))
					GOTO EndOfScript
				END
					
			END


			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItems]
				([DocId]
				,[PrdId]
				,[Quantity]
				,[CostId]
				,[Cost]
				,[Description]
				,[IsFix]
				,[WHId]
				,[UserId]
				,[Month1]
				,[FPId]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[NotVisible]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[FCR]
				,[FCRI]
				,[Tracing]
				,[PrdUnitId]
				,[QuantityTotal]
				,[PrdUnitIdI]
				,[PrdUnitIRateResult]
				,[PrdNickname])
			VALUES
				(@diDocId
				,@diPrdId
				,@diQuantity
				,@diCostId
				,@diCost
				,@diDescription
				,@diIsFix
				,@diWHId
				,@UserId
				,@Month1
				,@FPId
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@diNotVisible
				,@AppType
				,@LanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@diFCR
				,@diFCRI
				,@diTracing
				,@diPrdUnitId
				,@diQuantityTotal
				,@diPrdUnitIdI
				,@diPrdUnitIRateResult
				,@diPrdNickname
				)

				SET @diInsertedDocItemID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

				UPDATE @DocItemsTbl SET
					[InsertedDocItemID] = @diInsertedDocItemID
				WHERE [DocItemID] = @diDocItemID

				UPDATE @DocItemSerialsTbl SET 
					[ItemId] = @diInsertedDocItemID
				WHERE [ItemId] = @diDocItemID

				/******* Insert to lang table ********/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItems_Lang]
					(
					[DocItemID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[Description]
					,[PrdNickname]
					)
				SELECT
					@diInsertedDocItemID
					,LN.LanguageID
					,@diDescription
					,@diPrdNickname
				FROM dbo.Languages LN 
				WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
				AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1
	
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrDocItem
		INTO @diInsertedDocItemID, @diDocItemID, @diDocId, @diPrdId, @diQuantity, @diCostId, @diCost,
				@diDescription, @diIsFix, @diWHId, @diNotVisible, @diFCR, @diFCRI, @diTracing, @diPrdUnitId,
				@diQuantityTotal, @diPrdUnitIdI, @diPrdUnitIRateResult, @diPrdNickname
   
		END CLOSE CrsrDocItem DEALLOCATE CrsrDocItem



		/**************************************************************************/
		/***************************Doc Item serial inset**************************/
		/**************************************************************************/

		DECLARE CrsrDocItemSerial CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		SELECT 
			[InsertedDocItemSerialID], [DocItemSerialID],[ItemId],[Serial],[StatusId],[Description],[IsFix],
			[WHId],[SerialId],[IsAutoBarcode],[tblTmpBarcodeId],[ExpDate],[ExpDatePersian],[InfoI],[InfoII],
			[InfoIII],[InfoIV],[InfoV],[InfoVI],[RootId], [Tracing]
		FROM @DocItemSerialsTbl OPEN CrsrDocItemSerial FETCH NEXT
		FROM CrsrDocItemSerial INTO @disInsertedDocItemSerialID, @disDocItemSerialID, @disItemId, @disSerial, @disStatusId,
							@disDescription, @disIsFix, @disWHId, @disSerialId, @disIsAutoBarcode, @distblTmpBarcodeId, @disExpDate,
							@disExpDatePersian, @disInfoI, @disInfoII, @disInfoIII, @disInfoIV, @disInfoV, @disInfoVI, @disRootId,
							@disTracing
		WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0 BEGIN 

			IF(ISNULL(@disExpDatePersian, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				SET @disExpDate = dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@disExpDatePersian, NULL)
			END

			IF(@OprType = 2)
			BEGIN
				DELETE @DuplicateSerialTmpTbl
				SET @DupDocNumber = NULL

				SET @disPrdId = (SELECT DI.[PrdId] FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI WHERE [DocItemID] = @disItemId)

				INSERT INTO @DuplicateSerialTmpTbl
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicatePrdSerial]
					@OprId = @OprId,
					@FPId = @FPId,
					@WHID = @disWHId,
					@PrdId = @disPrdId,
					@Serial = @disSerial,
					@CurrId = 0,
					@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
					@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
					@DocItemSerialID = @disSerialId

				SELECT TOP 1
					@DupDocNumber = [DocNumber]
				FROM @DuplicateSerialTmpTbl

				IF(ISNULL(@DupDocNumber, 0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Serial=Dup|^|DocNumber=' + CAST(@DupDocNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)) + 
						'|^|Serial=' + @disSerial
					GOTO EndOfScript
				END
			END
					  
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
			   ([ItemId]
			   ,[Serial]
			   ,[StatusId]
			   ,[Description]
			   ,[IsFix]
			   ,[WHId]
			   ,[UserId]
			   ,[Month1]
			   ,[FPId]
			   ,[RegisterDate]
			   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[SerialId]
			   ,[IsAutoBarcode]
			   ,[tblTmpBarcodeId]
			   ,[ExpDate]
			   ,[ExpDatePersian]
			   ,[AppType]
			   ,[InfoI]
			   ,[InfoII]
			   ,[InfoIII]
			   ,[InfoIV]
			   ,[InfoV]
			   ,[InfoVI]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
			   ,[RootId])
			VALUES
			   (@disItemId
			   ,@disSerial
			   ,@disStatusId
			   ,@disDescription
			   ,@disIsFix
			   ,@disWHId
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@Month1
			   ,@FPId
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@disSerialId
			   ,@disIsAutoBarcode
			   ,@distblTmpBarcodeId
			   ,@disExpDate
			   ,@disExpDatePersian
			   ,@AppType
			   ,@disInfoI
			   ,@disInfoII
			   ,@disInfoIII
			   ,@disInfoIV
			   ,@disInfoV
			   ,@disInfoVI
			   ,@DefaultLanguageID
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@disRootId
			   )

			SET @disInsertedDocItemSerialID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

			UPDATE @DocItemSerialsTbl SET
				[InsertedDocItemSerialID] = @disInsertedDocItemSerialID
			WHERE [DocItemSerialID] = @disDocItemSerialID
			AND [ItemId] = @disItemId

			/******* Insert to lang table ********/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials_Lang]
				(
				[DocItemSerialID]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[Description]
				,[InfoI]
				,[InfoII]
				,[InfoIII]
				,[InfoIV]
				,[InfoV]
				,[InfoVI]
				)
			SELECT
				@disInsertedDocItemSerialID
				,LN.LanguageID
				,@disDescription
				,@disInfoI
				,@disInfoII
				,@disInfoIII
				,@disInfoIV
				,@disInfoV
				,@disInfoVI
			FROM dbo.Languages LN
			WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1
			/******* Update docitem serial info ********/
			IF(@OprType = 2 OR @OprType = 3)
			BEGIN
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspUpdateDocItemSerialInfoById]
					@DocItemSerialId = @disInsertedDocItemSerialID,
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@Month1 = @Month1,
					@FPId = @FPId,
					@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
					@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
					@AppType = @AppType
			END
			/******* Add tracing code from serial ********/
			IF(@OprType = 2 OR @OprType = 3)
			BEGIN
				IF(ISNULL(@SysAddTracingCodeFromSerial, 0) = 1)
				BEGIN
					IF(ISNULL(@disTracing, '') <> '')
					BEGIN
						EXEC [dbo].[MobspUpdateDocItemTracing]
							@DocItemID = @disItemId,
							@Tracing = @disTracing,
							@UserId = @UserId,
							@Month1 = @Month1,
							@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
							@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
							@AppType = @AppType
					END
				END
			END


					  
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrDocItemSerial INTO @disInsertedDocItemSerialID, @disDocItemSerialID, @disItemId, @disSerial,
							@disStatusId,@disDescription, @disIsFix, @disWHId, @disSerialId, @disIsAutoBarcode, @distblTmpBarcodeId,
							@disExpDate, @disExpDatePersian,
							@disInfoI, @disInfoII, @disInfoIII, @disInfoIV, @disInfoV, @disInfoVI, @disRootId, @disTracing
						  END CLOSE CrsrDocItemSerial DEALLOCATE CrsrDocItemSerial


	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Error'
	END CATCH


	EndOfScript:
		PRINT 'EndOfScript:'

	IF((ISNULL(@ResErrorMessage, '') <> ''))
	BEGIN
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
		SET @ResInsertedDocId = NULL
		SET @ResInsertedDocItemIds = NULL
		SET @ResInsertedDocItemSerialIds = NULL
		SELECT 0
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
		SET @ResInsertedDocId = @InsertedDocId
		SET @ResInsertedDocItemIds = 
			(SELECT [DocItemID] AS [Id], [InsertedDocItemID] AS [InsertId] FROM @DocItemsTbl FOR JSON PATH)
		SET @ResInsertedDocItemSerialIds = 
			(SELECT [DocItemSerialID] AS [Id], [InsertedDocItemSerialID] AS [InsertId] FROM @DocItemSerialsTbl FOR JSON PATH)
		SELECT 1
	END

/**/
END








-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[MobspInsertPreOrderItemFromLinkedPrd]
-- ==================================================






/*AI14000204*/
/*AI14000221- Modify Quantity*/
/*AI14000310- Show currencyPrice when price is zero and currency in header is not equal price currency and Insert duplicate product*/
/*AI14010706 - Add LinkedPrdAddDiscount - Add parameter - Add field*/
/*AI14020627 - Add PrdNickname - Add pordIValidityExpireDate - Add tax - Change Ceiling to Round*/
/*AI14020912 - Set discount percent by cmpGroupIIId and prdGroupId - Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspInsertPreOrderItemFromLinkedPrd]
(		
/*
	EXEC MobspInsertPreOrderItemFromLinkedPrd 
	@ProductId = 7927,
	@PreOrderId = 2685, 
	@ProductPriceGroupId = 1,
	@PordIWHId  = 2,
	@UserId = 3,
	@FPId = 1,
	@Month1 = 12,
	@RegisterDate = '2021-03-10',
	@RegisterDatePersian = '13991220',
	@AppType = 'dasti',
	@LanguageID = 1065,
	@LinkedPrdIds = NULL
*/
@ProductId INT,
@PreOrderId INT, 
@ProductPriceGroupId INT,
@PordIWHId INT,
@UserId INT,
@FPId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT,
@LinkedPrdIds NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, /*AI14010706- Add LinkedPrdAddDiscount and parameter*/
@PreOrderItemId INT = NULL/*AI14020627- Add tax*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*-------------------------- Begin define variables -----------------------*/
DECLARE @LinkedProductId INT,@LinkedPrdId INT,
@LinkedPrdQuantity FLOAT,@LinkedPrdNotVisible BIT,
@LinkedPrdNotInSum BIT,@LinkedPrdAddQuantity BIT,
@LinkedPrdAddPrice BIT,
@Price NUMERIC(18,2),
@TotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2),
@PrdDescription NVARCHAR(MAX),/*AI14010425- Add length nvarchar to max*/
@Discount NUMERIC(18,2),
@DiscountPrecent NUMERIC(18,2),
@PordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2),
@PordIDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2),			
@prdUnitId INT,@PordItemId INT,
@FCR FLOAT,@FCRI FLOAT,@Quantity FLOAT,
@PordIOrderView INT,
@PordCurrencyUnitId INT,
@MainPordIQuantity FLOAT, /*AI14000221- Modify Quantity*/
@LinkedPrdAddDiscount BIT,/*AI14010706- Add LinkedPrdAddDiscount and parameter*/
@PrdColorId INT/*AI14010706- Add PrdColorId*/
/*AI14010706- Add PrdUnitIdI and other fields*/
,@PrdUnitIdI INT,@PrdUnitIRate FLOAT,
@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT,
@PrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT
/*AI14010706- End*/
,@PrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL/*AI14020627- Add PrdNickname*/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
SET @PordCurrencyUnitId = (SELECT [pordCurrencyUnitId] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId)
/*AI14000221- Modify Quantity*/
SET @MainPordIQuantity = (SELECT TOP 1 pordIQuantity FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] 
						  WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId 
						  AND pordIPrdId = @ProductId
						  AND pordIWHId = @PordIWHId
						  AND pordIPrdPriceGroupID = @ProductPriceGroupId)
/*AI14000221- End*/

/*AI14020627- Add pordIValidityExpireDate*/
DECLARE @PordDateTime DATETIME
/*SET @PordDateTime = (SELECT pordDateTime FROM dbo.WSPreOrder WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId) AI14020912*/
/*AI14020627- End*/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*AI14020912- Set discount percent by cmpGroupIIId and prdGroupId*/
DECLARE @CompanyId INT = NULL,@FullDiscountPerWithRateInAdd  BIT = NULL

SELECT TOP(1)
	@PordDateTime = pordDateTime,
	@CompanyId = pordCompanyId
FROM dbo.WSPreOrder 
WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId

SET @FullDiscountPerWithRateInAdd = (SELECT TOP(1) FullDiscountPerWithRateInAdd FROm [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
/*AI14020912- End*/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*AI14020627- Add tax*/
DECLARE @pordITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@pordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@pordICalculateTax BIT = NULL,@pordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@cuUnitIsDecimal BIT

SELECT TOP(1) 
	@pordITaxIndex = pordITaxIndex,
	@pordICalculateTax = pordICalculateTax
FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
WHERE PreOrderItemID = ISNULL(@PreOrderItemId,0)

SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @PordCurrencyUnitId)
/*AI14020627- End*/
/*-------------------------- End define variables --------------------------*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*AI14010706- Add LinkedPrdAddDiscount and parameter*/
IF(ISNULL(@LinkedPrdIds,'') ='')
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @LinkedPrdIds = (STUFF((SELECT ',' + CAST(LP.[LinkedProductId] AS NVARCHAR(200)) 		
						FROM  [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts] LP
						/*WHERE ISNULL([LinkedPrdIsSelect],0) = 1*//*This part use in insert| edit item in pord,ord,bord, because not set LinkedPrdIsSelect*/
						FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))
/**/
END
/*AI14010706- End*/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
BEGIN TRY	
/*--------------------------------------*/
	BEGIN TRANSACTION
	/*--------------------------------------*/
	DECLARE Crsr_Add_Item CURSOR LOCAL FOR	
		SELECT 
			 [LinkedProductId]
			/*,[LinkedPrdId]	*/
			/*AI14000310- Insert duplicate product*/		
			,(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
				   THEN LinkedPrdId
				   ELSE PrdId									
			 END) [LinkedPrdId]
			 /*AI14000310- End*/
			,[LinkedPrdQuantity]
			,[LinkedPrdNotVisible]
			,[LinkedPrdNotInSum]
			,[LinkedPrdAddQuantity]
			,[LinkedPrdAddPrice]
			,[LinkedPrdAddDiscount]/*AI14010706- Add LinkedPrdAddDiscount and parameter*/
		FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
		WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)
		/*AI14010706- Add LinkedPrdAddDiscount and parameter*/
		AND [LinkedProductId] IN (SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@LinkedPrdIds,','))
		ORDER BY [OrderView]
		/*AI14010706- End*/

		/*AI14000310- Insert duplicate product*/
		/*AND [LinkedPrdId] NOT IN (SELECT pordIPrdId 
								  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] 
								  WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)*/
		/*AI14000310- End*/	
						
	OPEN Crsr_Add_Item
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @LinkedProductId,@LinkedPrdId,
									   @LinkedPrdQuantity,@LinkedPrdNotVisible,
									   @LinkedPrdNotInSum,@LinkedPrdAddQuantity,
									   @LinkedPrdAddPrice,@LinkedPrdAddDiscount/*AI14010706- Add LinkedPrdAddDiscount and parameter*/
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		SET @PordItemId = NULL				
		SET @PrdDescription = NULL		
		SET @prdUnitId = NULL		
		SET @FCR = NULL
		SET @FCRI = NULL
		SET @Quantity = NULL
		SET @Price = NULL
		SET @Discount = NULL
		SET @DiscountPrecent = NULL
		SET @PordIPriceSum = NULL
		SET @PordIDiscountSum = NULL
		SET @TotalPrice = NULL
		SET @PordIOrderView = NULL		
		SET @PrdColorId = NULL/*AI14010706- Add PrdColorId*/
		/*AI14010706- Add PrdUnitIdI and other fields*/
		SET @PrdUnitIdI = NULL
		SET @PrdUnitIRate = NULL
		SET @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = NULL
		SET @PrdQtyNoFCR = NULL
		SET @PrdUnitIRateResult = NULL
		/*AI14010706- End*/
		SET @PrdNickname = NULL/*AI14020627- Add PrdNickname*/
		/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*AI14020627- Add tax*/
		SET @pordITax = NULL
		SET @pordITaxSum = NULL		
		/*AI14020627- End*/
		/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*AI14020912- Set discount percent by cmpGroupIIId and prdGroupId*/
		DECLARE @CmpGroupIIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
		IF(ISNULL(@FullDiscountPerWithRateInAdd,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*-----------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @Tmp_CmpGroupIIRate TABLE(
				PrdGroupId INT,
				SGCompanyGroupIIRateJson NVARCHAR(MAX),
				Rate FLOAT,
				cmpGroupIdII INT,
				CmpGroupIIRate FLOAT
			)
			DELETE FROM @Tmp_CmpGroupIIRate
			/**/
			INSERT INTO @Tmp_CmpGroupIIRate
				(PrdGroupId,
				SGCompanyGroupIIRateJson,
				Rate,
				cmpGroupIdII,
				CmpGroupIIRate)
			EXEC [dbo].[WSspReturnCmpGroupIIRateFromPrdGroup]
				@CompanyId = @CompanyId,
				@ProductId = @LinkedPrdId
			/*-------*/
			SET @CmpGroupIIDiscountPercent = CAST(((SELECT TOP(1) ISNULL([Rate],CmpGroupIIRate) FROM @Tmp_CmpGroupIIRate)) AS NUMERIC(18,2))
			/*-----------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END		
		/*AI14020912- End*/
		/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*-------------- Set variables -------------------*/		
		SELECT 
			@PrdDescription = PrdDescription,
			@prdUnitId = prdUnitId,
			@FCR = (SELECT FCR FROM [dbo].[WSProductUnits] WHERE ProductUnitId = prdUnitId),
			@FCRI = (SELECT FCRI FROM [dbo].[WSProductUnits] WHERE ProductUnitId = prdUnitId),
			@PrdColorId = PrdColorId,/*AI14010706- Add PrdColorId*/			
			/*AI14010706- Add PrdUnitIdI and other fields*/
			@PrdUnitIdI = [PrdUnitIdI],
			@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
			@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
			@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR],
			/*AI14010706- End*/
			@PrdNickname = [PrdNickname]/*AI14020627- Add PrdNickname*/
		FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
		WHERE ProductId = @LinkedPrdId		
		/**/

		SET @Quantity = ISNULL(@LinkedPrdQuantity,1)/*AI14010706- Add LinkedPrdAddDiscount and parameter*/		
		IF(ISNULL(@LinkedPrdAddQuantity,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*SET @Quantity = ISNULL(@LinkedPrdQuantity,1)*/
			SET @Quantity = ISNULL(@Quantity,1) * ISNULL(@MainPordIQuantity,1)/*AI14010706- Add LinkedPrdAddDiscount and parameter*/
		/**/
		END
		
		/*SET @Quantity = ISNULL(@Quantity,1) * ISNULL(@MainPordIQuantity,1)*//*AI14000221- Modify Quantity*/ 

		/*AI14010706- Add PrdUnitIdI and other fields*/
		DECLARE @FCRExtra FLOAT = NULL,@FCRExtraI FLOAT = NULL,@QtySum FLOAT = NULL
		SET @FCRExtra = @FCR
		SET @FCRExtraI = @FCRI
		IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @FCRExtra = 1
			SET @FCRExtraI = 1
		/**/
		END
		/*----------------------------------------------------*/
		SET @QtySum = ISNULL(@Quantity,1) * ISNULL(@FCRExtra,1) * ISNULL(@FCRExtraI,1)
		IF(/*ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto,0) = 1 AND*/ ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0) /*AI14020912- Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*SET @PrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@QtySum,1)*/
			SET @PrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@Quantity,1)/*AI14010706- Neutralize PrdQtyNoFCR and FRCS in PrdUnitIRateResult*/

			IF(ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @PrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@PrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
			/**/
			END
		/**/
		END
		/*AI14010706- End*/



		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@LinkedPrdAddPrice,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Price = ISNULL(((SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL(Price, 0) 
								  FROM dbo.WSProductPrice 
								  WHERE PrdId = @LinkedPrdId 
								  AND ProductPriceGroupId = @ProductPriceGroupId 
								  ORDER BY DocDateTime DESC)),0)
			/*AI14000310- Show currencyPrice when price is zero and currency in header is not equal price currency*/
			DECLARE @CurrencyPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@CurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,@CurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL
			SET @CurrencyPrice = ISNULL(((SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL(CurrencyPrice, 0) 
								  FROM dbo.WSProductPrice 
								  WHERE PrdId = @LinkedPrdId 
								  AND ProductPriceGroupId = @ProductPriceGroupId 
								  ORDER BY DocDateTime DESC)),0)

			SET @CurrencyUnitId = ISNULL(((SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitId] 
								  FROM dbo.WSProductPrice 
								  WHERE PrdId = @LinkedPrdId 
								  AND ProductPriceGroupId = @ProductPriceGroupId 
								  ORDER BY DocDateTime DESC)),0)
			
			SET @CurrencyRate = ISNULL(((SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyRate] 
															  FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnitRate] CUR
															  WHERE ISNULL(CUR.[SrcCurrencyUnitId],0) = ISNULL(PP.CurrencyUnitId,0)
															  AND ISNULL(CUR.[DestCurrencyUnitId],0) = ISNULL(@PordCurrencyUnitId,0)
															  ORDER BY [RateDate] DESC),0) 
								 FROM dbo.WSProductPrice PP
								 WHERE PrdId = @LinkedPrdId 
								 AND ProductPriceGroupId = @ProductPriceGroupId 
								 ORDER BY DocDateTime DESC)),0)
			/*-------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@Price,0) = 0 AND  ISNULL(@CurrencyPrice,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@CurrencyRate,0) <> 0 AND
				(ISNULL(@PordCurrencyUnitId,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@CurrencyUnitId,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@PordCurrencyUnitId,0) <> ISNULL(@CurrencyUnitId,0)))
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Price = ISNULL(@CurrencyPrice,0) * ISNULL(@CurrencyRate,1)
			/**/
			END			
			/*AI14000310- End*/			
			/**/
			
			IF(ISNULL(@LinkedPrdAddDiscount,0) = 1) /*AI14010706- Add LinkedPrdAddDiscount and parameter*/	
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Discount = ISNULL(((SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL(Discount, 0) 
								  FROM dbo.WSProductPrice 
								  WHERE PrdId = @LinkedPrdId 
								  AND ProductPriceGroupId = @ProductPriceGroupId 
								  ORDER BY DocDateTime DESC)),0)
				/**/
				SET @DiscountPrecent = ISNULL(((SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL(DiscountPrecent, 0) 
									  FROM dbo.WSProductPrice 
									  WHERE PrdId = @LinkedPrdId 
									  AND ProductPriceGroupId = @ProductPriceGroupId 
									  ORDER BY DocDateTime DESC)),0)

				/*AI14020912- Set discount percent by cmpGroupIIId and prdGroupId*/
				IF(@CmpGroupIIDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @Discount = NULL
					SET @DiscountPrecent = @CmpGroupIIDiscountPercent
				/**/
				END
				/*AI14020912- End*/	
			/**/
			END						
			/**/
			DECLARE @TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, @TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = 0
			/**/
			/*SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@Quantity,1) * ISNULL(@FCR,1) * ISNULL(@FCRI,1)*/
			SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@QtySum,1)/*AI14010706- Add PrdUnitIdI and other fields*/
			/*SET @TmpII = ISNULL(@Price,0) - ISNULL(@Discount,0)*/

			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountPrecent,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @PordIDiscountSum = ((ISNULL(@Price,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)) * ISNULL(@DiscountPrecent,0)) / 100
				SET @Discount = (ISNULL(@Price,0) * ISNULL(@DiscountPrecent,0)) / 100
			/**/
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @PordIDiscountSum = (ISNULL(@Discount,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
			/**/
			END

			SET @TmpII = ISNULL(@Price,0) - ISNULL(@Discount,0)
			SET @PordIPriceSum = (ISNULL(@TmpII,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
			/*SET @TotalPrice = @PordIPriceSum		*/

			/*AI14020627- Add tax*/
			IF(ISNULL(@pordITaxIndex,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @pordITaxSum = (ISNULL(@PordIPriceSum,0) * ISNULL(@pordITaxIndex,0)) / 100	
				SET @pordITax = ISNULL(@pordITaxSum,0) / ISNULL(@TmpI,0)
			/**/
			END
			SET @TotalPrice = @PordIPriceSum + ISNULL(@pordITaxSum,0)
			IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
				/*AI14020627-Change Ceiling to Round*/
				SET @Price = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@Price))
				SET @pordIDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordIDiscountSum))
				SET @Discount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@Discount))
				SET @pordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordITaxSum))
				SET @pordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordITax))			
				SET @pordIPriceSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordIPriceSum))
				SET @TotalPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@TotalPrice))
				/*AI14020627- End*/
			END
			/*AI14020627- End*/
		/**/
		END		
		/**/				
		DECLARE @ItemCount INT = NULL
		SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
		SET @PordIOrderView = (@ItemCount + 1) + (@ItemCount * 4 ) - 1
		/*-------------------------------------------*/	
		/*----------------------------------------*/
		/*AI14020627- Add pordIValidityExpireDate*/
		DECLARE @pordIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL
		,@PrdValidityDay INT = NULL,@PrdValidityTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,@IsCalcValidityExpireDate BIT = NULL
		,@PrdValidityTimeHour INT = NULL,@PrdValidityTimeMinute INT = NULL
		SELECT  
			 @PrdValidityDay = [PrdValidityDay]
			,@PrdValidityTime = [PrdValidityTime]      
		FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
		WHERE [ProductID] = @LinkedPrdId
		AND ISNULL([PrdValidity],0) = 1

		IF((@PrdValidityDay IS NOT NULL))
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
		/**/
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@PrdValidityTime,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
			SET @PrdValidityTimeHour = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,1,2)) AS INT)
			SET @PrdValidityTimeMinute = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,4,2)) AS INT)
		/**/
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@IsCalcValidityExpireDate,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @pordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(DAY, ISNULL(@PrdValidityDay,0), @PordDateTime)
			SET @pordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(HOUR, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeHour,0), @pordIValidityExpireDate)
			SET @pordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(MINUTE, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeMinute,0), @pordIValidityExpireDate)
			SET @pordIValidityExpireDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](@pordIValidityExpireDate))
		/**/
		END		
		/*AI14020627- End*/
		/*----------------------------------------*/


		/*Insert WSPreOrderItems*/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
			([PreOrderId]
			,[pordIPrdId]
			,[pordIQuantity]
			,[pordIPrice]
			,[pordITotalPrice]
			,[pordIDescription]
			,[UserId]
			,[FPId]
			,[Month1]
			,[RegisterDate]
			,[RegisterDatePersian]
			,[pordIDiscount]
			,[pordIDiscountPercent]
			,[pordIPrdUnitId]
			,[pordICurrencyUnitId]
			,[pordIOrderView]
			,[pordIFCR]
			,[pordINotVisible]
			,[pordIFCRI]
			,[pordINotInSum]
			,[pordIWHId]
			,[AppType]
			,[LanguageID]
			,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			,[FirstRegisterDate]
			,[pordIPrdPriceGroupID]
			,[pordIPriceSum]
			,[pordIDiscountSum]
			,[pordIPrdColorId]/*AI14010706- Add PrdColorId*/
			/*AI14010706- Add PrdUnitIdI and other fields*/
			,[pordIPrdUnitIdI]
			,[pordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
			/*AI14010706- End*/
			,[pordIPrdNickname]/*AI14020627- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*AI14020627- Add pordIValidityExpireDate*/
			,[pordIValidityExpireDate]
			,[pordIValidityExpireDatePersian]		
			/*AI14020627- End*/
			/*AI14020627- Add tax*/
			,[pordITax]
			,[pordITaxIndex]
			,[pordICalculateTax]
			,[pordITaxSum]
			/*AI14020627- End*/
			)
		VALUES
			(@PreOrderId
			,@LinkedPrdId
			,ISNULL(@Quantity,1)
			,@Price
			,@TotalPrice
			,@PrdDescription
			,@UserId
			,@FPId
			,@Month1
			,@RegisterDate
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@Discount
			,@DiscountPrecent
			,@PrdUnitId
			,@PordCurrencyUnitId
			,@PordIOrderView
			,@FCR
			,@LinkedPrdNotVisible
			,@FCRI
			,@LinkedPrdNotInSum
			,@PordIWHId
			,@AppType
			,@LanguageID
			,@UserId
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@RegisterDate
			,@ProductPriceGroupId
			,@PordIPriceSum
			,@PordIDiscountSum
			,@PrdColorId/*AI14010706- Add PrdColorId*/
			/*AI14010706- Add PrdUnitIdI and other fields*/
			,@PrdUnitIdI
			,@PrdUnitIRateResult
			/*AI14010706- End*/
			,@PrdNickname/*AI14020627- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*AI14020627- Add pordIValidityExpireDate*/
			,@pordIValidityExpireDate
			,@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian
			/*AI14020627- End*/
			/*AI14020627- Add tax*/
			,@pordITax
			,@pordITaxIndex
			,@pordICalculateTax
			,@pordITaxSum
			/*AI14020627- End*/
			)				
		/*-------------------------------------------*/	
		SET @PordItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		/*-------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@PordItemId,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			/*Insert records in lang table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems_Lang] (
				 PreOrderItemId,
				 LanguageID,
				 pordIDescription,
				 pordIPrdNickname/*AI14020627- Add PrdNickname*/
			) 
			SELECT
				@PordItemId,
				PLN.LanguageID,
				PrdDescription,
				PrdNickname/*AI14020627- Add PrdNickname*/
			FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN 
			WHERE  PLN.ProductID = @LinkedPrdId
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			/*Insert specification*/
			DECLARE @ProductSpecificationID INT,@Specification NVARCHAR(300),
			@SpecificationI NVARCHAR(300),@SpecificationII NVARCHAR(300)						
			/*--------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE Crsr_Add_Spec CURSOR LOCAL FOR				
				SELECT
					PS.ProductSpecificationID, 
					PS.Specification,
					PS.SpecificationI,
					PS.SpecificationII					
				FROM [dbo].[WSProductSpecification] PS
				WHERE PS.PrdId = @LinkedPrdId
			OPEN Crsr_Add_Spec
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Spec INTO @ProductSpecificationID,@Specification,@SpecificationI,@SpecificationII
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN
				/*--------------------------------------------*/
				DECLARE @PordItemSpecId INT = NULL
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification]
					([PreOrderItemId]
					,[Specification]
					,[UserId]
					,[Month1]
					,[FPId]
					,[RegisterDate]
					,[RegisterDatePersian]					
					,[SpecificationI]
					,[SpecificationII]
					,[AppType]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[FirstRegisterUserId]
					,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
					,[FirstRegisterDate])
				VALUES
					(@PordItemId
					,@Specification
					,@UserId
					,@Month1
					,@FPId
					,@RegisterDate
					,@RegisterDatePersian					
					,@SpecificationI
					,@SpecificationII
					,@AppType
					,@LanguageID
					,@UserId
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDate)					
				SET @PordItemSpecId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
				/*Insert records in lang table*/
				IF(ISNULL(@PordItemSpecId,0) > 0 )
				BEGIN
				/**/
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification_Lang] (
						 PreOrderItemSpcflID
						,LanguageID
						,Specification	
						,SpecificationI
						,SpecificationII			    			  
					) 
					SELECT
						@PordItemSpecId,
						PSLN.LanguageID,
						PSLN.Specification,
						PSLN.SpecificationI,
						PSLN.SpecificationII
					FROM dbo.WSProductSpecification_Lang PSLN 
					WHERE PSLN.ProductSpecificationID = @ProductSpecificationID
				/**/
				END
				
				/*--------------------------------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Spec INTO @ProductSpecificationID,@Specification,@SpecificationI,@SpecificationII
			END
			CLOSE Crsr_Add_Spec
			DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Spec					
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @LinkedProductId,@LinkedPrdId,
										   @LinkedPrdQuantity,@LinkedPrdNotVisible,
										   @LinkedPrdNotInSum,@LinkedPrdAddQuantity,
										   @LinkedPrdAddPrice,@LinkedPrdAddDiscount/*AI14010706- Add LinkedPrdAddDiscount and parameter*/
	END
	CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item
	DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item

	/*-------------------------------*/
	SELECT 1 AS Result		
	COMMIT
/*--------------------------------------*/
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
    /*CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item
	DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item
	CLOSE Crsr_Add_Spec
	DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Spec*/
	SELECT 0 AS Result	
END CATCH
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/		
END

-- ==================================================






/*RZ970917*/
/*AI14020525 - Apply web changes*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspReturnPrdQuantity]
(		
/* 
	EXEC MobspReturnPrdQuantity 7927, 2, 1, 0, 1065, 1065 ,null,null,N'AA-10-20'	
*/
@PrdId INT, 
@FPId INT, 
@WHId INT, 
@CurrId INT, 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@FCR FLOAT = NULL,
@FCRI FLOAT = NULL,
@Tracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
@QuantityRemain FLOAT OUTPUT
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @QuantityIn FLOAT, 
	@QuantityOut FLOAT, 
	@RemainCount FLOAT,
	@PrdName NVARCHAR(1000), 
	@NetPreOrderCount FLOAT,
	@QuantityInAllWH FLOAT, 
	@QuantityOutAllWH FLOAT, 
	@RemainCountAllWH FLOAT,
	@RemainCurrAndAll VARCHAR(300),
	@TracingQuantityIn FLOAT,
	@TracingQuantityOut FLOAT,
	@TracingRemainCount FLOAT
	
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/*---------------------------------------*/
SELECT 
	@QuantityIn = IsNull(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0), 
	@PrdName = (SELECT PrdName FROM dbo.WSProducts WHERE ProductID = DI.PrdId)
FROM dbo.WSDoc D 
INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)
WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType IN (1,3) )
And DI.PrdId = @PrdId 
And D.WHId = @WHId
AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
GROUP BY DI.PrdId
/*-----*/
SELECT 
	@QuantityOut = IsNull(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0)
FROM dbo.WSDoc D 
INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)
WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 2)/*2--@OprId*/
And DI.PrdId = @PrdId
And D.WHId = @WHId
AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
OR ( 
	(OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 3)) 
	And DI.PrdId = @PrdId
	And D.WHIdI = @WHId
	AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
)
GROUP BY DI.PrdId
/*----*/
SELECT 
	@QuantityInAllWH = IsNull(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0)
FROM dbo.WSDoc D 
INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)
WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType IN (1,3) )
And DI.PrdId = @PrdId 
AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
GROUP BY DI.PrdId
/*-----*/
SELECT 
	@QuantityOutAllWH = IsNull(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0)
FROM dbo.WSDoc D 
INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)
WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 2)/*2--@OprId*/
And DI.PrdId = @PrdId
AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
OR ( 
	(OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 3)) 
	And DI.PrdId = @PrdId
	AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
)
GROUP BY DI.PrdId
/*----*/
SELECT 
	@NetPreOrderCount = ISNULL(SUM(pordIQuantity), 0) 
FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
WHERE PreOrderId IN (
	SELECT IsNull(PreOrderID , '') 
	FROM dbo.WSPreOrder
	WHERE PreOrderID NOT IN (SELECT PreOrderID FROM dbo.WSOrder)
)
AND pordIPrdId = @PrdId
/*----*/
SET @RemainCount = IsNull(@QuantityIn, 0) - IsNull(@QuantityOut, 0)
SET @RemainCountAllWH = IsNull(@QuantityInAllWH, 0) - IsNull(@QuantityOutAllWH, 0)
SET @RemainCurrAndAll = CAST(CAST(ISNULL(@RemainCount,0) AS NUMERIC(18,3)) AS VARCHAR(100)) + '/' 
					  + CAST(CAST(ISNULL(@RemainCountAllWH,0) AS NUMERIC(18,3))  AS VARCHAR(100))
SET @RemainCurrAndAll = REPLACE(@RemainCurrAndAll,'.000','')
/*----*/
IF(ISNULL(@Tracing,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		@TracingQuantityIn = IsNull(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0)
	FROM dbo.WSDoc D 
	INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)
	WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType IN (1,3) )
	And DI.PrdId = @PrdId 
	And D.WHId = @WHId
	AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
	AND ( 
			(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(DI.[Tracing] AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1610), NCHAR(1740)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) =
			(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Tracing AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1610), NCHAR(1740)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
		)
	GROUP BY DI.PrdId
	/*----*/
	SELECT 
		@TracingQuantityOut = IsNull(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0)
	FROM dbo.WSDoc D 
	INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)
	WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 2)/*2--@OprId*/
	And DI.PrdId = @PrdId
	And D.WHId = @WHId
	AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
	AND ( 
			(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(DI.[Tracing] AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1610), NCHAR(1740)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) =
			(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Tracing AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1610), NCHAR(1740)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
		)
	OR ( 
		(OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 3)) 
		And DI.PrdId = @PrdId
		And D.WHIdI = @WHId
		AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
		AND ( 
			(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(DI.[Tracing] AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1610), NCHAR(1740)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) =
			(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Tracing AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1610), NCHAR(1740)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
		)
	)
	GROUP BY DI.PrdId
	/*----*/
	SET @TracingRemainCount = ISNULL(@TracingQuantityIn, 0) - ISNULL(@TracingQuantityOut, 0)
/**/
END
ELSE
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @TracingRemainCount = @RemainCount
/**/
END
/*----*/
SELECT
CAST(CAST(@RemainCount AS NUMERIC(18,3)) AS FLOAT)QuantityRemain,

@QuantityIn QuantityIn, @QuantityOut QuantityOut, @PrdId PrdId, @PrdName PrdName, 
	CAST(CAST(@RemainCount AS NUMERIC(18,3)) AS FLOAT)RemainCount
	, @NetPreOrderCount NetPreOrderCount, @RemainCountAllWH RemainCountAllWH, @RemainCurrAndAll RemainCurrAndAll 
	,CAST(CAST(@TracingRemainCount AS NUMERIC(18,3)) AS FLOAT)TracingRemainCount 
/*----*/

SET @QuantityRemain = ISNULL(@RemainCount,CAST('0' AS FLOAT))

END
/*--==========================================================================================*/
ELSE
/*--==========================================================================================*/
BEGIN
SELECT 
	@QuantityIn = IsNull(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0), 
	@PrdName = (SELECT PrdName FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = DI.PrdId  AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
FROM dbo.WSDoc D 
INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)
WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType IN (1,3) )
And DI.PrdId = @PrdId
And D.WHId = @WHId
AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
GROUP BY DI.PrdId
/*-----*/
SELECT 
	@QuantityOut = IsNull(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0)
FROM dbo.WSDoc D 
INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)
WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 2)/*2--@OprId*/
And DI.PrdId = @PrdId
And D.WHId = @WHId
AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
OR ( 
	(OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 3)) 
	And DI.PrdId = @PrdId
	And D.WHIdI = @WHId
	AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
)
GROUP BY DI.PrdId
/*----*/
SELECT 
	@QuantityInAllWH = IsNull(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0) 
FROM dbo.WSDoc D 
INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)
WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType IN (1,3) )
And DI.PrdId = @PrdId
AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
GROUP BY DI.PrdId
/*-----*/
SELECT 
	@QuantityOutAllWH = IsNull(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0)
FROM dbo.WSDoc D 
INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)
WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 2)/*2--@OprId*/
And DI.PrdId = @PrdId
AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
OR ( 
	(OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 3)) 
	And DI.PrdId = @PrdId
	AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
)
GROUP BY DI.PrdId
/*----*/
SELECT 
	@NetPreOrderCount = ISNULL(SUM(pordIQuantity), 0) 
FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
WHERE PreOrderId IN (
	SELECT IsNull(PreOrderID , '') 
	FROM dbo.WSPreOrder
	WHERE PreOrderID NOT IN (SELECT PreOrderID FROM dbo.WSOrder)
)
AND pordIPrdId = @PrdId
/*----*/
SET @RemainCount = IsNull(@QuantityIn, 0) - IsNull(@QuantityOut, 0)
SET @RemainCountAllWH = IsNull(@QuantityInAllWH, 0) - IsNull(@QuantityOutAllWH, 0)
SET @RemainCurrAndAll = CAST(CAST(ISNULL(@RemainCount,0) AS NUMERIC(18,3)) AS VARCHAR(100)) + '/' 
					  + CAST(CAST(ISNULL(@RemainCountAllWH,0) AS NUMERIC(18,3))  AS VARCHAR(100))
SET @RemainCurrAndAll = REPLACE(@RemainCurrAndAll,'.000','')
/*----*/
IF(ISNULL(@Tracing,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		@TracingQuantityIn = IsNull(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0)
	FROM dbo.WSDoc D 
	INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)
	WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType IN (1,3) )
	And DI.PrdId = @PrdId 
	And D.WHId = @WHId
	AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
	AND ( 
			(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(DI.[Tracing] AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1610), NCHAR(1740)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) =
			(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Tracing AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1610), NCHAR(1740)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
		)
	GROUP BY DI.PrdId
	/*----*/
	SELECT 
		@TracingQuantityOut = IsNull(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0)
	FROM dbo.WSDoc D 
	INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)
	WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 2)/*2--@OprId*/
	And DI.PrdId = @PrdId
	And D.WHId = @WHId
	AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
	AND ( 
			(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(DI.[Tracing] AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1610), NCHAR(1740)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) =
			(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Tracing AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1610), NCHAR(1740)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
		)
	OR ( 
		(OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 3)) 
		And DI.PrdId = @PrdId
		And D.WHIdI = @WHId
		AND DI.DocItemID <> @CurrId
		AND ( 
			(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(DI.[Tracing] AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1610), NCHAR(1740)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) =
			(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Tracing AS NVARCHAR(2000)),NCHAR(1610), NCHAR(1740)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
		)
	)
	GROUP BY DI.PrdId
	/*----*/
	SET @TracingRemainCount = ISNULL(@TracingQuantityIn, 0) - ISNULL(@TracingQuantityOut, 0)
/**/
END
ELSE
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @TracingRemainCount = @RemainCount
/**/
END
/*----*/

	SELECT
		CAST(CAST(@RemainCount AS NUMERIC(18,3)) AS FLOAT)QuantityRemain,

		@QuantityIn QuantityIn, @QuantityOut QuantityOut, @PrdId PrdId, @PrdName PrdName, 
	CAST(CAST(@RemainCount AS NUMERIC(18,3)) AS FLOAT)RemainCount
	, @NetPreOrderCount NetPreOrderCount, @RemainCountAllWH RemainCountAllWH, @RemainCurrAndAll RemainCurrAndAll 
	,CAST(CAST(@TracingRemainCount AS NUMERIC(18,3)) AS FLOAT)TracingRemainCount

SET @QuantityRemain = ISNULL(@RemainCount,CAST('0' AS FLOAT))

END
/**/
END
