-- [dbo].[MobspReturnPreOrderNumRef]
-- ==================================================






/*AI14000205*/
/*AI14010505 - Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspReturnPreOrderNumRef]
(	
/* 
	EXEC MobspReturnPreOrderNumRef 6156,6891,3,45888,1,1,1,1033,1065
*/
@PordCompanyId INT,
@PordProjectId INT,
@PordReferUserId INT,
@PordNumber BIGINT,
@PordNumberRevision BIGINT,
@PreOrderOprId INT = NULL,
@PordTypeId INT = NULL,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@selectedNumRef NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*------------------------ Define variabels -------------------*/
DECLARE 
@ValueForCmpName NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForCmpNameEng NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForCmpNumber NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForCmpCode NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForPrjInquiryNum NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForPrjNumR NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForPrjNumRef NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForPreOrderOpr NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForPreOrderType NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForSysNum NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForSysNumR NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForUserFName NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForUserLName NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForUserFullName NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForUserPersCode NVARCHAR(1000),
@pordNumRef NVARCHAR(150),
@pordNumRefFormula NVARCHAR(4000)
/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
SELECT TOP 1 
    @pordNumRefFormula = [pordNumRefFormula]    
FROM [dbo].[CrmNumRefSetting]
/*------------------ Check prjNumRefFormula -----------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@pordNumRefFormula,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	/*------------------------ Set variabels -------------------*/
	/*SELECT 
		@ValueForCmpName = cmpName, 
		@ValueForCmpNameEng = cmpNameEng,
		@ValueForCmpNumber = CAST(cmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)),
		@ValueForCmpCode = cmpCode			
	FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] 
	WHERE CompanyID = @PordCompanyId*/

	/*AI14010505 - Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 
			@ValueForCmpName = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
									   THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
											  ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
									   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
													ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
											FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
											WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
											)				
								 END), 
			@ValueForCmpNameEng = cmpNameEng,
			@ValueForCmpNumber = CAST(cmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)),
			@ValueForCmpCode = cmpCode			
		FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
		WHERE CompanyID = @PordCompanyId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 
			@ValueForCmpName = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
									THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
											ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
									ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
												ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
										FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
										WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
										)				
								END), 
			@ValueForCmpNameEng = cmpNameEng,
			@ValueForCmpNumber = CAST(cmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)),
			@ValueForCmpCode = cmpCode			
		FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
		WHERE CompanyID = @PordCompanyId
	/**/
	END	
	/*AI14010505- End*/

	SELECT 
		@ValueForPrjInquiryNum = prjInquiryNum, 
		@ValueForPrjNumR = CAST(prjNumR AS NVARCHAR(50)),			
		@ValueForPrjNumRef = prjNumRef
	FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] 
	WHERE ProjectID = @PordProjectId

	SELECT 
		@ValueForPreOrderOpr = [PreOrderOpr]		
	FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderOpr]
	WHERE [PreOrderOprID] = @PreOrderOprId

	SELECT 
		@ValueForPreOrderType = [PreOrderType]		
	FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderTypes]
	WHERE [PreOrderTypeId] = @PordTypeId


	SET @ValueForSysNum = @PordNumber
	SET @ValueForSysNumR =  CAST(@PordNumberRevision AS NVARCHAR(50))
	
	/*SELECT 
		@ValueForUserFName = [FName],
		@ValueForUserLName = [LName],
		@ValueForUserFullName = LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL([FName],'') + ' ' +  ISNULL([LName],''))),
		@ValueForUserPersCode = [PersCode]    
	FROM [dbo].[Users] U
	WHERE U.[UserId] = @PordReferUserId*/

	/*AI14010505 - Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 
			@ValueForUserFName = [FName],
			@ValueForUserLName = [LName],
			@ValueForUserFullName = LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL([FName],'') + ' ' +  ISNULL([LName],''))),
			@ValueForUserPersCode = [PersCode]    
		FROM [dbo].[Users] U
		WHERE U.[UserId] = @PordReferUserId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 
			@ValueForUserFName = ISNULL((SELECT [FName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''),
			@ValueForUserLName = ISNULL((SELECT [LName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''),
			@ValueForUserFullName =  LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT [FName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') 
								  + ' ' 
								  +  ISNULL((SELECT [LName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''))),
			@ValueForUserPersCode = [PersCode]    
		FROM [dbo].[Users] U
		WHERE U.[UserId] = @PordReferUserId
	/**/
	END	
	/*RZ14000616- End*/
	/*------------------------ Set NumRef ---------------------*/
	SET @pordNumRef = @pordNumRefFormula
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#CmpName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpName, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#CmpNameEng#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpNameEng, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#CmpNumber#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpNumber, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#CmpCode#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpCode, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#SysNum#]', ISNULL(@ValueForSysNum, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#SysNumR#]', ISNULL(@ValueForSysNumR, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#PrjInquiryNum#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrjInquiryNum, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#PrjNumR#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrjNumR, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#PrjNumRef#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrjNumRef, '') )

	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#PordOprId#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPreOrderOpr, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#PordTypeId#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPreOrderType, '') )

	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#UserFName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserFName, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#UserLName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserLName, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#UserFullName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserFullName, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#UserPersCode#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserPersCode, '') )	
/**/
END
/**/
SET @selectedNumRef = @pordNumRef
/**/
END







-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[MobspUpdateDoc]
-- ==================================================



/*AI114020531*/
/*GHO14030205 - Update [WSspUpdateDocItemsOrdItemId] And [WSspUpdateDocItemsBordItemId]*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspUpdateDoc]
/*
	EXEC MobspUpdateDoc
*/
(
@DocParams NVARCHAR(MAX),
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
/**/
@ResErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,
@ResUpdatedDocId INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
	BEGIN TRY
		SET @ResErrorMessage = ''

		/**************************************************************************/
		/***********************************HELPER*********************************/
		/**************************************************************************/
		DECLARE @RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8)
		SET @RegisterTime = (CONVERT(CHAR(8), @RegisterDate, 108))	

		DECLARE @DupDocNumber INT ,@actNotDupNumRef BIT, @OprType INT

		DECLARE @CheckDuplicateDocNumRefTmpTbl TABLE(
				[DocNumber] INT,
				[DocNumRef] NVARCHAR(150)
		)

		/**************************************************************************/
		/**********************************Get params******************************/
		/**************************************************************************/
		BEGIN TRY
			DECLARE
				@DocID INT
				,@OprId INT
				,@DocNumber INT
				,@DocDate NVARCHAR(8)
				,@SenderId INT
				,@SenderName NVARCHAR(500)
				,@FactorNumber NVARCHAR(50)
				,@Description NVARCHAR(4000)
				,@IsFix SMALLINT
				,@WHId INT
				,@OrderId INT
				,@BuyOrderId INT
				,@SendAddress NVARCHAR(500)
				,@DriverName NVARCHAR(100)
				,@DriverTel NVARCHAR(50)
				,@FreightName NVARCHAR(100)
				,@BijakNumber NVARCHAR(50)
				,@CarNumber NVARCHAR(50)
				,@DocDateTime DATETIME
				,@WHIdI INT
				,@CompanyId INT
				,@CompanyPersonId INT
				,@ProjectId INT
				,@cmpTitle NVARCHAR(300)
				,@DocNumRef NVARCHAR(150)
				,@IsVerified BIT
				,@VerifierId INT
				,@VerifiedRegisterDate DATETIME
				,@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8)
				,@VerifierRequestId INT
				,@VerificationWorkFlowId INT
				,@PreOrderId INT

			IF(ISNULL(@DocParams, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				SELECT
					@DocID = DJ.DocID,
					@OprId = DJ.OprId,
					@DocNumber = DJ.DocNumber,
					@DocDate = DJ.DocDate,
					@SenderId = DJ.SenderId,
					@SenderName = DJ.SenderName,
					@FactorNumber = DJ.FactorNumber,
					@Description = DJ.[Description],
					@IsFix = DJ.IsFix,
					@WHId = DJ.WHId,
					@OrderId = DJ.OrderId,
					@BuyOrderId = DJ.BuyOrderId,
					@SendAddress = DJ.SendAddress,
					@DriverName = DJ.DriverName,
					@DriverTel = DJ.DriverTel,
					@FreightName = DJ.FreightName,
					@BijakNumber = DJ.BijakNumber,
					@CarNumber = DJ.CarNumber,
					@DocDateTime = DJ.DocDateTime,
					@WHIdI = DJ.WHIdI,
					@CompanyId = DJ.CompanyId,
					@CompanyPersonId = DJ.CompanyPersonId,
					@ProjectId = DJ.ProjectId,
					@cmpTitle = DJ.cmpTitle,
					@DocNumRef = DJ.DocNumRef,
					@IsVerified = DJ.IsVerified,
					@VerifierId = DJ.VerifierId,
					@VerifiedRegisterDate = DJ.VerifiedRegisterDate,
					@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian = DJ.VerifiedRegisterDatePersian,
					@VerifierRequestId = DJ.VerifierRequestId,
					@VerificationWorkFlowId = DJ.VerificationWorkFlowId,
					@PreOrderId = DJ.PreOrderId
				FROM OPENJSON(@DocParams)
				WITH(
					DocID INT '$.DocID',
					OprId INT '$.OprId',
					DocNumber INT '$.DocNumber',
					DocDate NVARCHAR(8) '$.DocDate',
					SenderId INT '$.SenderId',
					SenderName NVARCHAR(500) '$.SenderName',
					FactorNumber NVARCHAR(50) '$.FactorNumber',
					[Description] NVARCHAR(4000) '$.Description',
					IsFix SMALLINT '$.IsFix',
					WHId INT '$.WHId',
					OrderId INT '$.OrderId',
					BuyOrderId INT '$.BuyOrderId',
					SendAddress NVARCHAR(500) '$.SendAddress',
					DriverName NVARCHAR(100) '$.DriverName',
					DriverTel NVARCHAR(50) '$.DriverTel',
					FreightName NVARCHAR(100) '$.FreightName',
					BijakNumber NVARCHAR(50) '$.BijakNumber',
					CarNumber NVARCHAR(50) '$.CarNumber',
					DocDateTime DATETIME '$.DocDateTime',
					WHIdI INT '$.WHIdI',
					CompanyId INT '$.CompanyId',
					CompanyPersonId INT '$.CompanyPersonId',
					ProjectId INT '$.ProjectId',
					cmpTitle NVARCHAR(300) '$.cmpTitle',
					DocNumRef NVARCHAR(150) '$.DocNumRef',
					IsVerified BIT '$.IsVerified',
					VerifierId INT '$.VerifierId',
					VerifiedRegisterDate DATETIME '$.VerifiedRegisterDate',
					VerifiedRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) '$.VerifiedRegisterDatePersian',
					VerifierRequestId INT '$.VerifierRequestId',
					VerificationWorkFlowId INT '$.VerificationWorkFlowId',
					PreOrderId INT '$.PreOrderId'
				) AS DJ
			END

		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
			SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'ParseData=Error'
			GOTO EndOfScript
		END CATCH

		SET @OprType = ISNULL((SELECT [OprType] FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE [DocOprID] = @OprId), 0)

		SET @DupDocNumber = (SELECT TOP 1 [DocNumber] FROM [dbo].[MobfnCheckDuplicateDocNumber](@DocNumber, @DocID, @OprId))
		IF(ISNULL(@DupDocNumber, 0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'DocNumber=Dup|^|DupDocNumber=' + CAST(@DupDocNumber AS NVARCHAR(10))
			SET @DupDocNumber = NULL
			GOTO EndOfScript
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@DocNumRef, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			
			IF(@OprType = 1)
			BEGIN
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
					@FormName = 'frmDocDataEntry',
					@ActionName = 'actNotDupNumRef',
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@HasAction = @actNotDupNumRef OUTPUT
			END
			ELSE IF(@OprType = 2)
			BEGIN
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
					@FormName = 'frmDraftDataEntry',
					@ActionName = 'actNotDupNumRef',
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@HasAction = @actNotDupNumRef OUTPUT
			END
			ELSE IF(@OprType = 3)
			BEGIN
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
					@FormName = 'frmWareHouseMovementDataEntry',
					@ActionName = 'actNotDupNumRef',
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@HasAction = @actNotDupNumRef OUTPUT
			END

			IF(@actNotDupNumRef = 1)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO @CheckDuplicateDocNumRefTmpTbl
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateDocNumRef]
					@DocNumRef = @DocNumRef,
					@CurrDocId = @DocID,
					@DocOprId = @OprId

				SELECT TOP 1 
					@DupDocNumber = [DocNumber]
				FROM @CheckDuplicateDocNumRefTmpTbl

				IF(ISNULL(@DupDocNumber, 0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'DocNumRef=Dup|^|DupDocNumber=' + CAST(@DupDocNumber AS NVARCHAR(10))
					SET @DupDocNumber = NULL
					GOTO EndOfScript
				END

			END
		END

		/**************************************************************************/
		/***********************************UPDATE*********************************/
		/**************************************************************************/


		/*GHO14030205 - Update [WSspUpdateDocItemsOrdItemId] And [WSspUpdateDocItemsBordItemId]*/
		BEGIN
			DECLARE
			@OldOrderId INT
			,@OldBuyOrderId INT

			SET @OldOrderId = ISNULL((SELECT [OrderId] FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] WHERE [DocId] = @DocId),0)

			SET @OldBuyOrderId = ISNULL((SELECT [BuyOrderId] FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] WHERE [DocId] = @DocId),0)
		END
		/*GHO14030205-End*/

		IF(ISNULL(@DocDate, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			SET @DocDateTime = dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@DocDate, @RegisterTime)
		END


		UPDATE [dbo].[WSDoc]
			SET [OprId] = @OprId
			,[DocNumber] = @DocNumber
			,[DocDate] = @DocDate
			,[SenderId] = @SenderId
			,[SenderName] = @SenderName
			,[FactorNumber] = @FactorNumber
			,[Description] = @Description
			,[IsFix] = @IsFix
			,[WHId] = @WHId
			,[UserId] = @UserId
			,[Month1] = @Month1
			,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
			,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian
			,[OrderId] = @OrderId
			,[BuyOrderId] = @BuyOrderId
			,[SendAddress] = @SendAddress
			,[DriverName] = @DriverName
			,[DriverTel] = @DriverTel
			,[FreightName] = @FreightName
			,[BijakNumber] = @BijakNumber
			,[CarNumber] = @CarNumber
			,[DocDateTime] = @DocDateTime
			,[WHIdI] = @WHIdI
			,[CompanyId] = @CompanyId
			,[CompanyPersonId] = @CompanyPersonId
			,[ProjectId] = @ProjectId
			,[cmpTitle] = @cmpTitle
			,[AppType] = @AppType
			,[DocNumRef] = @DocNumRef
			,[IsVerified] = @IsVerified
			,[VerifierId] = @VerifierId
			,[VerifiedRegisterDate] = @VerifiedRegisterDate
			,[VerifiedRegisterDatePersian] = @VerifiedRegisterDatePersian
			,[VerifierRequestId] = @VerifierRequestId
			,[VerificationWorkFlowId] = @VerificationWorkFlowId
			,[PreOrderId] = @PreOrderId
		WHERE [DocID] = @DocID

		/*********** Update lang table ***********/
		UPDATE [dbo].[WSDoc_Lang]
			SET [SenderName] = @SenderName
			,[Description] = @Description
			,[SendAddress] = @SendAddress
			,[DriverName] = @DriverName
			,[FreightName] = @FreightName
			,[cmpTitle] = @cmpTitle
		FROM dbo.Languages LN 
		WHERE [DocID] = @DocID
		AND LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
		AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1

		/*GHO14030205 - Update [WSspUpdateDocItemsOrdItemId] And [WSspUpdateDocItemsBordItemId]*/
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*--------------------UPDATE WSspUpdateDocItemsOrdItemId---------------------*/
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
		

		IF(ISNULL(@OrderId,0) <> ISNULL(@OldOrderId,0))
		BEGIN
			/**/
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
			/*---------------- Clear old ItemId ------------------*/
			UPDATE [dbo].[WSDocItems]
				SET  [UserId] = @UserId
					,[Month1] = @Month1		
					,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
					,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian
					,[AppType] = @AppType		
					,[OrderItemId] = NULL		
				WHERE [DocId] = @DocId
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
			/*---------------- Update new ItemId -----------------*/
			IF(@OrderId > 0)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSDocItems]
				SET  [OrderItemId] = (SELECT TOP(1) OI.OrderItemID
								  FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
								  WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId
								  AND OI.ordIPrdId = DI.PrdId 
						    	   	AND OI.ordIWHId = DI.WHId 
								 )
					,[UserId] = @UserId
					,[Month1] = @Month1		
					,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
					,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian
					,[AppType] = @AppType	
				FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI
				WHERE DI.DocId = @DocId
				AND ISNULL(OrderItemId,0) = 0	
				/**/
				END	   
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END

		/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*--------------------UPDATE WSspUpdateDocItemsBordItemId--------------------*/
		/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderId,0) <> ISNULL(@OldBuyOrderId,0))
		BEGIN
			/**/
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
			/*---------------- Clear old ItemId ------------------*/
			UPDATE [dbo].[WSDocItems]
			SET  [UserId] = @UserId
				,[Month1] = @Month1		
				,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
				,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian
				,[AppType] = @AppType		
				,[BuyOrderItemId] = NULL		
			WHERE [DocId] = @DocId
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
			/*---------------- Update new ItemId -----------------*/	
			IF(@BuyOrderId > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSDocItems]
				SET  
					[BuyOrderItemId] = (SELECT TOP(1) BOI.BuyOrderItemID
										FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] BOI
										WHERE BOI.BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId
										AND BOI.bordIPrdId = DI.PrdId 
										AND BOI.bordIWHId = DI.WHId 
										)
					,[UserId] = @UserId
					,[Month1] = @Month1		
					,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
					,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian
					,[AppType] = @AppType	

				FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI
				WHERE DI.DocId = @DocId
				AND ISNULL(BuyOrderItemId,0) = 0 
			/**/
			END	  
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END
		/*GHO14030205-End*/


	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Error'
	END CATCH

	EndOfScript:
		PRINT 'EndOfScript:'

	IF((ISNULL(@ResErrorMessage, '') <> ''))
	BEGIN
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
		SET @ResUpdatedDocId = NULL
		SELECT 0
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
		SET @ResUpdatedDocId = @DocID
		SELECT 1
	END



/**/
END







-- ==================================================
-- [dbo].[WSspIssueStockSupplyFromPreOrder]
-- ==================================================







/*RZ14020915*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[WSspIssueStockSupplyFromPreOrder]	
(		
/*
	declare @InsertedStockSupplyId INT = 0, @InsertedStsupNumber INT = 0
	exec WSspIssueStockSupplyFromPreOrder  	
	@PreOrderId = 3356,
	@StsupDate = '14020915',
	@StsupDateTime = '2023-12-06',
	@PordItemList = N'',	
	@UserId = 3,
	@Month1 = 9,
	@FPId = 30,
	@RegisterDate = '2023-12-06',
	@RegisterDatePersian = '14020915',
	@AppType = 'dasti',
	@LanguageID = 1033,
	@DefaultLanguageID = 1065,
	@InsertedStockSupplyId = @InsertedStockSupplyId OUTPUT,
	@InsertedStsupNumber = @InsertedStsupNumber OUTPUT
	
	select @InsertedStockSupplyId,@InsertedStsupNumber
*/
@PreOrderId INT,
@StsupDate NVARCHAR(8),
@StsupDateTime DATETIME,
@PordItemList NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@FPId INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50),
@LanguageID INT,
@DefaultLanguageID INT,
@InsertedStockSupplyId INT OUTPUT,
@InsertedStsupNumber BIGINT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*---------------------------- Set PordItemList -------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordItemList,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/		
	/*---------------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [StockSupplyId] FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply] WHERE [stsupPreOrderId] = @PreOrderID ),0) = 0) /*PreOrder not have any stockSupply*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @PordItemList = (SELECT 
								PreOrderItemID [Id],			
								pordIQuantity [Value]			
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
							WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID								
							AND pordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
											      FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
											      WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )							
							ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	
							FOR JSON PATH)
	/**/
	END
	ELSE /*IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [StockSupplyId] FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply] WHERE [stsupPreOrderId] = @PreOrderID ),0) = 0)*/ /*PreOrder have stockSupply*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @PordItemList = (SELECT 
								PreOrderItemID [Id],			
								(ISNULL(pordIQuantity,0) - 
								 ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL([stsupIQuantity],0)) 
										FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] SI
										WHERE SI.stsupIPreOrderId = POI.PreOrderId
										AND SI.stsupIPreOrderItemId = POI.PreOrderItemID										
										AND SI.stsupIPrdId = pordIPrdId
										AND ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(ST.stsupIsCanceled,0) FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply] ST WHERE ST.StockSupplyId = SI.StockSupplyId),0) = 0),0)
	
								) [Value]				
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems POI
							WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID								
							AND pordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
											      FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
											      WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )							
							ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	
							FOR JSON PATH)
	/**/
	END
	/*---------------------------------------*/
/**/
END
/*------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @FinalResult INT = NULL
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*Check preorder have stockSupply*/
DECLARE @TmpCheckPordHaveStockSupply TABLE(
		[Result] INT
)

INSERT INTO @TmpCheckPordHaveStockSupply ([Result])
EXEC [dbo].[WSspCheckPreOrderHaveStockSupply] @PreOrderId = @PreOrderId

IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1)[Result] FROM @TmpCheckPordHaveStockSupply),0) = 1)
BEGIN
	SET @FinalResult = -1	
END
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @StockSupplyOprId INT,@StockSupplyId INT,@stsupNumber BIGINT,
@stsupVerificationWorkFlowId INT,@stsupVerifierRequestId INT,
@stsupCompanyId INT,@stsupCompanyPersonId INT,@stsupProjectId INT
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
SET @StockSupplyOprId = 1

SELECT TOP (1) 
     @stsupVerifierRequestId = [stsupDefVerifierRequestId]
    ,@stsupVerificationWorkFlowId = [stsupDefVerificationWorkFlowId]
FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]
/*---------------------*/
SELECT TOP(1)
	@stsupCompanyId = pordCompanyId,
	@stsupCompanyPersonId = pordCompanyPersonId,
	@stsupProjectId = pordProjectId		
FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] PO
WHERE PO.PreOrderID = @PreOrderId
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*--********************MAINTRAN*/
DECLARE @ErrorNumber INT, @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50)
BEGIN TRANSACTION MainIssueStsupFromPord
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION START ------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/	
IF(ISNULL(@FinalResult,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@PordItemList,'') <>'')
BEGIN
/**/	
	/*--------------------------------------------*/		
	SET @StockSupplyId = NULL
	SET @InsertedStockSupplyId = NULL
	SET @stsupNumber = NULL
	SET @InsertedStsupNumber = NULL
	/*---------------- StockSupply Number ----------------*/
	SELECT @stsupNumber =  (SELECT [dbo].[WSfnReturnStockSupplyNumber](@StockSupplyOprId))
	SET @InsertedStsupNumber = @stsupNumber
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*--------------------- tran1 ----------------*/
	BEGIN TRAN
	/*--------------------------------------------*/	
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSStockSupply]
			([StockSupplyOprId]
			,[stsupReferUserId]
			,[stsupNumber]
			,[stsupDateTime]
			,[stsupDate]
			,[stsupCompanyId]
			,[stsupCompanyPersonId]
			,[stsupProjectId]
			,[stsupIsFixed]				
			,[stsupVerifierRequestId]				
			,[stsupVerificationWorkFlowId]				
			,[stsupPreOrderId]
			,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			,[FirstRegisterDate]
			,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			,[UserId]
			,[FPId]
			,[Month1]
			,[RegisterDate]
			,[RegisterDatePersian]				
			,[AppType]
			,[LanguageID])
		VALUES
			(@StockSupplyOprId
			,@UserId
			,@stsupNumber
			,@stsupDateTime
			,@stsupDate
			,@stsupCompanyId
			,@stsupCompanyPersonId
			,@stsupProjectId
			,1 /*stsupIsFixed*/
			,@stsupVerifierRequestId				
			,@stsupVerificationWorkFlowId
			,@PreOrderId
			,@UserId
			,@RegisterDate
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@UserId
			,@FPId
			,@Month1
			,@RegisterDate
			,@RegisterDatePersian				
			,@AppType
			,@DefaultLanguageID)
				
		SELECT @StockSupplyId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

		IF(ISNULL(@StockSupplyId,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*-------------------------------------------------*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSStockSupply_Lang]
				([StockSupplyId]
				,[LanguageID])
			SELECT
					@StockSupplyId
				,L.LanguageID
			FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
			WHERE ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
			AND LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			/*-------------------------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*------------------- tran1 ------------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN 
		SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSStockSupply' 
		SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
	ROLLBACK TRAN 
	END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	SET @InsertedStockSupplyId = @StockSupplyId
	/*------------------- tran2 --------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@StockSupplyId,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/			
		DECLARE @PreOrderItemID INT = NULL, @pordIPrdId INT = NULL, @pordIQuantity float = NULL 
		,@pordIDescription NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, @pordIWHId INT = NULL
		,@pordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@pordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL
		,@pordITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL
		,@pordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL
		,@pordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL
		,@pordIPrdUnitIdI INT = NULL,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL
		,@PrdUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT  = NULL
		, @TmpI FLOAT = NULL
		,@pordIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL
		,@pordiQuantityDesc NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL
		,@pordIPrdColorId INT = NULL,@pordILinkAccWHIdStr  NVARCHAR(50) = NULL
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*------------------- tran2 --------------------------*/
		BEGIN TRAN
		/*---------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE CrsrPordStsupItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		/**/
			SELECT 
					[PreOrderItemID]
				,[pordIPrdId]
				,[pordIQuantity]
				,[pordIDescription]
				,[pordIPrdUnitId]
				,[pordIFCR]
				,[pordIFCRI]
				,[pordITracing]
				,[pordIQuantityTotal]
				,[pordIPrdUnitIdI]
				,[pordIPrdUnitIRateResult]	
				,[pordIPrdNickname] 
				,[pordiQuantityDesc]
				,[pordIPrdColorId]
				,[pordILinkAccWHIdStr]
				,[pordIWHId]
			FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
			WHERE [PreOrderId] = @PreOrderId			
			AND PreOrderItemID IN(SELECT [Id]
									FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
									WITH (
									[Id] INT '$.Id',
									[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
									) AS JS	
								)					
			AND pordIPrdId NOT IN(SELECT ProductID 
									FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] 
									WHERE ISNULL(PrdKind,1) = 2 )				
		ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	
		/**/
		OPEN CrsrPordStsupItem
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPordStsupItem INTO	 @PreOrderItemID,@pordIPrdId,@pordIQuantity,@pordIDescription
												,@pordIPrdUnitId,@pordIFCR,@pordIFCRI,@pordITracing,@pordIQuantityTotal
												,@pordIPrdUnitIdI,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult
												,@pordIPrdNickname,@pordiQuantityDesc,@pordIPrdColorId,@pordILinkAccWHIdStr,@pordIWHId
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*------------------------- WSStocksupplyItems ------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/

			DECLARE @CurrQty FLOAT = NULL
			SET @CurrQty = (SELECT TOP(1) [Value]
							FROM OPENJSON(ISNULL(@PordItemList,'[]'))
							WITH (
								[Id] INT '$.Id',
								[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
							) AS JS	
							WHERE [Id] = @PreOrderItemID)
			IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) <= 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/	
				FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPordStsupItem INTO	 @PreOrderItemID,@pordIPrdId,@pordIQuantity,@pordIDescription
												,@pordIPrdUnitId,@pordIFCR,@pordIFCRI,@pordITracing,@pordIQuantityTotal
												,@pordIPrdUnitIdI,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult
												,@pordIPrdNickname,@pordiQuantityDesc,@pordIPrdColorId,@pordILinkAccWHIdStr,@pordIWHId
				CONTINUE
			/**/
			END
			ELSE /*IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) > 0)*/
			BEGIN
			/**/					
				SET @pordIQuantity = @CurrQty
			/**/
			END			
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
			SET @PrdUnitIRate = NULL
			SET @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = NULL
			SET @PrdQtyNoFCR = NULL	
			SET @TmpI = NULL
			/*--------------*/			
			SELECT 				
				@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
				@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
				@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]				
			FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
			WHERE ProductId = @pordIPrdId
			/*--------------*/
			SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCRI,1)
			IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1) 					
			/**/
			END
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
			IF(/*ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto,0) = 1 AND*/ ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0  AND ISNULL(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/				
				SET @pordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1)

				IF(ISNULL(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @pordIPrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END			
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @StockSupplyItemId INT = 0,@stsupIOrderView INT = 0,@ItemCount INT = NULL
			SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] STI WHERE STI.StockSupplyId = @StockSupplyId)
			SET @stsupIOrderView = (@ItemCount + 1) + (@ItemCount * 4 ) - 1
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems]
				([StockSupplyId]
				,[stsupIPrdId]
				,[stsupIQuantity]
				,[stsupIDescription]
				,[stsupIQuantityDesc]
				,[stsupIPrdUnitId]
				,[stsupIOrderView]
				,[stsupIFCR]
				,[stsupIFCRI]
				,[stsupIWHId]
				,[stsupITracing]
				,[stsupIPrdUnitIdI]
				,[stsupIPrdUnitIRateResult]
				,[stsupIPrdColorId]
				,[stsupIPrdNickname]
				,[stsupIPreOrderId]
				,[stsupIPreOrderItemId]
				,[stsupILinkAccWHIdStr]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[UserId]
				,[FPId]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]					
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID])
			VALUES
				(@StockSupplyId
				,@pordIPrdId
				,@pordIQuantity
				,@pordIDescription
				,@pordIQuantityDesc
				,@pordIPrdUnitId
				,@stsupIOrderView
				,@pordIFCR
				,@pordIFCRI
				,@pordIWHId
				,@pordITracing
				,@pordIPrdUnitIdI
				,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult
				,@pordIPrdColorId
				,@pordIPrdNickname
				,@PreOrderId
				,@PreOrderItemID
				,@pordILinkAccWHIdStr
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@UserId
				,@FPId
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@AppType
				,@DefaultLanguageID)

			SELECT @StockSupplyItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			IF(ISNULL(@StockSupplyItemId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems_Lang]
					([StockSupplyItemId]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[stsupIDescription]
					,[stsupIQuantityDesc]
					,[stsupIPrdNickname])
				SELECT
					@StockSupplyItemId
					,POILN.LanguageID
					,pordIDescription
					,pordIQuantityDesc
					,pordIPrdNickname
				FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems_Lang] POILN 
				WHERE  POILN.PreOrderItemID = @PreOrderItemID
				AND POILN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			/**/
			END			
			/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
		/**/	
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPordStsupItem INTO	 @PreOrderItemID,@pordIPrdId,@pordIQuantity,@pordIDescription
												,@pordIPrdUnitId,@pordIFCR,@pordIFCRI,@pordITracing,@pordIQuantityTotal
												,@pordIPrdUnitIdI,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult
												,@pordIPrdNickname,@pordiQuantityDesc,@pordIPrdColorId,@pordILinkAccWHIdStr,@pordIWHId
		END
		CLOSE CrsrPordStsupItem
		DEALLOCATE CrsrPordStsupItem
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSStockSupplyItems' 
			SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
		ROLLBACK TRAN 
		END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateStockSupplyFields]
		@StockSupplyId = @StockSupplyId,
		@Month1 = @Month1,
		@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
		@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID			
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END		
	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/*--------------------------------------------*/		 
	/*--------------------------------------------*/	
/**/
END		
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- MAIN TRANSACTION END --------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@FinalResult,0) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION MainIssueStsupFromPord	
		SET @InsertedStockSupplyId = 0
		SET @InsertedStsupNumber = 0	
		SET @FinalResult = 0	
	/**/
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
	/**/
		COMMIT TRANSACTION MainIssueStsupFromPord
		SET @FinalResult = 1	
	/**/
	END	
/**/
END
ELSE
BEGIN
/**/
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION MainIssueStsupFromPord	
/**/
END
/*--------------------------------------------*/
SELECT @FinalResult AS 'Result'
/*--------------------------------------------*/	
/**/
END
